### [87] [Projection geometry and relaxed quasi-orthogonality for inf-sup stable Galerkin methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16265)
*Tsogtgerel Gantumur*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文给出无坐标初等证明：Hilbert空间上一致inf-sup稳定的嵌套Petrov-Galerkin方法满足松弛的一般拟正交性，累积Galerkin增量平方被窗口初始误差平方乘以N^σ界定（σ<1）；并构造反例表明完全拟正交性一般不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于拟正交性的结果常依赖矩阵表示、小波基或LU分解等具体工具，缺乏坐标无关的一般性证明；同时需厘清一致inf-sup稳定性究竟能保证何种程度的拟正交性，以及完全拟正交性是否由该稳定性单独推出。

Method: 利用一致有界相容投影链的相邻块之间的Hilbert空间夹角，结合二进分解与对偶性；避免矩阵表示、小波基和LU因子分解。对对称不定问题，将论证联系到Galerkin细节空间的正负谱分裂，得到符号分解方法的有限窗口版本。进一步构造固定自伴对合与固定嵌套一致inf-sup稳定Galerkin序列作为反例。

Result: 证明了一致inf-sup稳定嵌套Petrov-Galerkin方法满足松弛一般拟正交性：任意N个连续层上的累积Galerkin增量平方不超过窗口起始误差平方乘以N^σ（σ<1），σ与常数因子仅依赖Galerkin投影的一致界。反例构造显示完全一般拟正交性失效，且对每个0<α<1存在有限支撑目标使全尾比至少按N^α增长。

Conclusion: 一致inf-sup稳定性可保证次线性的有限窗口拟正交性，但完全拟正交性一般需要额外的层级信息。证明方法坐标无关，适用于Hilbert空间抽象框架，并给出了对称不定问题的有限窗口符号分解解释。

Abstract: We give an elementary, coordinate-free proof that uniformly inf-sup stable nested Petrov-Galerkin methods on Hilbert spaces satisfy relaxed general quasi-orthogonality. More precisely, the accumulated squared Galerkin increments over any window of $N$ consecutive levels are bounded by the squared error at the beginning of the window times $N^σ$, where $σ<1$, and both $σ$ and the constant prefactor depend explicitly only on a uniform bound for the Galerkin projections.
  The proof uses the Hilbert space angle between consecutive blocks of a uniformly bounded compatible projection chain, together with a dyadic decomposition and duality. It avoids matrix representations, wavelet bases, and LU-factorization. For symmetric indefinite problems, we relate the argument to the positive and negative spectral splittings of the Galerkin detail spaces and obtain a valid finite-window version of the sign-decomposition approach.
  We also construct a fixed self-adjoint involution and a fixed nested, uniformly inf-sup stable Galerkin sequence for which full general quasi-orthogonality fails. The same construction yields, for every $0<α<1$, finite-support targets whose full-tail ratios grow at least like $N^α$. Thus uniform inf-sup stability guarantees sublinear finite-window quasi-orthogonality, whereas full quasi-orthogonality requires additional hierarchical information in general.

</details>


### [88] [Physics Informed Random Feature Neural Networks for Solving PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16406)
*Chi-An Chen,Chunyang Liao,Ming Zhong*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理信息随机特征方法求解偏微分方程，可缓解PINN类求解器面临的谱偏差问题，并降低计算复杂度。

  <summary>Details</summary>

We introduce a physics-informed random feature method for countering part of the spectral bias which PINN-based solvers are facing for a certain class of PDEs. Random feature method was originally proposed to approximate large-scale kernel machines and can be viewed as a specialized randomized neural network. Compared to other state-of-the-art PINN-based solvers which require a large number of collocation points, our proposed method reduces the computational complexity. In this paper, we develop a rigorous approximation error analysis and derive high-probability error bounds on the $H^1$ norm. We provide extensive numerical tests for verifying our theoretical guarantees on error decay rates, as well as several comparison tests to showcase our claimed capability for combating spectral bias in these deep learning based methods.

The Schur complement is solved by matrix-free GMRES, while the subproblems are handled by FFT-based fast solvers. To the best of our knowledge, this level of computational efficiency has not previously been achieved in either the literature or practice. Using energy estimates and Fourier analysis, we derive a new $L^2$-estimate for Poisson equations with inexact Dirichlet boundary and then prove the convergence of the proposed scheme. We provide ample numerical experiments to confirm the accuracy, efficiency, and further apply the fast and accurate solver to triharmonic equations, high-wavenumber problems, Stokes flow, and plate bending problems.

</details>

Abstract: Reduced-order modeling of transport-dominated systems remains challenging because moving fronts and shocks are poorly represented by low-dimensional linear subspaces. We develop a structure-informed data-driven reduced-order model(ROM) for scalar hyperbolic conservation laws based on the kinetic defect formulation. This formulation separates the nonlinear dynamics into known characteristic transport and a kinetic entropy defect localized on the shock manifold. We exploit this structure by first removing the known transport from the solution snapshots. We then extract and register the remaining defect-driven dynamics in a shock-attached coordinate system. Separate ROMs are used to evolve the shock geometry and the registered defect-driven source. During prediction, the predicted shock geometry is used to inverse-register the learned defect-driven source, which advances the kinetic state and recovers the physical solution. Numerical examples in one and two spatial dimensions demonstrate accurate reconstruction and prediction of nonlinear transport with shocks, including evolution beyond the training interval, while accurately capturing the mass and entropy-dissipation behavior of the reference solution.

</details>

*Shengchang Chen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的反演范式——结构驱动反演（SDI），通过挖掘问题内在的数学结构与物理结构来构造求解方法，而非依赖迭代搜索，并给出MSDI与PSDI两类代表性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流反演方法基于优化驱动范式（ODI），将问题表述为目标函数最小化并通过迭代搜索求解，但该方法在效率、可解释性与多参数解耦方面存在局限。

Method: SDI挖掘问题的内在结构（数学结构与物理结构）以构造求解方法，对应数学结构驱动反演（MSDI）与物理结构驱动反演（PSDI）。MSDI的代表方法为MSDPII，通过酉对角化在谱域构造伪逆；PSDI的代表方法包括PSDWII，通过虚拟源投影实现反演，以及PSDBP，通过物理系统类比为深度神经网络实现三种结构投影。

Result: 作者指出，目前尚无研究系统地将结构驱动反演作为一种独立范式提出，本文旨在填补这一空白，并将SDI定位为对ODI的补充与扩展而非否定。

Conclusion: SDI是一种独立的反演范式，可与优化驱动反演互补，提升反演的效率、可解释性与多参数解耦能力。

Abstract: Inverse problems are predominantly solved within the optimization-driven paradigm, which formulates the problem as objective-function minimization and approaches the solution by iterative search. Though universal, it suffers from limitations in efficiency, interpretability, and multi-parameter decoupling. This paper proposes a new paradigm---Structure-Driven Inversion (SDI). Instead of iterative search, SDI identifies and exploits the intrinsic structure of the problem to construct a solution method. It has two types of structure---mathematical structure and physical structure---and two corresponding driving inversion types: Mathematical Structure-Driven Inversion (MSDI) and Physical Structure-Driven Inversion (PSDI). The current representative method of MSDI is Mathematical Structure-Driven Pseudo-Inverse Inversion (MSDPII), which constructs a pseudo-inverse in the spectral domain via unitary diagonalization. The current representative methods of PSDI are Physical Structure-Driven Waveform Inversion Imaging (PSDWII), which performs inversion through virtual-source projection, and Physical Structure-Driven Back-Propagation (PSDBP), which implements three structural projections for deep neural networks via physical-system analogy. SDI is not a rejection but a complement and extension of Optimization-Driven Inversion (ODI). To the best of the author's knowledge, no existing study has systematically presented structure-driven inversion as an independent paradigm; this paper aims to fill that gap.

</details>

*Sarthak Sourav Dash,Abhinav Jha*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对四边形/六面体网格上代数通量修正(CBA)格式仅保线性不足的问题，提出保双线性(bilinearity preservation)条件，给出极限器参数的精确取值与网格刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数通量修正格式依赖保线性(limit器在解为仿射时失效)来避免多余的人工耗散。但在四边形和六面体网格上，有限元空间还包含双线性函数，轴对齐网格上可被精确再现，因此仅保线性不足，导致非线性迭代无法收敛到精确的双线性解。

Method: 1) 定义网格单元片(patch)的保双线性性质，证明其等价于中心节点落在邻居节点(每个邻居按坐标差乘积提升后)的凸包内；2) 在单元边平行坐标轴的条件下，推导极限器参数的锐利显式值；3) 刻画映射双线性有限元空间精确再现双线性函数的网格类别，说明一般四边形上任何极限器的固有限制；4) 数值实验验证，并进行参数敏感性分析。

Result: 理论证明：网格片保双线性当且仅当中心节点位于提升后邻居的凸包中；给出轴对齐网格上极限器参数的锐利显式值；并刻画了映射双线性空间精确再现双线性函数的网格条件，确立了一般四边形上的固有限制。数值实验表明：轴对齐网格上所提极限器可将双线性解再现到非线性迭代的精度，而保线性极限器不能；敏感性研究显示保双线性所需参数接近定点迭代可求解范围的上界。

Conclusion: 在四边形/六面体网格上，仅靠保线性无法避免多余耗散，需引入保双线性条件；本文给出的几何判据与锐利参数值可指导极限器设计，但所需参数接近定点迭代稳定上界，暗示求解算法与极限器设计需协同考虑。

Abstract: Algebraic flux correction schemes for convection--diffusion--reaction equations rely on linearity preservation to avoid unnecessary artificial diffusion: the limiter is inactive whenever the discrete solution is affine. On quadrilateral and hexahedral meshes, this is insufficient, since the finite element space also contains bilinear functions that are reproduced exactly on axis-aligned meshes. We show that a mesh patch admits bilinearity preservation if and only if the central node lies in the convex hull of its neighbours after lifting each neighbour by the product of its coordinate differences, and we derive a sharp explicit value for the limiter parameter on such patches when the cell edges are parallel to the coordinate axes. We further characterize the meshes on which a mapped bilinear finite element space reproduces a bilinear function exactly, thereby establishing a fundamental limitation on what any limiter can achieve on general quadrilaterals. Numerical experiments confirm the theory: on axis-aligned grids, the proposed limiter reproduces a bilinear solution to the accuracy of the nonlinear iteration, whereas the linearity-preserving limiter does not. A sensitivity study shows that the parameter required for bilinearity preservation lies close to the upper end of the range for which the nonlinear problem can be solved by the fixed-point iteration considered here.

</details>


### [93] [Toward Pólya's Conjecture: Improving the Individual Li-Yau Bound via Energy Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17219)
*Yifan Wang,Hehu Xie*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为 Dirichlet Laplacian 的单个特征值建立了两类互补的下界机制：一是基于能量正交性、Bessel 估计与径向容量浴缸原理，得到保持 Weyl 标度并严格改进 Li-Yau 和不等式个体推论的下界；二是保留特征值和上界中被舍弃的谱亏损，通过精确一阶 Riesz 均值恒等式导出单调标量方程，给出优于纯体积下界的判据，并在有界 Lipschitz 区域上定量实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Li-Yau 和不等式只给出特征值求和的约束，其个体推论通常弱于直接的单特征值下界；同时既有方法在将特征值和替换为其最大项时丢弃了部分谱亏损，导致信息浪费。作者希望在同一几何框架内挖掘更强且保持正确 Weyl 标度的单个特征值下界，并明确区分框架自身改进与依赖额外几何/谱假设的更强估计。

Method: 第一机制利用能量正交性给出有限谱投影 Fourier 密度的频率相关上界，结合标准 L^2 Bessel 估计与径向容量浴缸原理，在 R^n（n≥2）任意有限正测度开集上导出带显式常数 c_n 的特征值下界；第二机制保留特征值和被最大项界定时丢弃的谱亏损，先得到计数函数下界，再通过精确一阶 Riesz 均值恒等式积分，化为严格单调标量方程，其唯一正根给出新下界，并用 Jiang-Lin 的定量估计在有界 Lipschitz 区域上实现。

Result: 第一机制给出 λ_k ≥ c_n(2π)^2 ω_n^{-2/n}|Ω|^{-2/n} k^{2/n}，其中 n/(n+2)<c_n<1，c_n 由标量方程刻画，c_2=0.5383068077…；该估计保持 Weyl 标度，严格改进 Li-Yau 和不等式的个体推论，但不改进该和不等式的尖锐首项系数。第二机制得到严格单调标量方程，其唯一正根不弱于纯体积下界，并给出相对该基线及任意独立下界严格改进的充要判据；在有界 Lipschitz 区域上借助 Jiang-Lin 定量估计给出显式实现。数值例子比较了本框架内的改进与额外几何/谱假设下更强估计。

Conclusion: 论文建立了两种互补的单特征值下界机制：一种通过能量正交性与容量论证获得保持 Weyl 标度的显式下界并改进 Li-Yau 的个体推论，另一种通过保留谱亏损并利用 Riesz 均值恒等式获得可判定的改进型下界。比较与数值实验表明，这些改进属于该框架内可达到的改进，与依赖附加几何或谱假设的更强结果有明确界限。

Abstract: We establish two complementary lower-bound mechanisms for individual eigenvalues of the Dirichlet Laplacian. First, energy orthogonality yields a frequency-dependent cap on the Fourier density of a finite spectral projection. Combining this cap with the standard $L^2$ Bessel estimate and a radial-capacity bathtub principle gives, on every open set of finite positive measure in $\mathbb R^n$ with $n\geq2$, \[ λ_k\geq c_n(2π)^2ω_n^{-2/n}|Ω|^{-2/n}k^{2/n}, \qquad \frac{n}{n+2}<c_n<1. \] The constant $c_n$ is characterized by a scalar equation, with $c_2=0.5383068077\ldots$. This estimate preserves Weyl scaling and strictly improves the individual consequence of the Li-Yau sum inequality, although it does not improve the sharp leading coefficient in that sum inequality. Second, we retain part of the spectral deficit discarded when an eigenvalue sum is bounded by its largest term. A lower bound for the counting function, integrated through the exact first Riesz-mean identity, leads to a strictly monotone scalar equation. Its unique positive root is no weaker than the volume-only bound, and we give necessary and sufficient criteria for strict improvement over both that baseline and any independent lower bound. Quantitative estimates of Jiang-Lin provide an explicit implementation on bounded Lipschitz domains. The final comparisons and numerical example distinguish improvements within this framework from stronger estimates available under additional geometric or spectral assumptions.

</details>

Method: 对热方程同时进行空间与时间离散，时间方向使用Runge-Kutta方法，形成包含所有时间步数值近似及Runge-Kutta级的大型稀疏线性系统；采用适合并行的预处理迭代法求解，并在混合精度框架下施加预条件子。

Result: 顺序（串行）结果证明预条件子具有鲁棒性，即便在混合精度下应用依然稳健；并行环境下的数值实验表明混合精度策略性能提升显著，CPU时间最多减少50%。

Conclusion: 混合精度预处理策略在保持预条件子鲁棒性的同时可显著提高并行求解效率，是加速热方程时空数值积分的有效手段。

Abstract: We study the effect of mixed precision on the numerical integration of the heat equation discretized with a Runge--Kutta method in time. A full space-time discretization is applied, which results in a very large and sparse linear system to be solved for the numerical approximations and the Runge--Kutta stages of all time steps. The linear system is solved by applying a suitable preconditioned iterative method that can be run in parallel. In order to speed up the solution process, the preconditioner is applied using a mixed precision framework. Sequential results show the robustness of the preconditioner, even when applied in mixed precision. Finally, we present numerical evidence of the improved performance of the mixed precision strategy when applied in a parallel environment, achieving up to a 50% reduction in CPU time.

</details>


### [95] [Well-posedness, Regularity, and Strong Approximations of Superlinear Stochastic Reaction-Diffusion Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16979)
*Zhihui Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为含超线性漂移与扩散系数的随机反应扩散方程（SRDE）建立了适定性、正则性与强逼近的一般框架，首次给出超线性扩散SPDE的强逼近结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有适定性结果（Liu & Röckner 2010；Liu 2013）未涵盖Gelfand三元组中的超线性扩散情形，且超线性扩散SPDE的强逼近（数值）结果尚属空白，需要统一的适定性、正则性与数值分析框架。

Method: 在Gelfand三元组V↪H↪V*中推广适定性至超线性扩散；通过对‖X‖_V^p（p≥2）建立新的Itô公式得到矩估计；将抽象结果应用于SRDE，证明初值属于同一Sobolev空间时的高阶空间正则性H^{1+γ}（γ∈[0,1]）与时间Hölder正则性；构造一般驯服函数假设下的驯服有限元方法（tamed-FEM），并分析其长时间无条件稳定性与强收敛率。

Result: 建立了含超线性扩散的SRDE适定性与矩估计；获得空间正则性H^{1+γ}（γ∈[0,1]）及时间Hölder正则性；tamed-FEM具备长时间无条件稳定性并达到最优强收敛阶。

Abstract: This paper develops a general framework for the well-posedness, regularity, and strong approximation of the stochastic reaction--diffusion equation (SRDE) with superlinear drift and diffusion coefficients. We first extend the well-posedness results in \emph{W. Liu and M. Röckner, J. Funct. Anal., 2902--2922, 2010} and \emph{W. Liu, J. Differential Equations, 572--592, 2013} to the case of superlinear diffusion in the Gelfand triple \(V \hookrightarrow H \hookrightarrow V^*\), with \(V\) equipped with the norm \(\|\cdot\|_V\), and derive a moment estimate by establishing a new Itô formula for \(\|X\|_V^p\) with general \(p \ge 2\). We then apply this abstract result to the SRDE, establish higher spatial regularity \(\dot H^{1+γ}\) for any \(γ\in [0,1]\) whenever the initial datum lies in the same Sobolev space, and obtain temporal Hölder regularity. Finally, we construct a family of tamed finite element methods (tamed-FEMs) for the SRDE under general assumptions on the tamed functions, derive their long-time unconditional stability, and establish optimal strong convergence rates. To our knowledge, this is the first strong approximation result for SPDEs with superlinear diffusion coefficients.

</details>


### [96] [Nonlinear filtering stabilizations for the quasi-geostrophic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16983)
*Lander Besabe,Sachin Kumar,Annalisa Quaini*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出Evolve-Filter-Relax (EFR)三步算法，通过非线性Helmholtz滤波与松弛步骤建模未解析尺度，实现在粗网格上高效且稳定地模拟海洋QGE流动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋流动数值模拟需细致网格以解析Munk尺度，导致计算成本高昂。基于滤波的大涡模拟(LES)可通过建模未解析尺度效应来放宽网格尺寸要求，但需要针对准地转方程(QGE)设计合适的实现策略。

Method: 提出三步EFR算法：(i)求解QGE问题；(ii)利用指示函数对位涡场进行非线性Helmholtz滤波；(iii)执行最终松弛步骤，并给出松弛参数的实用选取方法。理论上将EFR解释为带附加耗散的扰动QGE问题的分裂格式。作为对比，同时研究QGE的非线性Bardina正则化方法。

Result: 在经典基准算例上，EFR与非线性Bardina正则化均显著提升了无LES模型粗网格模拟的精度与稳定性。在地中海(更真实几何)算例中，采用基于反卷积指示函数的EFR方法在精度、稳定性与计算效率间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: EFR算法为海洋流动LES提供了一种理论可解释、参数可实用的实现框架，其中基于反卷积指示函数的版本在真实几何场景下综合表现最优，适合推广至粗网格海洋模拟。

Abstract: Numerical simulations of ocean flows typically require fine computational meshes to resolve the Munk scale, leading to high computational costs. Filtering-based large eddy simulation (LES) provides a way to relax the mesh size requirement by modeling the effects of the unresolved scales. For the implementation of this strategy, we propose a three-step algorithm called Evolve-Filter-Relax (EFR) that requires (i) the solution of a QGE problem, (ii) a nonlinear Helmholtz filter for the potential vorticity field leveraging an indicator function, and (iii) a final relaxation step. We show that the EFR algorithm can be interpreted as a splitting scheme for a perturbed QGE problem with additional dissipation and provide a practical choice for the relaxation parameter. For comparison, we also investigate a nonlinear Bardina regularization of the QGE. Numerical results on a classical benchmark show that both the EFR approach and the nonlinear Bardina regularization significantly improve the accuracy and stability of coarse mesh simulations with no LES model. Additionally, the EFR method with a deconvolution-based indicator function delivered the best balance between accuracy, stability, and computational efficiency in a test case involving a more realistic geometry (Mediterranean Sea).

</details>


### [97] [Near-Optimal Nonconvex Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17048)
*Jian-Feng Cai,Xiliang Lu,Juntao You*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析黎曼梯度下降（RGD）和黎曼高斯-牛顿（RGN）方法用于矩阵补全，证明其样本复杂度可达与凸方法同阶的线性依赖（关于维度与秩），并给出线性与二阶收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵补全中，凸方法样本复杂度为关于维度和秩的线性阶（忽略对数因子），而常用非凸方法的全局保证对秩有多项式级更高的依赖，二者之间存在样本复杂度差距，本文旨在弥合该差距。

Method: 针对n×n、秩为r、不相干参数μ、条件数κ的矩阵，采用多尺度残差初始化，并同时控制谱误差与不相干性；在此基础上分析黎曼梯度下降（RGD）与黎曼高斯-牛顿（RGN）两类非凸优化方法。

Result: RGD以高概率从O(μnr log n log(nκ))个观测中精确恢复；RGN以高概率从O(μnr log n log(2μrκ))个观测中精确恢复。RGD迭代线性收敛，RGN最终达到Q-二次收敛。

Conclusion: 通过多尺度残差初始化与谱误差、不相干性的联合控制，非凸RGD与RGN在样本复杂度上达到了与凸方法相当（关于维度和秩线性、至多对数因子）的全局保证，从而闭合了非凸方法的秩依赖差距。

Abstract: We study nonconvex methods for matrix completion, the problem of recovering a low-rank matrix from a subset of its entries. Convex methods achieve sample complexity linear in the matrix dimension and the rank, up to logarithmic factors, whereas global guarantees for commonly used nonconvex methods require a higher polynomial dependence on the rank. We close this gap by analyzing Riemannian gradient descent (RGD) and Riemannian Gauss--Newton (RGN) methods. For an $n\times n$ matrix of rank $r$ with incoherence parameter $μ$ and condition number $κ$, the two methods achieve exact recovery with high probability from $O(μnr\log n\log(nκ))$ and $O(μnr\log n\log(2μrκ))$ observations, respectively. The methods use a multiscale residual initialization, while the analysis simultaneously controls the spectral error and incoherence. The resulting RGD iterates converge linearly, whereas RGN eventually converges Q-quadratically.

</details>


### [98] [A Reynolds-Semi-Robust, Globally Divergence-Free E-HDG/IMEX-SAV Method for Variational Initial-State Data Assimilation of the Navier-Stokes Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17096)
*Ya Min,Xian Zhang,Xiaoping Xie*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出E-HDG空间离散与IMEX-SAV时间离散相结合的OTD方法，用于非定常不可压Navier-Stokes方程的变分初值数据同化，并给出局部适定性及Reynolds半鲁棒误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非定常不可压Navier-Stokes方程初值数据同化问题，需要一种既能保证全局无散度、又能实现无条件能量稳定、且在低黏度（高Reynolds数）下误差常数不显含黏度负幂次的数值方法。

Method: 采用optimize-then-discretize（OTD）策略。空间上使用嵌入式混合间断Galerkin（E-HDG）：速度与压力分别用k和k-1次间断分片多项式，速度迹为k次连续、压力迹为k次间断，使状态速度、伴随速度及重构初速度全局无散度。时间上用一阶IMEX-SAV格式，前向状态格式无条件能量稳定。在正则性与局部轨迹假设（界中不显含黏度负幂次）、网格条件Δt≲h^2及Tikhonov参数收缩性条件下分析全离散最优性系统。

Result: 建立了全离散OTD最优性系统的局部存在唯一性，并得到状态速度、伴随速度与重构初速度的Reynolds半鲁棒L^2误差估计，阶为O(h^k+Δt)，常数不显含黏度负幂次。数值实验验证了光滑算例的收敛性、机器精度级离散无散度、非线性求解器行为良好，并在小黏度情形下表现稳定。

Conclusion: E-HDG与IMEX-SAV相结合可有效求解Navier-Stokes变分初值数据同化问题，兼具全局无散度、无条件能量稳定与Reynolds半鲁棒误差估计，适用于小黏度（高Reynolds数）场景。

Abstract: This paper develops an embedded-hybridized discontinuous Galerkin (E-HDG) method combined with a first-order implicit-explicit scalar auxiliary variable (IMEX-SAV) time discretization for variational initial-state data assimilation governed by the unsteady incompressible Navier-Stokes equations. We adopt an optimize-then-discretize strategy. The spatial discretization uses discontinuous piecewise polynomials of degrees $k$ and $k-1$ for the element velocity and pressure, respectively, a continuous degree-$k$ velocity trace, and a discontinuous degree-$k$ pressure trace. The resulting state and adjoint velocities, as well as the reconstructed initial velocity, are globally divergence-free. The forward IMEX-SAV state scheme is unconditionally energy stable. Under suitable regularity and local-trajectory assumptions whose bounds introduce no explicit negative powers of the viscosity, the mesh-dependent condition $Δt\lesssim h^2$, and a contractivity condition on the Tikhonov parameter, we establish local existence and uniqueness of the fully discrete OTD optimality system and Reynolds-semi-robust $L^2$ error estimates of order $O(h^k+Δt)$ for the state, adjoint and reconstructed initial velocities; the constants contain no explicit negative powers of the viscosity. Numerical experiments confirm convergence on smooth tests and demonstrate machine-precision discrete incompressibility, effective nonlinear-solver behavior, and stable performance in small-viscosity regimes.

</details>


### [99] [An arbitrary-order BGG-based discrete scheme for the Reissner--Mindlin plate problem on polygonal meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17122)
*Arax Leroy*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对一般多边形网格上的Reissner--Mindlin板问题，设计并分析了一种任意阶数值格式，基于离散BGG扭转复形的Hodge--Laplacian导出，采用基于离散H_2的横向位移构造，证明了离散Korn不等式与收敛性，最低阶误差估计关于板厚一致即无locking，数值实验验证了理论结果与增强连续性的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reissner--Mindlin板问题在薄板极限下容易出现locking现象，传统低阶方法误差随板厚减小而恶化；同时实际工程与自适应计算常需要一般多边形网格上的高阶、鲁棒离散格式，因此需要构造并分析在多边形网格上、对板厚一致收敛（无locking）的任意阶格式。

Method: 基于离散Bernstein--Gelfand--Gelfand（BGG）扭转复形所关联的Hodge--Laplacian导出格式；对横向位移采用基于离散H_2的构造；在离散de Rham（DDR）空间上建立离散Korn不等式；进而证明方法的收敛性，并在最低阶进行误差估计。

Result: 建立了DDR空间的离散Korn不等式；证明了该任意阶格式的收敛性；在最低阶得到关于板厚一致的误差估计，表明格式无locking；并在若干多边形网格族上进行了数值实验，验证了理论结果，同时展示了横向位移离散增强连续性带来的好处。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般多边形网格上的Reissner--Mindlin板问题提供了一个任意阶、无locking的DDR型数值格式，理论上由离散Korn不等式与收敛性分析支撑，数值上得到验证，显示增强横向位移连续性具有实际优势。

Abstract: We design and analyse an arbitrary-order numerical scheme for the Reissner--Mindlin plate problem on general polygonal meshes. The scheme is derived from the Hodge--Laplacian associated with a discrete Bernstein--Gelfand--Gelfand (BGG) twisted complex and exploits a discrete $H_2$-based construction for the transverse displacement. We establish a discrete Korn inequality for the underlying Discrete de Rham method (DDR) spaces and prove the convergence of the method. At the lowest order, the analysis yields an error estimate that is uniform with respect to the plate thickness, showing that the scheme is locking-free. Numerical experiments on several families of polygonal meshes support the theoretical results and illustrate the benefits of the enhanced continuity of the transverse displacement discretisation.

</details>


### [100] [Latent Inversion of Material Coefficients from Boundary Data via Finite Tests and Neural Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17137)
*Erik Burman,Mats G. Larson,Karl Larsson,Jonatan Vallin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究标量椭圆方程中空间变系数的反问题，通过将系数限制在低维潜空间中并利用至多 \(m\) 次边界激励实现局部唯一性与Lipschitz稳定性，并将稳定性传递到有限元离散与代理模型误差分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从有限次边界激励重建空间变系数是严重不适定的，且反复求解PDE代价高昂。作者希望借助低维参数化降低问题难度与计算成本，并建立可量化的稳定性与误差分离理论。

Method: 将系数限制在低维族（解析指定或从样本学习），在潜坐标中求解反问题；考虑具有 \(m\) 个潜坐标、在参考点满秩导数的 \(C^1\) 参数化。若连续线性化Neumann-to-Dirichlet映射在对应切空间上单射，则至多 \(m\) 次激励即可给出局部单射性与Lipschitz稳定性；再通过系数敏感性收敛将稳定性传递至协调有限元离散，并在局部残差比较条件下分析代理前向映射精度带来的系数误差界。

Result: 在连续线性化ND映射于切空间单射的条件下，至多 \(m\) 次激励足以实现局部单射与Lipschitz稳定性；网格足够细时稳定性常数与邻域可独立于网格尺寸。局部残差比较条件下，代理前向映射的一致精度给出分离表示误差、数据噪声、有限元误差与代理误差的系数误差界；导数精度还能保持代理自身的局部稳定性。二维数值实验结合解析与学习表示（内含物、裂纹状系数）及神经前向代理，展示潜空间重建、在线成本降低及可选FEM精化的进一步改进。

Conclusion: 该工作为低维潜空间中的椭圆反问题提供了局部稳定性理论、有限元离散传递及代理误差分解框架。所有稳定性结论均为相对参考系数的局部结果；数值实验验证了潜空间重建与计算效率，并表明可选FEM精化可进一步提升精度。

Abstract: We reconstruct a spatially varying material coefficient in a scalar elliptic equation from finitely many boundary excitations, each producing a full Dirichlet trace. To mitigate the ill-posedness and the cost of repeated PDE solves, we restrict the coefficient to a low-dimensional family, specified analytically or learned from samples, and solve the inverse problem in its latent coordinates. We consider a \(C^1\) parametrization with \(m\) latent coordinates and full-rank derivative at a reference point. If the continuous linearized Neumann-to-Dirichlet map is injective on the corresponding tangent space, at most \(m\) excitations suffice for local injectivity and Lipschitz stability. Convergence of the coefficient sensitivities then transfers this stability to conforming finite element discretizations. For sufficiently fine meshes, the stability constant and neighborhood can be chosen independently of the mesh size. Under a local residual-comparison condition, uniform accuracy of the surrogate forward map yields coefficient-error bounds separating representation error, data noise, finite element error, and surrogate error. Derivative accuracy additionally preserves the surrogate's own local stability. All stability statements are local to a reference coefficient. Two-dimensional numerical experiments combine analytic and learned representations of inclusions and crack-like coefficients with neural forward surrogates. They illustrate latent-space reconstruction, reduced online cost, and further improvement from optional FEM-based refinement.

</details>


### [101] [Error Bounds for Boundary-Stopped Positive Cubature Schemes for Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17186)
*Haoran Xu,Xingye Yue*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文为有界域上带Dirichlet数据的退化抛物型HJB方程建立边界停止正求积格式的误差界，得到最大范数收敛率 \(O(h^{1/7})\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对退化抛物型 Hamilton–Jacobi–Bellman 方程在有界域带 Dirichlet 边界数据的数值求解，需要处理边界停止、对跖分支平衡与秩亏扩散等难点，并给出均匀一致性与收敛性保证。

Method: 构造边界停止正求积格式：在首次边界接触处平衡对跖分支，引入控制相关的有效时间 \(\tau\)；分析停止障碍余项与插值分支总质量，除以有效时间后得到 \(\tau\to0\) 一致的相容性界；在严格边界障碍与网格条件下结合内部相容性、系数抖动、切换与局部比较论证。

Result: 停止障碍余项为 \(O(\tau\Delta t^{\gamma/2})\)，插值分支总质量至多 \(2\tau/\Delta t\)；误差界为 \(\|(u-u_h)^+\|_\infty\le C(\Delta t^{1/4}+h\Delta t^{-1/2})\) 与 \(\|(u_h-u)^+\|_\infty\le C(\Delta t^{1/10}+h^{1/2}\Delta t^{-1/4})\)；取 \(\Delta t\asymp h^{10/7}\) 时最大范数界为 \(O(h^{1/7})\)。

Conclusion: 该分析适用于任意固定的中心对称正二次求积，并允许秩亏扩散；数值算例考察了向冷壁的并行热损失以及控制相关、秩亏扩散情形。

Abstract: We derive error bounds for a boundary-stopped positive cubature scheme for degenerate parabolic Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equations with Dirichlet data on bounded domains. The scheme balances antipodal branches at their first boundary contacts and uses a control-dependent effective time. Its stopped-barrier remainder is \(O(τΔt^{γ/2})\), and the total mass of interpolated branches is at most \(2τ/Δt\), where \(τ\) is the effective time. After division by the effective time, these estimates give a consistency bound that is uniform as \(τ\to0\). Under a common strict boundary barrier and the stated mesh conditions, interior consistency, coefficient shaking, switching, and localized comparison yield \[ \|(u-u_h)^+\|_\infty\le C(Δt^{1/4}+hΔt^{-1/2}), \qquad \|(u_h-u)^+\|_\infty\le C(Δt^{1/10}+h^{1/2}Δt^{-1/4}). \] Thus \(Δt\asymp h^{10/7}\) gives an \(O(h^{1/7})\) maximum-norm bound. The analysis applies to any fixed centrally symmetric positive degree-two cubature and permits rank-deficient diffusion. Numerical examples examine parallel heat loss to a cold wall and control-dependent, rank-deficient diffusion.

</details>


### [102] [A provably convergent MM-GKS variant for large-scale inverse problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17229)
*Mirjeta Pasha,Eric de Sturler,Misha Kilmer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种压缩-扩展交替的MM-GKS变体，保持严格单调收敛，在极小搜索空间维度下仍可证明收敛，显著降低内存与计算成本，并推广至流式问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶图像锐利边缘需要广义ℓ_q范数梯度正则化，常通过ℓ_2范数加加权梯度的序列近似，并用混合Krylov子空间法求解。但每次更新正则化算子都需重建Krylov子空间；MM-GKS用单一广义Krylov子空间（GKS）解决此问题，然而大规模问题下收敛慢时内存与计算开销过大，且原有收敛证明依赖解空间基最终张满全空间。

Method: 提出MM-GKS的变体：交替压缩与扩展搜索空间（compress-and-expand），同时保持严格单调收敛；在搜索空间维度保持很小时仍可证明收敛到所选泛函的最小值；并进一步推广到数据不能同时获取、或因内存限制需按流式处理的情形。

Result: 方法在内存需求与计算复杂度两方面均可高效求解大规模问题；收敛性证明不依赖搜索空间张满全空间，改进了MM-GKS的既有理论结果；通过图像去模糊、动态光声层析、流式X射线CT等数值实验验证有效性。

Conclusion: 所提变体在保持严格单调收敛的前提下大幅降低大规模问题的内存与计算成本，理论保证更强（不要求搜索空间张满全空间），并可扩展至流式重建场景。

Abstract: For high-quality images with sharp edges, a popular choice for edge-preserving regularization is using a general(ized) $\ell_q$-norm of the gradient of the image. This can be implemented efficiently using the $\ell_2$-norm and a sequence of weighted gradients, with weights derived from the current solution estimate. We can solve the resulting sequence of regularized least squares problems using hybrid Krylov subspace methods, which efficiently compute the regularization parameter using the problem projected on the Krylov subspace. However, each update of the regularization operator requires a new Krylov subspace. The majorization-minimization generalized Krylov subspace method (MM-GKS) addresses this problem by using a single, generalized, Krylov subspace (GKS). Unfortunately, for large-scale problems, if convergence is not fast, MM-GKS has overwhelming memory requirements and computational costs. We propose a variant of MM-GKS that alternately compresses and expands the search space while maintaining strict monotonic convergence. We show that our method provably converges to the minimum of the selected functional, even if the search space dimension is kept very small. This substantially improves on previous theoretical results for MM-GKS, where the convergence proof relies on the basis for the solution space (eventually) spanning the full space. We show that our method can solve large-scale problems efficiently both in terms of memory requirements and computational complexity. We further generalize our proposed method to handle streaming problems, where the data is either not all available simultaneously or needs to be treated as such because of the extreme memory requirements. We use numerical examples from image deblurring, dynamic photoacoustic tomography, and streaming X-ray computed tomography (CT) to illustrate the effectiveness of our proposed methods.

</details>


### [103] [Algebraic convergence analysis for the Interface Control Domain Decomposition (ICDD) method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17271)
*Marco Discacciati,Paola Gervasio,Alfio Quarteroni*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对ICDD（接口控制域分解）方法的收敛性进行了分析，给出了GMRES迭代次数的理论估计，其收敛率为O(δ^{-1}p^{3/2}log p)，且与网格尺寸h无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICDD是一种基于最优控制框架的重叠型区域分解方法，但其收敛性的理论分析尚不完善。作者希望为该方法的GMRES迭代求解提供严格的理论收敛率估计，并借此扩展至相关方法（SRAS与RAS）的分析。

Method: 在重叠区域上采用协调离散、各子域使用hp-FEM逼近带可能间断系数的椭圆问题；结合ICDD舒尔补矩阵的新谱估计与经典GMRES收敛理论，推导非对称接口舒尔补系统的迭代次数上界。

Result: 证明了收敛率表现为O(δ^{-1}p^{3/2}log p)，其中δ为重叠宽度、p为局部多项式阶数，且不依赖于网格尺寸h；数值实验验证了理论预测，并展示了该方法在计算域存在大系数跳变时的有效性。

Conclusion: 在协调情形下，所考虑的ICDD格式与SRAS方法一致，因此该分析同时给出了二维SRAS的收敛估计；通过已知的等价关系，也间接为RAS方法提供了收敛估计。

Abstract: We develop the convergence analysis of the Interface Control Domain Decomposition (ICDD) method, an overlapping domain decomposition method based on an optimal control framework with Dirichlet interface control functions and interface observation. We consider elliptic problems with possible discontinuous coefficients approximated by $hp-$FEM in each subdomain. When the discretizations are conforming on the overlap between 2D domains, we provide theoretical estimates of the number of GMRES iterations needed to solve the non-symmetric interface Schur complement system associated with ICDD. Our results are obtained by combining novel spectral estimates for the Schur complement matrix of ICDD and classical GMRES convergence theory. We prove that the convergence rate behaves as $\mathcal{O}(δ^{-1} p^{3/2}\log p)$, where $δ$ denotes the overlap width and $p$ the local polynomial degree, while remaining independent of the mesh size $h$. Numerical experiments verify the theoretical predictions and show the effectiveness of ICDD in the presence of large coefficient jumps in the computational domain. Since in the conforming case, the considered ICDD formulation coincides with the Substructured Restricted Additive Schwarz (SRAS) method, the analysis also provides convergence estimates for SRAS in two dimensions and, through its known equivalence, for the Restricted Additive Schwarz (RAS) method.

</details>


### [104] [The sharp CFL condition of the piecewise constant sparse grid discontinuous Galerkin method for high-dimensional transport equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17312)
*Juntao Huang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文为分段常数稀疏网格DG方法配合前向Euler时间离散求解常系数输运方程，建立了尖锐的CFL条件：当且仅当 Δt ≤ h/max|c_ℓ| 时L²稳定；相比满网格迎风格式的 Δt ≤ h/Σ|c_ℓ|，允许时间步放大约1至d倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏网格DG相比满网格迎风格式具有更宽松的时间步限制，但其尖锐CFL条件尚缺乏严格刻画，尤其是多维、任意维情形下的充分性与必要性证明，以及一般下闭指标集空间下的稳定性判据。

Method: 针对常系数输运方程、周期区域、均匀网格，证明分段常数稀疏网格DG + 前向Euler的L²稳定性。充分性利用多级Haar分解的投影泄漏恒等式，将能量估计中的混合方向项由稀疏网格投影丢弃的能量吸收；必要性依据一维最细层交替模态构造反例。进一步给出放大算子的L²算子范数与谱半径显式公式，并对一般下闭指标集导出充分CFL条件及尖锐性的几何判据。二维、四维数值实验验证。

Result: 证明方案L²稳定当且仅当 Δt ≤ h/max_{1≤ℓ≤d}|c_ℓ|；满网格迎风格式需 Δt ≤ h/Σ|c_ℓ|；稀疏网格将允许时间步放大 (Σ|c_ℓ|)/(max|c_ℓ|) 倍，介于1与d之间，各向同性输运达d倍。同时得到放大算子的L²算子范数与谱半径显式表达式，以及一般下闭指标集下的充分CFL条件与尖锐性几何判据。数值实验（d=2,4）与理论一致。

Conclusion: 稀疏网格DG的尖锐CFL条件由最大方向速度决定，而非速度分量之和，故在多维输运中显著放宽时间步限制；投影泄漏恒等式是稳定性证明的关键工具，一维交替模态给出尖锐性；结果可推广至一般下闭指标集，并由数值实验验证。

Abstract: We establish the sharp CFL condition for the piecewise constant sparse grid discontinuous Galerkin (DG) method with forward Euler time stepping, applied to transport equations with constant coefficients on periodic domains in arbitrary dimensions. For the transport velocity $\boldsymbol c=(c_1,\ldots,c_d)$ and a uniform mesh of size $h$, we prove that the scheme is $L^2$ stable if and only if $Δt \leq {h}/{\max_{1\leq \ell\leq d}|c_\ell|}$, whereas the corresponding full grid upwind scheme is well-known to require $Δt\leq h/\sum_{\ell=1}^d |c_\ell|$. The sparse grid discretization therefore enlarges the admissible time step by a factor of ${(\sum_{\ell=1}^d |c_\ell|)}/{(\max_{1\leq \ell\leq d}|c_\ell|)}$, which lies between $1$ and $d$ and reaches $d$ for isotropic transport. The proof of sufficiency relies on projection leakage identities for the multilevel Haar decomposition, which allow the mixed directional terms in the energy estimate to be absorbed by the energy discarded by the sparse grid projection. The proof of sharpness follows from alternating modes in one dimension at the finest level. As a by-product, we obtain explicit formulas for the $L^2$ operator norm and the spectral radius of the amplification operator. For spaces over general downward closed index sets, we derive an explicit sufficient CFL condition and a geometric criterion for its sharpness. Numerical experiments in two and four dimensions confirm the theoretical results.

</details>


### [105] [On the Existence of Pressure-Equilibrium-Preserving Numerical Fluxes for Supercritical Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17492)
*Robin Ben Klein*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出超临界流体压力平衡保持(PEP)数值通量函数的新存在性定理，用状态方程的几何性质刻画PEP格式的存在性，并通过数值实验验证条件的限制性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期基于一般状态方程导出的PEP相容性条件依赖于某些热力学导数的存在性，而这些导数在基本热力学原理下并不保证有定义。本文旨在建立不依赖这些导数的几何判据。

Method: 将一致数值通量函数满足代数PEP性质的存在性问题，转化为状态方程的热力学几何性质；该几何条件仅要求状态方程满足基本热力学原理，并在导数有定义时退化为已有相容性条件。结合数值实验对两种超临界流体进行验证。

Result: 当同一条等压线上两状态的几何性质不满足时，包含这些状态的区域内不存在所考虑形式的PEP格式；数值实验表明对两种超临界流体，存在性条件具有很强的限制性，因而所指定区域内不存在此类PEP格式；几何视角还揭示了PEP格式产生数值问题的机制。

Conclusion: PEP格式的存在性与状态方程的几何性质紧密相关，新几何判据更为普适且推广了已有相容性条件，同时为理解PEP格式的数值失效机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: In this work we propose a new existence theorem for numerical flux functions for supercritical fluids that are pressure-equilibrium preserving (PEP). In particular, we characterize the existence of consistent numerical flux functions that satisfy an algebraic PEP property. Our theory links the existence of PEP schemes to geometric properties of the equation of state describing the thermodynamics of the fluid. When these geometric properties fail for a pair of states on the same isobar, no PEP schemes of the considered form can exist on a domain containing those states. In our analysis the equation of state itself can be fully general only needing to satisfy some fundamental thermodynamic principles. Recently, PEP compatibility conditions for general equations of state have been derived \cite{channodal} that rely on the existence of certain thermodynamic derivatives which are not guaranteed to be defined under fundamental thermodynamic principles. The geometric conditions in our theory do not depend on the existence of these derivatives and recover the recent compatibility conditions in the case that these derivatives are defined. Finally, using numerical experiments we demonstrate for two supercritical fluids that our existence conditions are restrictive and thus that no PEP schemes of the form we consider exist on the domain we specify for these fluids. Using our geometric perspective, we also shed light on mechanisms by which PEP schemes can develop numerical issues, which we also demonstrate using numerical experiments.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Projection, Memory, and the Validity of Scalar Early-Warning Signals: A Schur--Volterra Theory with an AMOC Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16235)
*Mauricio Herrera-Marín*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的算子理论，将高维动力系统经由投影得到的单标量预警信号的多种可能机制（谱失稳、隐状态记忆、非正规放大、物理平衡转折等）显式区分，给出Volterra约化、Markov提升、Schur分解与协方差定理，并证明五类失效机制，最后以CESM AMOC滞后实验为例说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预警信号通常仅由少量可观测量计算，而真实动力学是高维的；投影会造成结构性歧义：同一标量记录可能来自谱失稳、隐状态记忆、非正规放大或物理平衡转折等不同机制。作者需要一套统一的理论框架来显式区分这些替代解释，并厘清标量在什么条件下才能继承临界慢化。

Method: 针对resolved-hidden分块线性化，推导精确的Volterra约化、有限与扩散型Markovian提升，以及稳定性问题的Schur-resolvent因式分解；区分三种记忆（realized pathwise、structural Schur return、finite-horizon projected）。给出协方差定理，建立标量继承临界慢化的充分条件（谱分离、特征向量条件数有界、临界模态可观测、该模态受随机激励、准平稳强迫），并证明若干失效机制。

Result: 证明的失效机制包括：临界模态可能不可见或未被激励；振荡穿越不一定提升lag-one相关；非正规族可在稳定谱下产生发散标量方差；物理转折点不一定与谱边界重合；有限时域数据无法唯一识别长记忆。CESM 4400年AMOC滞后实验显示：单独EOF1可探测磁化率改变，而EOF1-EOF2是最小resolved空间，在恢复后揭示Schur return与projected memory的变化；F_ovS在崩溃前26年达最小值，而模态margin并未趋零，说明两种诊断探测互补机制。

Conclusion: 投影标量预警信号的解释存在多重结构性歧义，需借助统一算子理论区分机制；标量继承临界慢化需满足若干充分条件，而多种失效机制说明标准预警指标可能失灵。该应用属机理性、单轨迹分析，未声称给出经验证的预警信号。

Abstract: Early-warning signals are usually computed from one or a few observables, although the underlying dynamics are high dimensional. Projection creates a structural ambiguity: the same scalar record may reflect spectral loss of stability, hidden-state memory, nonnormal amplification, a turning point of a physical balance, or a combination of these. We develop a unified operator theory that makes these alternatives explicit. For a resolved-hidden block linearization we derive exact Volterra reductions, finite and diffusive Markovian liftings, and a Schur-resolvent factorization of the stability problem, distinguishing three notions of memory: realized pathwise, structural Schur return, and finite-horizon projected. A covariance theorem gives sufficient conditions for a scalar to inherit critical slowing down from a real multiplier approaching the unit circle: spectral separation, bounded eigenvector conditioning, observability of the critical mode, stochastic excitation of that mode, and quasi-stationary forcing. We then prove failure mechanisms: a critical mode can be invisible or unexcited; an oscillatory crossing need not raise lag-one correlation; a nonnormal family can produce diverging scalar variance with a fixed stable spectrum; a physics-based turning point need not coincide with a spectral boundary; and finite-horizon data cannot identify long memory uniquely. The theory is illustrated with a 4400-year CESM AMOC hysteresis experiment. EOF1 alone detects altered susceptibility, but EOF1-EOF2 is the smallest resolved space revealing changed Schur return and projected memory after recovery; F_ovS reaches its minimum 26 years before collapse while the modal margin does not decrease toward zero: the two diagnostics probe complementary mechanisms. The application is mechanistic and single-trajectory; no validated early-warning signal is claimed.

</details>


### [107] [Nationally Consistent, Locally Incomplete: A Bayesian Remote-Sensing Audit of Rooftop Photovoltaic Registries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16294)
*Gabriel Kasmi,Yves-Marie Saint-Drenan,Laurent Dubus,Philippe Blanc*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个贝叶斯框架，将不完美的屋顶光伏遥感检测器转化为具备不确定性量化的测量工具，从而估计真实屋顶光伏装机容量。以法国为例，校正后的检测低估容量为4.03 GWp（99%可信区间3.96–4.11），与输电系统运营商数据在全国层面误差仅3.3%，并揭示局部低报高达61%及开放数据中的截断偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屋顶光伏因分布式特性难以追踪，官方统计存在已知但未被量化的不准确性。遥感可独立识别屋顶光伏系统，但检测器并不完美，直接使用会产生偏差。因此需要一个能将不完美检测转化为可信容量估计的方法。

Method: 引入贝叶斯框架，从遥感检测结果中估计真实屋顶光伏容量，将不完美检测器转化为不确定性感知的测量工具，并对检测结果进行偏差校正。

Result: 应用于法国：校正后检测估计低于36 kWp的屋顶光伏容量为4.03 GWp（99%可信区间3.96–4.11），与输电系统运营商连接数据在全国层面匹配误差仅3.3%；同时识别出局部低报高达当地容量的61%，并记录和量化了法国屋顶光伏开放数据中显著的截断偏差。

Conclusion: 该方法提高了屋顶光伏容量估计的可靠性，可推广至全球范围，为实现更可靠的屋顶光伏装机统计提供了路径。

Abstract: Tracking the energy transition requires reliable statistics on renewable deployment. Rooftop photovoltaics (PV) are especially hard to track, owing to their decentralised nature, and the resulting inaccuracies in official statistics are known but not quantified. Remote sensing offers an independent way to identify rooftop PV systems. We introduce a Bayesian framework to estimate the ground-truth rooftop PV capacity from remote sensing detections, turning an imperfect detector into an uncertainty-aware measurement instrument. Applied to France, the corrected detections estimate a capacity of 4.03 GWp [3.96--4.11] (99% credible interval) of rooftop PV below 36 kWp, matching the transmission system operator's connection data within 3.3% nationally, while identifying local under-reports of up to 61% of local capacity. We also document and quantify a significant truncation bias in French rooftop PV open data. Beyond France, the approach paves the way for more reliable estimates of rooftop PV capacity worldwide.

</details>


### [108] [Observational constraints on net radiative forcing confirm aviation contrail warming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16451)
*Aaron Sonabend-W,Scott Geraedts,Nita Goyal,Joe Yue-Hei Ng,Christopher Van Arsdale,Kevin McCloskey*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究采用因果框架，将平流航班路径作为凝结尾迹代理，扩展连续处理方法来捕捉短波冷却效应，首次给出12小时生命周期的净辐射强迫观测估计：2019年4月至2020年4月美洲上空航班每飞行公里净增温能量强迫为33.7 GJ（95% CI: 20.8–47.8）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝结尾迹卷云是航空非CO2气候影响的关键部分，但其净辐射强迫（长波增温与短波冷却的平衡）缺乏直接观测约束。现有评估几乎完全依赖CoCiP等微物理模型和全球气候模拟；已有经验估计多局限于年轻、线状尾迹，因卫星检测掩膜对扩散并与自然卷云融合的尾迹召回率低，导致对长寿命、非线状凝结尾迹的认识存在结构性空白。

Method: 采用因果框架分离美洲上空航班交通的净辐射凝结尾迹效应。在近期利用平流航班路径作为凝结尾迹代理、量化长波增温效应的进展基础上，扩展这一连续处理（continuous treatment）方法，以捕捉高度偏斜的短波冷却影响，从而得到12小时生命周期的净观测辐射强迫。

Result: 2019年4月至2020年4月，观测到每飞行公里具有统计显著的净增温能量强迫33.7 GJ（95% CI: 20.8, 47.8）。该结果为长生命周期凝结尾迹影响提供了大规模经验量化，与既往自下而上模拟估计量级相当，但略偏大。

Conclusion: 该观测基准为凝结尾迹气候影响的符号与量级提供了独立的证据线，有助于约束长期存在争议的凝结尾迹净辐射强迫问题。

Abstract: Contrail cirrus represents a critical component of aviation's non-CO2 climate impact, but its net radiative forcing, the balance between longwave warming and shortwave cooling, remains poorly constrained by direct observations. As a result, current assessments rely almost exclusively on microphysical models such as CoCiP and global climate simulations. Existing empirical estimates are largely restricted to young, linear tracks, because satellite detection masks have a poor recall of contrails once they spread and merge with natural cirrus, leaving a structural gap in our understanding of long-lived, non-linear contrail cirrus. To address this we use a causal framework that isolates the net radiative contrail effect of flight traffic over the Americas. Building on recent progress that quantified the longwave warming contrail effect using advected flight paths as a proxy for contrails, we expand this continuous treatment approach to capture the highly skewed shortwave cooling impact, delivering a 12-hour lifespan net observational radiative forcing. Our analysis reveals a statistically significant net warming energy forcing of 33.7 (95% CI: 20.8, 47.8) GJ per km flown from April 2019 to April 2020, providing a large-scale empirical quantification of long contrail lifespan impact of the same order as, though somewhat larger than, previous bottom-up simulation estimates. This observational benchmark offers an independent line of evidence on the sign and magnitude of the climate impact of contrails.

</details>


### [109] [Anthropogenic Forcing, Climate Change, and the Shape of Warming: Statistical Inference for Distributional Cointegration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16509)
*Won-Ki Seo,Kyungsik Nam*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该文提出基于残差的协整检验、完全修正最小二乘估计及模拟推断方法，研究集成预测向量与密度值响应之间的长期关系，并将其应用于局地温度异常密度与CO₂/非CO₂强迫组合，揭示了标量回归掩盖的分布异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人为强迫的不同组成部分遵循不同的长期路径，持续的温度变化可能涉及均值以外的分布变化。标量回归将各强迫成分加总并仅保留平均温度，掩盖了不同强迫路径与持续性分布变化之间的关系。

Method: 针对集成预测向量与密度值响应之间的长期关系，开发了新的检验、估计与推断方法：一是基于残差的“协整间”（between-cointegration）检验，判断预测变量的趋势是否解释了响应密度中的所有随机趋势；二是完全修正最小二乘估计量，用于估计各预测变量特定的函数响应；三是基于模拟的推断，用于可解释的投影。实证中将人为有效辐射强迫拆分为CO₂与非CO₂组合，应用于观测局地温度异常密度。

Result: 检验结果与这些组合的持续变动在统计上解释了异常分布的持续演化相一致，残差中未检测到额外的随机趋势；联合检验拒绝了将两种组合加总所施加的共同响应约束。拟合的CO₂响应主要将质量移向更暖的异常并提高中心集中度，而非CO₂响应产生较小位移但带来更大的离散度和偏离中心的重塑；两者均为正的平均响应，掩盖了上述差异。

Conclusion: 标量聚合会丢失关于强迫路径与温度异常分布关系的重要信息，需要采用密度值响应与多预测变量的长期关系方法加以刻画。

Abstract: Anthropogenic forcing components follow different long-run paths, while persistent temperature change can involve distributional changes beyond the mean. Scalar regressions aggregate these components and retain only mean temperature, obscuring how distinct forcing paths relate to persistent distributional change. We develop new testing, estimation, and inference methods for long-run relations between an integrated predictor vector and a density-valued response. These comprise a residual-based test of between-cointegration (whether predictor trends account for all stochastic trends in the response density), a fully modified least-squares estimator of predictor-specific functional responses, and simulation-based inference for interpretable projections. We apply the methods to densities of observed local temperature anomalies and anthropogenic effective radiative forcing divided into CO$_2$ and non-CO$_2$ portfolios. The test results are consistent with persistent movements in these portfolios statistically accounting for the persistent evolution of the anomaly distribution, with no additional stochastic trend detected in the residual. A joint test rejects the common-response restriction imposed by aggregating the two portfolios. The fitted CO$_2$ response mainly shifts mass toward warmer anomalies and increases central concentration, whereas the non-CO$_2$ response produces a smaller shift but greater dispersion and off-center reshaping. Positive fitted mean responses for both portfolios conceal these contrasts, demonstrating the information lost through scalar aggregation.

</details>


### [110] [Combining Weather Forecast Aggregation and State-Space Models for Adaptive Probabilistic Electricity Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17000)
*Joseph de Vilmarest,Jonathan Dumas,Jean Thorey*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应概率负荷预测框架，融合多气象预报源，结合自适应GAM与状态空间（Viking算法）及GAMLSS概率层，并引入短期校正机制，在法国全国电力消费数据上取得优于单气象源方法的点预测与概率预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力负荷预测对实时供需平衡至关重要，天气条件与可再生能源并网使负荷波动加剧，气象预报误差会传导至负荷预测，单一气象源难以稳健应对，因此需要缓解气象误差影响并提升不确定性量化能力。

Method: 构建自适应概率预测框架：以自适应广义可加模型（GAM）为核心，采用Viking算法估计状态空间形式，实现参数随时间的动态更新；扩展GAMLSS概率层，使残差方差依赖于解释变量以改进不确定性量化；加入短期校正机制以适配不同预测时间尺度；关键设计是聚合多个气象预报提供者的输入，利用其多样性并纳入预报离散度信息。

Result: 在法国全国电力消费数据上评估，方法相较单气象提供者方案获得一致提升，其中概率预测精度改善显著，并能够更好刻画极端分位数（借助预报离散度）。

Conclusion: 多气象源聚合可同时提升点预测与概率预测表现，结合动态参数更新与GAMLSS不确定性建模的自适应框架，是应对气象预报误差与多时间尺度负荷预测的有效方案。

Abstract: Accurate electricity load forecasting is essential to ensure the real-time balance between supply and demand, especially in systems increasingly influenced by weather conditions and renewable energy integration. In this paper, we propose an adaptive probabilistic forecasting framework that leverages multiple meteorological forecast providers to mitigate the impact of weather prediction errors on load forecasts. Our approach combines an adaptive generalized additive model (GAM) with a state-space formulation estimated via the Viking algorithm, enabling dynamic parameter updates over time. To improve uncertainty quantification, we extend the framework with a probabilistic layer based on GAM for location, scale and shape (GAMLSS), allowing the residual variance to depend on explanatory variables. We further introduce a short-term correction mechanism to enhance performance across different forecast horizons. A key contribution of this work is the aggregation of multiple meteorological inputs, whose diversity is shown to improve both point and probabilistic forecasts, particularly in capturing extreme quantiles by incorporating forecast dispersion. The methodology is evaluated on French national electricity consumption data and demonstrates consistent gains over single-provider approaches, with significant improvements in probabilistic forecasting accuracy.

</details>


### [111] [Characterising mortality dynamics across countries and time using a multi-stage clustering approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17268)
*Pedro Menezes de Araújo,Ugofilippo Basellini,Thomas Brendan Murphy,Isobel Claire Gormley*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多阶段聚类框架，结合时变beta潜变量模型与序列聚类，对1960-2019年94个国家分性别的死亡率动态进行分析，识别出少量“死亡率状态”并按状态序列对国家聚类，揭示了死亡率转变时序与速度的异质性及跨国不平等先升后降的趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有跨国死亡率比较研究多局限于单一时点的国家间差异，或仅关注单个国家的轨迹演变，未能同时刻画“国家内变化”与“国家间差异”，因此存在分析视角上的空白。

Method: 采用多阶段聚类框架：第一阶段用时变beta潜变量模型对生命表死亡概率建模，提取每个国家在任一时点的“死亡率状态”；第二阶段依据各国的死亡率状态序列进行聚类。数据为世界人口展望中1960-2019年94个国家分性别的数据。

Result: 结果显示各国在死亡率状态转变的时间与速度上存在显著异质性，包括欧洲持续的东-西分化，以及拉丁美洲与青壮年男性死亡率偏高相关的独特模式；跨国死亡率不平等在1990年代前持续上升，之后趋稳并随后下降。

Conclusion: 该多阶段聚类框架提供了一种可解释、显式包含时间维度的死亡率动态描述，能同时捕捉国家内变化与国家间差异，并可推广至其他关注分类或离散化剖面轨迹的研究场景。

Abstract: Comparative analyses of mortality dynamics across countries have long shaped our understanding of mortality inequalities and patterns of divergence and convergence over time. However, most existing studies focus on either mortality differences across countries at a single point in time or on country trajectories, without considering both factors simultaneously. In this paper, we address this gap by introducing a multi-stage clustering framework, applied to 94 countries by sex over the period 1960-2019 using data from the World Population Prospects. Specifically, we model life-table probabilities of death using a time-dependent beta latent variable model and identify a small set of ``mortality states'' that characterise the mortality pattern of each country at any given point in time. We then cluster countries based on their sequences of mortality states. Our results reveal substantial heterogeneity in the timing and pace of transitions between mortality states, including a persistent East-West divide in Europe and distinctive Latin American patterns associated with elevated young-adult male mortality. Cross-country inequality increased for both sexes until the 1990s, before plateauing and subsequently declining. Our proposed multi-stage clustering framework provides an interpretable, time-explicit description of mortality dynamics that jointly captures within-country change and between-country differences, and can be applied to other settings where trajectories of categorical or discretised profiles are of interest.

</details>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Probabilistic, Structure-Intrinsic Clustering with an Hierarchical Embedding (PSICHE) in Time and Space Applied In Particle Physics Jet Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16276)
*Margaret Lazarovits,Christopher Rogan*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 提出PSICHE算法，在自洽概率框架下实现动态簇大小、多尺度无监督特征学习、时空聚类及实验不确定性建模，并验证于LHC喷注聚类场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喷注重建是粒子物理活跃的开放研究领域，涉及喷注尺寸、多重数、子结构及在堆积与噪声下的实验性能等问题；这些问题与无监督学习中的聚类与关联任务挑战相通，现有喷注聚类方法存在若干不足。

Method: 提出PSICHE（Probabilistic, Structure-Intrinsic Clustering with a Hierarchical Embedding），即带层次嵌入的概率化结构内在聚类算法，具备四项新颖特性：动态学习且可变的喷注（簇）尺寸；对涌现特征（如喷注子结构与多重数）的无监督多尺度学习；在空间与时间中聚类；纳入领域特定的实验不确定性。所有特性在计算可行的自洽概率框架内实现。

Result: 在LHC场景下针对多种物理现象、不同聚类输入、不同堆积条件和探测器性能参数，演示了该算法的上述特性。

Conclusion: PSICHE通过自洽概率框架统一解决喷注聚类中的多项挑战，具有在粒子物理及其他无监督聚类与关联应用中的潜在通用性。

Abstract: Jet reconstruction is an active and open area of particle physics research, with questions related to jet size, multiplicity, substructure, and experimental performance in the presence of pileup and noise. Analogous challenges are ubiquitous throughout other applications of unsupervised learning in the context of clustering and association. This paper introduces a new algorithm for clustering in the context of jet reconstruction: Probabilistic, Structure-Intrinsic Clustering with an Hierarchical Embedding (PSICHE). PSICHE addresses several challenges and shortcomings of existing jet clustering methods with novel features, including but not limited to: (i) dynamically-learned and variable jet (cluster) sizes, (ii) unsupervised, multi-scale learning of emergent features, like jet substructure and multiplicity, (iii) clustering in space and time, and (iv) incorporation of domain-specific experimental uncertainties. All of these properties are achieved in a self-consistent, probabilistic framework that is computationally tractable. Furthermore, demonstrations of these features are presented for a range of jet clustering examples in LHC scenarios for various physics phenomena, clustering inputs, pileup conditions, and detector performance parameters.

</details>

J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次搜索Cabbibo-suppressed半轻子衰变Λc+→pπ−e+νe，测量分支比为(2.96±0.95±0.23)×10−4，显著性4.2σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Λc+的Cabbibo-suppressed半轻子衰变此前缺乏实验测量，研究其分支比有助于检验重子弱衰变机制。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的4.5 fb−1 e+e−碰撞数据，质心能量4.600–4.699 GeV，对Λc+→pπ−e+νe进行首次搜索。

Result: 测得分支比为(2.96±0.95_stat±0.23_syst)×10−4，信号显著性为4.2σ。

Conclusion: 首次观测到Λc+→pπ−e+νe衰变证据，验证了该Cabbibo-suppressed半轻子衰变道。

Abstract: Based on $4.5\, \mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider at center-of-mass energies between $4.600\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and $4.699\,\mathrm{GeV}$, the first search for the Cabbibo-suppressed semileptonic decay $Λ_c^+\to pπ^-e^+ν_e$ is performed. The branching fraction of $Λ_c^+\to pπ^-e^+ν_e$ is measured to be $(2.96\pm0.95_{\rm stat}\pm0.23_{\rm syst})\times10^{-4}$ with a signal significance of $4.2σ$.

</details>


### [114] [Transverse momentum and azimuthal angle distributions of hadrons in DIS at COMPASS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17222)
*Vendula Benešová*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用2016年非极化液氢靶和160 GeV/c纵向极化μ子束数据，测量了半单举深度非弹性散射中强子多重数及方位角调制不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COMPASS实验在核子结构研究中具有重要地位。通过非极化靶与纵向极化μ子束的高统计量数据，可获取横向动量依赖的部分子分布函数与碎裂函数信息，进而检验Cahn效应、Boer-Mulders函数及束流自旋不对称等物理机制。

Method: 基于2016–2017年非极化液氢靶与160 GeV/c纵向极化μ子束进行半单举深度非弹性散射测量。分析2016年数据集，在x、Q²和P_T微分条件下确定强子多重数，并提取强子方位角分布中的方位角调制振幅；在四维分箱（与多重数一致）及部分变量积分条件下，提取非极化不对称性A^cosφh_UU、A^cos2φh_UU和束流自旋不对称A^sinφh_LU。

Result: 获得了2016年数据集中强子多重数随x、Q²、P_T的分布，以及方位角调制振幅；并在匹配多重数的四维分箱和部分变量积分两种方式下提取了A^cosφh_UU、A^cos2φh_UU与A^sinφh_LU。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究核子结构、横向动量依赖的部分子分布函数与碎裂函数提供了高统计量的实验数据，其中A^cosφh_UU与Cahn效应相关，A^cos2φh_UU对Boer-Mulders函数敏感，A^sinφh_LU为束流自旋不对称，有助于深化对相关物理机制的理解。

Abstract: The COMPASS experiment plays an important role in studies of nucleon structure. Measurements performed in 2016-2017 with an unpolarised liquid hydrogen target and a longitudinally polarised 160 GeV/$c$ muon beam provide a high-statistics data sample for the analysis of semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering, giving access to transverse-momentum-dependent parton distribution and fragmentation functions. The 2016 data set was analysed to determine the multiplicities of hadrons produced in DIS differential in $x$, $Q^2$ and $P_\text{T}$, as well as the amplitudes of azimuthal modulations in the hadron azimuthal-angle distributions. In particular, the unpolarised asymmetries $A^{\cos φ_\text{h}}_\text{UU}$ (related to the Cahn effect), $A^{\cos 2φ_\text{h}}_\text{UU}$ (sensitive to the Boer--Mulders function), and the beam-spin asymmetry $A^{\sin φ_\text{h}}_\text{LU}$ were extracted in 4-dimensional bins matching those of the multiplicities, and also integrated over some of the variables.

</details>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Navigating the Delicate Geometry of Beehive Mite Infestation with Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16316)
*Julia Saff,Bhargav R. Karamched*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文建立包含真社会性Allee效应的蜂巢-螨动力学模型，用分岔分析和最优控制评估化学杀螨策略，发现"冲击-维持"鸡尾酒疗法能在清除螨库与蜂群存活间取得最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寄生螨Varroa destructor对全球蜜蜂种群构成生存威胁，需要通过动力学建模评估化学干预的有效性及其毒性代价。作者希望揭示"软"杀螨剂（作用于携螨期螨）与"硬"杀螨剂（穿透封盖巢房作用于繁殖螨）之间的权衡，并寻找兼顾除螨与蜂群存活的最优策略。

Method: 构建蜂巢-螨相互作用的动力学系统模型，引入真社会性Allee效应；将处理分为作用于携螨期螨的"软"杀螨剂和穿透封盖巢房杀死繁殖螨的"硬"杀螨剂。通过分岔分析考察跨临界分岔与鞍结分岔边界；并用庞特里亚金极大值原理构建最优控制问题，数值求解动态策略；进一步扩展模型纳入抗药性菌株。

Result: 分岔分析揭示根本性权衡：硬处理通过推移跨临界分岔边界有效清除受保护的螨库，但对蜜蜂产生亚致死毒性；超过临界剂量阈值会引发灾难性鞍结分岔，导致蜂群崩溃。数值解表明动态"冲击-维持"鸡尾酒策略最优地平衡了螨库清除与蜂巢存活。扩展模型显示，单一化学药剂依赖迫使蜂巢陷入高毒、近乎不可持续的"化学跑步机"。模型还预测：根除边界与蜂群环境容纳量密切相关，因此螨压力将选择更小、天然抗性的蜂巢，而非商业农业偏好的大型蜂群。

Conclusion: 化学干预存在毒性上限，需通过动态混合策略维持可持续性；单一药剂依赖不可持续。生态上，螨害压力可能驱动蜂群向小型抗性方向演化，对商业农业偏好的大型蜂群模式构成挑战。

Abstract: The parasitic mite Varroa destructor poses a severe existential threat to global honey bee Apis mellifera populations. In this paper, we present a dynamical systems model of hive-mite interactions incorporating a eusocial Allee effect to evaluate the efficacy of chemical interventions. We partition treatments into ``soft'' miticides (targeting phoretic mites) and ``harsh'' miticides (penetrating the capped brood to target reproductive mites). Our bifurcation analysis reveals a fundamental trade-off: while harsh treatments effectively eradicate the protected mite reservoir by shifting the transcritical bifurcation boundary, they impose sublethal toxicity on the bees. We analytically show that exceeding a critical dosage threshold culminates in a catastrophic saddle-node bifurcation that guarantees colony collapse. To navigate this toxicity limit, we formulate an optimal control problem using Pontryagin's Maximum Principle. Numerical solutions reveal that a dynamic ``shock and maintain'' cocktail strategy optimally balances reservoir clearance with hive viability. Expanding the model to include treatment-resistant strains demonstrates that single-chemical reliance forces the hive onto a highly toxic, nearly unsustainable chemical treadmill. Finally, our mathematical framework yields a striking ecological prediction: because the eradication boundary scales intimately with the colony's carrying capacity, unchecked mite pressure will likely exert evolutionary forces that select for smaller, naturally resistant hives over the massive colonies favored by commercial agriculture.

</details>


### [116] [Anomalous First Passage in Evolution: Edge-KPZ Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16499)
*Tetsuhiro S. Hatakeyama*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 中性Wright-Fisher演化中，尽管突变是扩散式的，首次到达指定表型距离的平均时间却呈现反常标度，约随突变方差 (σ²) 的 -3/2 次幂变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演化的快慢取决于新表型出现的速度，因此理解新表型首次出现（首次通过）所需时间的标度规律具有重要意义。此前通常预期扩散式突变会带来常规扩散标度，但实际首次通过行为可能不同。

Method: 研究中性Wright-Fisher演化中的首次通过问题，分析首次到达指定表型距离的平均时间与突变方差 σ² 的标度关系；将溯祖理论（coalescent theory）与稀薄种群边缘处的Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) 涨落结合，建立覆盖三个区域的边缘-KPZ理论，并考察弱选择下的情形。

Result: 首次通过时间呈现反常标度，约与 (σ²)^(-3/2) 成正比；该区域两侧各存在一个交叉点，超出交叉点后恢复为方差倒数的标度。边缘-KPZ理论统一描述这三个区域，且该反常现象在弱选择下依然存在。

Conclusion: 中性Wright-Fisher演化中首次通过存在反常标度，由稀薄种群边缘的KPZ涨落主导，该现象在弱选择下持续存在，说明其对选择具有一定稳健性。

Abstract: The pace of evolution depends on how rapidly new phenotypes arise. We show that neutral Wright-Fisher evolution exhibits anomalous first passage despite diffusive mutations. The mean time for the first individual to reach a prescribed phenotypic distance scales approximately as $(σ^2)^{-3/2}$ with mutation variance $σ^2$. Two crossovers bound this regime, with inverse-variance scaling on either side. Combining coalescent theory with Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) fluctuations at the dilute population edge, we develop an edge-KPZ theory of all three regimes. The anomaly persists under weak selection.

</details>


### [117] [Graph construction in QUBO-based recursive phylogenetic tree reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16640)
*Yoshiki Kanazawa,Ashish Joshi,Takahiko Koyama*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文评估了递归归一化割(Ncut)系统发育重建中序列-亲和度表示的影响，发现图构建（亲和度表示）显著影响分裂恢复准确率，且表示、优化与递归建树需联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育重建的准确性不仅取决于建树方法，还取决于成对序列关系的表示方式（如何将序列关系转化为图/亲和度），但这一表示环节的作用尚缺乏系统评估，因此需要考察不同序列-亲和度表示对递归Ncut重建的影响。

Method: 在递归归一化割（Ncut）框架下评估序列-亲和度表示，将图划分子问题建模为二次无约束二值优化（QUBO）并用模拟分岔（Simulated Bifurcation）求解；使用模拟的氨基酸和核苷酸数据集，跨多种建树设置与演化分歧度，比较归一化bit-score亲和度与由序列相似度变换、演化距离派生的表示，考察互换后(post-swap)精化，并以邻接法（NJ）作为基于距离的比较基准。

Result: 亲和度表示显著影响内部裂分恢复，尤其对核苷酸数据；JC69局部亲和度随分歧度增加保持相对较高准确率，而归一化bit-score和BLAST派生核表示下降更明显。post-swap精化总体改善恢复但不稳定。在WAG和JC69距离下，NJ在所有条件下平均裂分恢复高于相应递归Ncut；但在某些条件下，递归Ncut在BLAST派生对数距离上优于NJ。

Conclusion: 图构建是递归Ncut系统发育重建的重要决定因素；在Ncut中表现良好的表示未必是对成对距离最准确的利用。因此成对表示、亲和度变换、优化与递归建树应联合评估。

Abstract: Molecular sequence data are used to reconstruct evolutionary relationships among taxa, but reconstruction accuracy depends not only on the tree-building method but also on how pairwise sequence relationships are represented. We evaluated sequence-to-affinity representations in a recursive normalized-cut (Ncut) framework whose graph-partitioning subproblems were formulated as quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) models and solved using Simulated Bifurcation. Using simulated amino-acid and nucleotide datasets spanning multiple tree-generation settings and evolutionary divergence, we compared normalized bit-score affinities with representations derived from transformed sequence similarities and evolutionary distances, examined post-swap refinement, and used neighbor joining (NJ) as a distance-based comparator.
  Affinity representation substantially affected internal split recovery, particularly for nucleotide data. JC69-based local affinities maintained comparatively high accuracy as divergence increased, whereas normalized bit-score and BLAST-derived kernel representations declined more markedly. Post-swap refinement generally improved recovery, but not consistently across individual reconstructions. NJ achieved higher mean split recovery than corresponding recursive Ncut reconstructions for WAG and JC69 distances across all evaluated conditions, whereas recursive Ncut outperformed NJ for BLAST-derived logarithmic distances under some conditions.
  These results show that graph construction is an important determinant of recursive Ncut-based phylogenetic reconstruction. A representation that performs well within Ncut does not necessarily provide the most accurate use of the underlying pairwise distances. Pairwise representation, affinity transformation, optimization, and recursive tree construction should therefore be evaluated jointly.

</details>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Gauged AKSZ sigma models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16043)
*Noriaki Ikeda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"规范AKSZ sigma模型"这一新模型类，通过QP-流形构造，以目标分次流形上的几何条件刻画AKSZ结构与规范化的兼容性，并以高维高阶Courant sigma模型的规范化为例进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将AKSZ构造与规范化（gauging）相结合，以统一描述带规范对称性的高阶几何sigma模型，并刻画其一致性条件。

Method: 基于QP-流形（Q-结构与P-结构）构造规范AKSZ sigma模型；利用目标分次流形上的几何条件刻画AKSZ结构与规范化之间的兼容性；以高维高阶Courant sigma模型为具体实例进行显式规范化分析，并引入通量与边界。

Result: 给出了规范高阶Courant sigma模型；在引入通量与边界后，发现矩映射（momentum map）与哈密顿G-空间的推广作为模型的一致性条件出现。

Conclusion: 规范AKSZ sigma模型为高阶几何与规范对称性的统一描述提供了框架，矩映射与哈密顿G-空间的自然推广表明该框架与辛几何、约化理论存在深刻联系。

Abstract: We propose a new class of sigma models that extend AKSZ sigma models, which we refer to as gauged AKSZ sigma models. These models are constructed using QP-manifolds. The compatibility between the AKSZ structure and gauging is characterized by geometric conditions on the target graded manifold. As an example, we explicitly analyze the gauging of higher Courant sigma models in higher dimensions. When fluxes and boundaries are introduced into gauged higher Courant sigma models, generalizations of momentum maps and Hamiltonian $G$-spaces arise as consistency conditions for the models.

</details>


### [119] [Reflectionless edge states in Dirac models of topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16949)
*Alexis Drouot*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究了以弯曲界面相连的拓扑绝缘体有效模型，Dirac算子含带角的domain wall，构造出反射无损耗的边缘态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释物理实验中对拓扑绝缘体弯曲界面边缘态无反射传输的观察，并弥补相关理论构造的缺失。

Method: 在L^2(R^2,C^2)上分析Dirac算子D=D_1σ_1+D_2σ_2+κ(x)σ_3，其中κ为带角的domain wall；在0附近的谱窗内构造透射系数模为1的反射less边缘态（扭曲平面波）。

Result: 对谱窗内大多数能量，构造出反射less边缘态，透射系数模为1；D存在局限在κ^{-1}({0})附近的模式，穿过弯折时既无体损耗也无反射。

Conclusion: 理论结果与物理文献实验观察及作者数值模拟一致，说明弯曲界面处边缘态可无损耗、无反射传输。

Abstract: We study an effective model of topological insulators joined along a bent interface. The Hamiltonian is a Dirac operator $\mathcal{D} = D_1σ_1 + D_2σ_2 + κ(x) σ_3$ on $L^2(\mathbb{R}^2,\mathbb{C}^2)$, where $κ(x)$ represents a domain wall with a corner: while initially straight, it undergoes a bend when passing from one side of $\mathbb{R}^2$ to the other. For most energies in a spectral window centered at $0$, we construct a reflectionless edge state of $\mathcal{D}$: a distorted plane wave with transmission coefficient of modulus $1$. This implies that $\mathcal{D}$ admits modes confined near $κ^{-1}(\{0\})$, that travel through the bend with no loss neither to the bulk nor to reflection. Our result explains experimental observations from the physics literature, as well as our own numerical simulations.

</details>


### [120] [Operator-theoretic and analytic properties of a driven single-mode Kerr cavity in Bargmann space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16969)
*Maciej Janowicz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Bargmann--Fock空间中研究相干驱动单模Kerr腔的定态谱：正Kerr耦合下哈密顿量自伴、下有界且具紧预解式；非零驱动时用极小解连分式给出精确标量谱条件（特征值全为单重）；经Liouville变换化为退化双合流Heun问题；用Olver型Volterra构造给出无穷远归一化扇区解及显式误差界；强驱动下证明首阶转折点能量系数，次阶系数依赖全局连接猜想，并以Bogoliubov与复WKB独立验证；弱驱动形式展开由Bargmann递推验证，上边缘三对角级数闭式求和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干驱动单模Kerr腔是量子光学中产生非经典态（如薛定谔猫态）的核心模型，但其在Bargmann--Fock表示下的定态谱结构（尤其是强驱动区的谱渐近）缺乏严格数学刻画。作者旨在建立该谱问题的严格谱理论框架，将特征值方程与已知特殊函数（退化双合流Heun方程）联系，并在强驱动与弱驱动两个极限给出可验证的谱渐近结果，从而为相关物理计算提供可靠数学基础。

Method: 采用Bargmann--Fock空间表示；对正Kerr耦合证明哈密顿量自伴、下有界、紧预解式；利用极小解连分式构造精确标量谱条件；通过Liouville变换将特征值方程化为退化双合流Heun方程并分类奇点；采用Olver型Volterra积分方程构造无穷远处归一化扇区解并给出显式误差界；强驱动区用转折点分析证明首阶能量系数，配合Bogoliubov与复WKB作一致性检验；弱驱动区由Bargmann递推验证形式展开并对上边缘三对角级数作闭式求和。

Result: 正Kerr耦合下哈密顿量自伴、下有界且具紧预解式；非零驱动时极小解连分式给出精确标量谱条件，所有特征值均为单重；特征值方程被识别为退化双合流Heun问题并完成奇点分类；Olver型Volterra构造给出无穷远归一化扇区解及显式误差界；强驱动下首阶转折点能量系数得到严格证明，次阶系数在给定全局连接猜想下成立，Bogoliubov与复WKB计算提供一致性检验；弱驱动形式展开由Bargmann递推验证，其上边缘三对角级数获得闭式求和。

Conclusion: 本文为相干驱动单模Kerr腔的定态谱提供了严格的数学框架：将谱问题精确归结为退化双合流Heun方程并给出扇区解的误差可控构造。强驱动渐近中首阶系数严格、次阶系数仍待全局连接猜想的证明，弱驱动展开则可严格验证且级数可闭式求和。探索性WKB迭代图像仅作直观辅助，不构成对猜想性全局连接的证明。

Abstract: We study the stationary spectrum of a coherently driven single-mode Kerr cavity in Bargmann--Fock space. For positive Kerr coupling the Hamiltonian is self-adjoint, bounded below, and has compact resolvent. For nonzero drive, a minimal-solution continued fraction yields an exact scalar spectral condition, with all eigenvalues simple. A Liouville transformation identifies the eigenvalue equation with a degenerate double-confluent Heun problem and classifies its singularities. An Olver-type Volterra construction gives normalized sectorial solutions at infinity with explicit error bounds. In the strong-drive regime, the leading turning-point energy coefficient is proved, whereas the next coefficient remains conditional on a stated global-connection conjecture; independent Bogoliubov and complex-WKB calculations provide consistency checks. A formal weak-drive expansion is verified from the Bargmann recurrence, and its upper-edge tridiagonal series is summed in closed form. Exploratory WKB iteration portraits are included only as visual aids and do not establish the conjectural global connection.

</details>


### [121] [Diassociative conformal algebras and their deformation cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17441)
*Anupam Sahoo,Apurba Das*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入双结合共形代数，统一结合共形代数与双结合代数；证明其范畴等价于形式分布双结合代数的等价类范畴；借助Maurer-Cartan刻画定义上同调并证明其具有Gerstenhaber代数结构；该上同调可用于研究形变与扩张；最后定义结合共形代数上的平均算子并揭示其与双结合共形代数的密切关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合共形代数与双结合代数分别在不同背景下被研究，缺乏统一框架；同时需要为双结合共形代数建立上同调理论以刻画其形变与扩张，并探索平均算子与双结合结构之间的联系。

Method: 1) 引入双结合共形代数概念，统一结合共形代数与双结合代数；2) 建立其范畴与形式分布双结合代数等价类范畴的等价性；3) 利用Maurer-Cartan刻画构造上同调；4) 证明上同调上承载Gerstenhaber代数结构；5) 定义结合共形代数上的平均算子。

Result: 双结合共形代数范畴与形式分布双结合代数的等价类范畴等价；所构造的上同调具有Gerstenhaber代数结构，并可应用于研究结构的形变与扩张；平均算子与双结合共形代数之间存在密切联系。

Conclusion: 该工作为双结合共形代数提供了统一框架与上同调理论，揭示了其与形式分布结构、平均算子之间的深刻联系，为后续形变、扩张及相关代数结构研究奠定基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce diassociative conformal algebras that unify both associative conformal algebras and diassociative algebras. We show that the category of diassociative conformal algebras is equivalent to the category of equivalence classes of formal distribution diassociative algebras. By employing its Maurer-Cartan characterization, we then define the cohomology of a diassociative conformal algebra and show that it carries a Gerstenhaber algebra structure. Our cohomology can be used to study deformations and extensions of the structure. Finally, we define averaging operators on associative conformal algebras and find their close relations with diassociative conformal algebras.

</details>


### [122] [Higher abelian gauge theory in BV formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17518)
*Shuhan Jiang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二次椭圆高阿贝尔规范理论的零模空间上构造BV结构，证明相关BV积分在温和假设下不依赖于零模拉格朗日量的选择，并给出若干p-形式阿贝尔规范理论的显式零模配分函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阿贝尔规范理论中零模的处理通常依赖人为选取的零模拉格朗日量，其合法性及对最终物理结果的影响缺乏系统论证。本文旨在为椭圆型二次高阿贝尔规范理论建立严格的BV（Batalin–Vilkovisky）框架，厘清零模积分的定义及其规范无关性。

Method: 在二次椭圆高阿贝尔规范理论的零模空间上构造BV结构（包括反括号、算子与作用量），定义相应的BV积分，并在温和假设下证明该积分与零模拉格朗日量的选取无关。随后将构造应用于具体的p-形式阿贝尔规范理论以计算零模配分函数。

Result: 证明了相关BV积分在温和假设下独立于零模拉格朗日量的选择，并由此得到了若干p-形式阿贝尔规范理论的显式零模配分函数。

Conclusion: 该工作为高阿贝尔规范理论零模的BV量子化提供了统一且规范无关的处理方式，显式配分函数的结果可用于进一步的物理与数学分析。

Abstract: A BV structure is constructed on the space of zero modes of a quadratic elliptic higher abelian gauge theory. The associated BV integral is shown to be independent of the choice of zero-mode Lagrangian under mild hypotheses. The construction yields explicit zero-mode partition functions for several $p$-form abelian gauge theories.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Bi-MoDe: Bilateral Control-based Imitation Learning via Modifier-Conditioned Decoding for Modulation of Execution Speed and Contact Intensity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16040)
*Takumi Kobayashi,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Bi-MoDe，一种通过 adaLN-Zero 将约束潜在变量注入 Transformer 动作解码器每一层的修饰条件解码框架，使行为指令直接作用于动作块生成，在白板擦拭任务上提升物理指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于双边控制的模仿学习能同时捕获位置与力信息，适合接触丰富的操作任务，但现有方法难以让操作者在推理阶段指定任务执行方式（如快慢、轻柔或用力）。

Method: 提出 Bi-MoDe 修饰条件解码框架，通过 adaLN-Zero 将约束潜在变量注入 Transformer 动作解码器的每一层，使行为指令直接影响动作块（action-chunk）生成。在真实世界白板擦拭任务上，以时间与物理修饰符的组合进行评估。

Result: Bi-MoDe 相比动作分块基线提升了物理指令跟随能力，同时保持可比的时间控制。消融实验表明解码器条件化与潜在空间组合存在交互作用，二者的结合对准确跟随物理指令很重要。

Conclusion: 将修正条件注入解码器各层并辅以潜在空间组合，是提升接触丰富任务中行为指令可控性的有效途径。

Abstract: Bilateral control-based imitation learning captures both position and force information, making it well suited to contact-rich manipulation. However, existing approaches provide limited means for an operator to specify how a learned task should be executed at inference time, such as slowly or quickly, gently or firmly. We propose Bi-MoDe, a modifier-conditioned decoding framework that injects a constrained latent into every layer of the Transformer action decoder via adaLN-Zero, allowing behavioral directives to directly influence action-chunk generation. We evaluate the method on a real-world whiteboard wiping task with combinations of temporal and physical modifiers. Bi-MoDe improves physical directive following over the action-chunking baseline while maintaining comparable temporal control. An ablation further shows that decoder conditioning and latent-space composition interact, and that their combination is important for accurate physical directive following. Additional material is available at the https://mertcookimg.github.io/bi-mode/

</details>


### [124] [MR-GLi: Mixed Reality-Based Gripper-Linked Overlays for Underwater Robot Arm Teleoperation via Bilateral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16041)
*Masashi Sasago,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MR-GLi混合现实界面，将反作用力矩指示器与腕部相机图像空间配准到机器人夹爪上，验证其在水下双边遥操作中的可行性与优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉力矩反馈可支持水下双边遥操作，但混合现实相较传统2D显示器是否具有优势尚不明确，尤其在信息获取方式对操作负担的影响方面缺乏研究。

Method: 20名被试在平衡化被试内设计中完成举升与抓取-放置任务，操作刚性与柔性物体；对比MR-GLi与2D显示器，二者视觉反馈内容相同，均采用四通道双边控制。

Result: MR-GLi在力矩调节性能上与2D显示器相当，并提供与夹爪联动的视觉反馈获取方式；主观评价显示，被试在切换工作空间与视觉反馈之间的感知负担降低。

Conclusion: 验证了夹爪联动式MR叠加层用于水下双边遥操作的可行性，并强调在任务性能之外还需重视信息获取方式的影响。

Abstract: Visual torque feedback supports underwater bilateral teleoperation, but the benefit of mixed reality (MR) over conventional monitor presentation remains unclear. We present MR-GLi, an MR interface that spatially registers a reaction torque indicator and wrist-camera image to the robot gripper. Twenty participants performed lift and pick-and-place tasks with rigid and compliant objects in a counterbalanced within-subject comparison with a 2D monitor, using identical visual-feedback content and four-channel bilateral control. MR-GLi provided gripper-linked access to visual feedback while maintaining a similar level of torque-regulation performance to the 2D monitor. Subjective evaluation further indicated reduced perceived burden associated with shifting attention between the workspace and visual feedback. These results demonstrate the feasibility of gripper-linked MR overlays for underwater bilateral teleoperation and highlight the importance of considering information access in addition to task performance. Additional material: https://mertcookimg.github.io/mr-gli/

</details>


### [125] [World-Action Models for Robot Learning and Control: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16074)
*Zuxing Lu,Hongjia Zhai,Guanzhi Wang,Huajian Zeng,Jiaqi Yang,Jingyu Liu,Lei Cheng,Yuantai Zhang,Yuheng Qiu,Zezhou Cheng,Ivan Laptev,Danfei Xu,Benjamin Riviere,Giuseppe Loianno,Eric Xing,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述面向机器人领域系统梳理"世界-动作模型"(WAMs),建立统一分类体系,回顾应用、数据集、评测协议与开放挑战,旨在为预测性世界建模与动作生成的融合提供技术基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放环境中的机器人面临部分可观测性、物理约束和动态任务情境,不仅需将观测与语言指令映射为动作,还需预判候选动作对未来状态及任务结果的影响。世界模型、视频生成与VLA策略的进展催生了耦合未来预测与动作生成的WAMs,但该领域缺乏面向机器人学的系统梳理。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法:界定WAMs相对传统世界模型、基于模型的强化学习、动作条件视频生成和反应式VLA策略的范围边界;构建统一分类法,涵盖表示、转移建模、动作接口、架构、训练流程、数据模态与扩展策略;并整理应用、数据集、基准、指标与评测协议。

Result: 形成了WAMs的统一技术分类框架,系统归纳其在操作、导航和自动驾驶中的应用,汇总了现有数据集、基准、指标与评测协议,并识别出动作对齐、世界-动作解耦、空间与多视角一致性、长时记忆、闭环策略学习的神经仿真以及高效推理等关键挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述为预测性世界建模与动作生成的整合提供了简洁的技术基础,指向更可靠的具身机器人智能,并通过项目主页持续维护相关资源。

Abstract: Robots operating in open environments act under partial observability, physical constraints, and dynamic task contexts. Beyond mapping observations and language instructions to actions, they must anticipate how candidate actions may affect future states and task-relevant outcomes. Recent advances in world models, video generation, and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have motivated the development of World-Action Models (WAMs), which couple future world prediction with executable action generation. This survey provides a robotics-oriented review of WAMs. We clarify their scope relative to conventional world models, model-based reinforcement learning, action-conditioned video generation, and reactive VLA policies, and organize existing methods through a unified taxonomy covering representations, transition modeling, action interfaces, architectures, training pipelines, data modalities, and scaling strategies. We further review applications of WAMs in manipulation, navigation, and autonomous driving, and we summarize the datasets, benchmarks, metrics, and protocols used to evaluate WAM systems. Finally, we discuss key challenges in action alignment, world-action factorization, spatial and multi-view consistency, long-horizon memory, neural simulation for closed-loop policy learning, and efficient inference. Taken together, this survey aims to provide a concise technical foundation for integrating predictive world modeling with action generation, toward more reliable embodied robot intelligence. Project page: https://rcl-robotics.github.io/Awesome-World-Action-Models.

</details>


### [126] [AssemblyGrid v1: A Benchmark for Multi-Robot Production with Temporary Coalitions, Local Information, and Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16075)
*Fouad Bahrpeyma,David Heik,Dirk Reichelt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 AssemblyGrid v1——一个面向多机器人柔性生产、可复现的任务级基准，涵盖流程推进、去中心化观测、物料转运、临时联盟与并发执行，并通过一致性检查、机制研究与集中式/去中心化及 MARL 算法实验验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性机器人生产要求对流程推进、物料路由、资源分配、临时协作与并发执行进行联合决策，各决策相互影响可行性；在去中心化控制下，各机器人仅依据有限局部信息行动，而系统进展依赖集体决策、共享资源、物料状态与工作空间兼容性，这构成典型的部分可观测与资源竞争下的协作多智能体决策难题，且缺乏统一、可复现的基准。

Method: 提出 AssemblyGrid v1 基准：在单一任务级形式化中整合显式流程推进、去中心化观测、物料传递、临时多机器人联盟、生产性并发与几何相关可行性；设计 Flow、Coalition、Concurrency 三类工作负载，每类含三个场景等级；任务成功与评价指标独立于学习奖励和求解方法；通过可执行一致性检查、机制研究以及算法实验（特权集中式参考、结构化去中心化控制器、IPPO/MAPPO/QMIX 等 MARL 方法）进行评估。

Result: 在集中式与去中心化控制下均实现了有效生产执行；MARL 实验表明去中心化策略能够仅凭局部观测与动作学习到有效的生产行为。

Conclusion: AssemblyGrid 可作为研究柔性机器人生产中协作决策的受控基准。

Abstract: Flexible robotic production requires joint decisions on process progression, material routing, resource assignment, temporary cooperation, and simultaneous execution, since each decision can affect the feasibility of the others. The challenge is greater under decentralized control, where each robot acts from bounded local information while system progress depends on collective decisions, shared resources, material state, and workspace compatibility. These properties closely match cooperative multi-agent decision making under partial observability and resource contention. This paper introduces AssemblyGrid v1, a reproducible benchmark for repeated multi-robot production that combines explicit process progression, decentralized observations, material transfer, temporary multi-robot coalitions, productive concurrency, and geometry-dependent feasibility within one task-level formulation. The benchmark includes Flow, Coalition, and Concurrency workload families, each with three scenario levels.
