Task success and evaluation measures are defined independently of learning reward and solution method, allowing learning-based and non-learning methods to address the same production problem. AssemblyGrid v1 is evaluated through executable conformance checks, mechanism studies, and algorithmic experiments using a privileged centralized reference, structured decentralized controllers, and MARL methods including IPPO, MAPPO, and QMIX. Results demonstrate productive execution under centralized and decentralized control. The MARL experiments further show that decentralized policies can learn effective production behavior from local observations and actions, supporting AssemblyGrid as a controlled benchmark for studying cooperative decision making in flexible robotic production.

</details>


### [127] [Structure-Preserving Quantum Circuit Architectures for Robot Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16089)
*Andrea Morghen,Pierluigi Arpenti,Roberto Schiattarella,Giovanni Acampora,Bruno Siciliano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向刚性变换的量子表示与电路架构，并专门用于串联开链机械臂的DH运动学，通过Bloch向量编码方向、选择器寄存器编码加权求和、读出量子比特恢复末端位姿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化空间数据需要既能保持几何关系、又暴露可观测量并可在有限深度硬件实现的量子编码。现有量子编码与运动学表示之间缺乏对几何可解释性和硬件可实现性的兼顾。

Method: 将每个平移分量分解为经典度量幅值与由单量子比特Bloch向量编码的有符号单位方向；参数化旋转复现坐标系的顺序传播。用选择器寄存器制备正比于各分量幅值的概率，指定读出量子比特的约化态编码其归一化加权和，再由保留的经典尺度重建度量末端位置；另设两个读出量子比特编码末端坐标系轴向。给出多种电路架构以实现相同表示并权衡量子比特数、电路深度、受控操作与测量需求。

Result: 在理想期望值层面，测量的Pauli可观测量可复现对应经典运动学量。串联机械臂验证显示位置与姿态重建误差在理想模拟下数值可忽略；有限采样、含噪执行、转译分析与硬件演示进一步刻画了统计误差、噪声敏感度与实现开销。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了几何可解释、位姿接口紧凑的量子运动学表示与电路实现，在不主张计算优势的前提下，系统评估了其统计、噪声与转译开销。

Abstract: Structured spatial data require quantum encodings that preserve geometric relations, expose measurable observables, and remain implementable on finite-depth hardware. This work introduces a quantum representation and circuit architecture for rigid-body transformations and specializes it to Denavit--Hartenberg kinematics of serial open-chain manipulators. Each translational contribution is factorized into a classical metric magnitude and a signed unit direction encoded by a single-qubit Bloch vector, while parameterized rotations reproduce the ordered propagation of frame directions. A selector register prepares probabilities proportional to the contribution magnitudes, and the reduced state of a designated readout qubit encodes their normalized weighted sum. The retained classical scale then reconstructs the metric end-effector position. Two additional readout qubits encode terminal-frame axes, providing a compact and geometrically interpretable pose interface. At the ideal expectation-value level, measured Pauli observables reproduce the corresponding classical kinematic quantities. Alternative circuit architectures realize the same representation with different tradeoffs in qubit count, circuit depth, controlled operations, and measurement requirements. Validation on a serial manipulator yields numerically negligible position and orientation reconstruction errors under ideal simulation. Finite-shot simulations, noisy executions, transpilation analysis, and a hardware demonstration further characterize statistical error, noise sensitivity, and implementation overhead without asserting computational advantage.

</details>


### [128] [Occupancy Network-Guided Autonomous Robotic Partial Nephrectomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16186)
*Ethan Kilmer,Pit Henrich,Jiawei Ge,Paul M. Scheikl,Laura Connolly,Soum D. Lokeshwar,Joseph Chen,Justin D. Opfermann,Kaitlyn Kumar,Lauren Shepard,Ahmed Ghazi,Nirmish Singla,Richard J. Cha,Kevin Cleary,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich,Axel Krieger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出首个视觉引导的自主软组织肿瘤切除系统，可在组织变形或切开后持续感知并自适应手术，在患者来源水凝胶体模上完成8次连续自主肾部分切除，全部实现阴性切缘，平均绝对切缘误差1.61±0.48 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主软组织肿瘤手术仅能处理器官表面干预，原因是系统无法在组织变形或被切开后感知并适应解剖结构变化。因此需要一种能在术中持续推断完整三维解剖并自主调整切除策略的系统，以实现完整肿瘤切除。

Method: 系统集成条件占用网络，完全在基于物理的仿真中训练，可从单视角部分点云推断完整三维解剖（肿瘤、边缘组织、肾脏）；占用网络在组织被切开和变形时仍能维持术中跟踪，从而支持自适应规划与执行。手术平台包括深度相机采集表面点云、双臂机器人进行电外科切割和真空组织操作，以及自主控制策略完成肿瘤切除。

Result: 在开放式肾部分切除条件下，于患者来源水凝胶体模中，机器人连续完成8次自主肿瘤切除，共77次电外科切割，所有切割均达到阴性手术切缘，平均绝对切缘误差为1.61±0.48 mm。

Conclusion: 该工作首次演示了面向监督式自主闭环、影像驱动、切缘阴性肿瘤切除的基础框架（在体模中验证），为软组织自主手术提供了可行性依据，并为后续临床转化研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Autonomous soft-tissue cancer surgery has been limited to interventions on organ surfaces, because current systems cannot perceive and adapt to anatomy once it deforms or is cut. We introduce the first vision-guided autonomous system capable of performing complete tumor resections for partial nephrectomy. Our system integrates conditional occupancy networks, trained entirely in a physics-based simulation, that infer full 3-D anatomy (tumor, margin tissue, and kidney) from single-view partial point clouds. These occupancy networks maintain intraoperative tracking even as tissue is cut and deformed, enabling adaptive planning and execution. The surgical platform combines a depth camera for capturing surface point clouds, dual robotic arms for electrosurgical cutting and vacuum-based tissue manipulation, and an autonomous control strategy for tumor resection. In patient-derived hydrogel phantoms under an open partial nephrectomy setting, the robot performed eight consecutive autonomous tumor resections comprising 77 electrosurgical cuts, with all cuts achieving negative surgical margins and 1.61 $\pm$ 0.48 mm mean absolute margin error. This work demonstrates, for the first time, a foundation for supervised autonomous closed-loop, imaging-driven, margin-negative tumor removal in phantoms.

</details>


### [129] [Tendon-Driven Continuum Robot with Modular Stiffness and In-Situ Self Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16256)
*Guo Ning,Sue,Zheng Cao,Junzhe Hu,Xiangyun Bu,David Quinn,Tiancheng Wu,Zackory Erickson,Carmel Majidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自包含模块化连续体机器人平台，将机械可重构性与本体位姿估计相结合，实现无需外部跟踪的自感知与自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续体机器人多为任务专用，且依赖外部传感基础设施，限制了其适应性与实际部署。

Method: 采用可互换的连续体关节，刚度经解析预计算，支持快速组装与形状直接编程；本体感知通过磁传感器与模块化学习框架实现，每个关节训练单一模型并跨配置复用。

Result: 在真实环境中完成实验验证，展示了自感知能力及无需外部跟踪的自适应表现。

Conclusion: 该平台通过机械可重构与本体感知结合，为连续体机器人的通用化与实地部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Continuum robots enable smooth shape morphing and safe interaction in confined environments. However, most existing systems are task-specific and depend on external sensing infrastructure, limiting their adaptability and real-world deployment. This paper presents a self-contained modular continuum robotic platform that combines mechanical reconfigurability with onboard pose estimation. The robot is constructed from interchangeable continuum joints with analytically precomputed stiffness, allowing rapid assembly and direct programming of the robot shape. Proprioceptive sensing is achieved using magnetic sensors and a modular learning-based framework, where a single model is trained per joint and reused across configurations. The system is experimentally validated in real world, demonstrating self-sensing capabilities and adaptation without external tracking.

</details>


### [130] [ConGraspXL: Controllable Constraint-Conditioned Dexterous Grasping Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16319)
*Hui Zhang,Mirko Meboldt,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出ConGraspXL，在GraspXL基础上实现了可控的约束条件抓取运动合成，支持多种任务驱动约束及其组合，并保持强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓取通常针对特定任务，产生异构约束（如特定接近方向、期望接触区域、指定手腕轨迹、功能性手部姿态）。作者此前工作GraspXL虽能对多样物体和手部形态进行可扩展抓取运动合成，但缺乏在这些任务驱动约束下的可控性。

Method: 提出ConGraspXL：引入分层约束表示，通过掩码残差接口实现灵活约束组合，并利用动态手部中心和前馈手腕引导提升控制精度。

Result: 在不损失GraspXL强泛化能力的前提下，ConGraspXL实现了对多种单一约束及其组合的精确灵活可控性。

Conclusion: ConGraspXL可作为即插即用的底层抓取控制器，服务于全身抓取补全、功能性抓取和人体运动模仿等下游应用。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping is usually conducted for specific tasks, leading to heterogeneous constraints such as specific approach directions, desired contact regions, specified wrist trajectories, and functional hand poses. Our previous work, GraspXL, achieves scalable grasping motion synthesis for diverse objects and hand morphologies, while lacking controllability for synthesis under such various task-driven constraints. In this paper, we propose ConGraspXL, which extends GraspXL with controllable constraint-conditioned grasp motion synthesis that accommodates diverse task-driven constraints and their combinations. We introduce a hierarchical constraint formulation, enable flexible constraint composition with a masked residual interface, and improve control precision with dynamic hand centers and feed-forward wrist guidance. Without losing the strong generalization capabilities of GraspXL, ConGraspXL enables precise and flexible controllability for various individual constraints and their combinations, providing a plug-and-play low-level grasp controller for downstream applications such as whole-body grasp completion, functional grasping, and human-motion imitation.

</details>


### [131] [ManiSkillFormer: Demonstration-Free Compositional Manipulation via Task-Conditioned Geometric Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16331)
*Peiqi Yu,Mosam Dabhi,Shangtao Li,Bowei Li,Laszlo Jeni,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManiSkillFormer 是一个神经符号框架，实现无演示、可组合的机器人操作：通过任务条件几何契约显式结构化感知与动作接口，由 LLM 生成契约与运动模板，并借助技能库复用模板完成多任务操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端视觉运动策略需要大量演示且难以跨物体、跨任务组合复用；作者希望在不依赖逐物体策略微调和额外演示的前提下，实现可组合、可复用的操作能力。

Method: 提出任务条件几何契约，规定每项操作技能所需的语义几何基元（如物体关键点、表面法向）；基于人工定义的技能结构，用 LLM 智能体生成契约及面向不同物体与任务上下文的运动模板；契约引导感知模块从观测中定位任务相关的 3D 基元，进而实例化存入技能库的可复用运动模板。

Result: 在 Galaxea R1-Lite 双臂机器人上评估三类设置：8 类物体、30 个实例的零样本抓放，功能操作（拧松、倾倒、按压、折叠）及 3 项长时程任务。平均成功率优于基线与两条消融流程：无演示抓放 88.24%，功能操作平均 75.00%，长时程任务完成率 50–80%。

Conclusion: 该设计实现了跨物体、跨任务的可组合与可复用操作，无需逐物体策略微调或额外机器人演示，验证了神经符号几何契约方法的有效性。

Abstract: We present ManiSkillFormer, a neuro-symbolic framework for demonstration-free and compositional robotic manipulation. Instead of learning end-to-end visuomotor policies, ManiSkillFormer introduces task-conditioned geometric contracts that explicitly structure the interface between perception and action. Each manipulation skill declares the semantic geometric primitives required for execution, such as object keypoints and surface normals. Building on human-defined skill structures, LLM agents generate these contracts and corresponding motion templates for different objects and task contexts. These contracts guide the perception module to ground task-relevant 3D primitives from observations, which are then used to instantiate reusable motion templates stored in a skill library. We evaluate ManiSkillFormer on Galaxea R1-Lite dual-arm robot across three settings: zero-shot pick-and-place over 8 object categories with 30 different instances, functional manipulation tasks including unscrewing, pouring, pressing, and folding, and 3 long-horizon tasks. ManiSkillFormer achieves higher average success rates than the evaluated baselines and two ablated pipelines: 88.24% for demonstration-free pick-and-place, 75.00% average success on functional manipulation and 50--80% completion rates across the long-horizon tasks. These results show that our design enables composable and reusable manipulation across objects and tasks without per-object policy fine-tuning or additional robot demonstrations.

</details>


### [132] [Auto-HSI: Personalized human control of a robot swarm on demand by using LLMs for online automatic code generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16346)
*Alessandro Nazzari,Nathan Cerisara,Dorian Tonnis,Raina Zakir,Lorenzo Labarile,Weixu Zhu,Marco Dorigo,Mary Katherine Heinrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Auto-HSI：通过自然语言描述与手势示教，按需自动生成个性化的机器人集群交互界面与状态机控制代码，支持集中式遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使未经培训的操作员无需编程，即可用自然语言和手势示教来自定义机器人集群对其手势的集体行为响应，降低集群交互界面的开发门槛。

Method: Auto-HSI 原型接收自然语言描述与手势演示作为输入，自动生成个性化状态机代码，实现集中式控制界面；支持单手/双手手势，用于遥操作机器人运动、编队形状及形状变形。

Result: 对手势跟踪与代码生成模块进行了性能基准测试；在基于物理的仿真器中由真实操作员集中控制50个模拟机器人，在正常与噪声条件下完成进球、穿越需变形的迷宫、分组同时进球两项任务；还演示了操作过程中对个性化界面的实时更新，并展示真实机器人的实时运行。

Conclusion: Auto-HSI 验证了按需生成个性化集群交互界面的可行性，可支持未经训练用户在仿真与真实场景中完成复杂集群任务并进行运行时界面定制。

Abstract: This paper presents Auto-HSI, a method for generating personalized human-swarm interaction (HSI) interfaces on demand. The objective is to enable untrained operators to use natural language descriptions and gesture demonstrations to explain how they want the robots to collectively behave in response to their gestures. Based on these inputs, the code should automatically be generated for personalized state machines that will control the robots as desired, in response to the desired gesture inputs. In the developed Auto-HSI prototype, the generated code produces a personalized interface for centralized control using one- and two-handed gestures, enabling a user to teleoperate the robots' motion, formation shape, and shape deformation. We test the gesture tracking and code generation components of Auto-HSI against performance benchmarks. We then test the full Auto-HSI prototype in ``live'' operation experiments, in which real human operators centrally control 50 simulated robots in a physics-based simulator, under nominal and noisy conditions. In these experiments, robots are teleoperated to: score a goal, traverse a maze that requires shape deformation, and score two simultaneous goals by splitting into two groups. We also demonstrate a real human operator making live updates to their personalized Auto-HSI interface during operation (in simulation). Finally, we demonstrate live operation of real robots.

</details>


### [133] [UDAV: Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive VLM Waypoint Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16368)
*Ghazal Farhani,Shabnam Shabani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UDAV：面向无人机引导无人地面车辆导航的不确定性驱动自适应VLM航点规划器，通过随机轨迹采样与中位轨迹选择估计预测不确定性，并据此选择性触发重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）可直接从航空影像生成越野导航路线，但其预测缺乏可靠性指示，无法判断何时可信、何时需要修正，存在较大规划误差风险。

Method: UDAV采样多条随机轨迹预测，选取其medoid作为自洽的标称路线，并以轨迹间的空间离散度估计预测不确定性；当内部航点最大不确定性超过阈值时触发"reconsideration（重新考虑）"阶段，否则直接返回medoid。

Result: 在两个无人机航班采集的400条留出轨迹查询上评估：随机medoid选择将平均位移误差（ADE）从确定性预测的147.4像素降至115.9像素；完整规划器平均ADE为110.4像素，较确定性规划降低25.1%，且对所有查询均产生有效轨迹；在包括更高预算K=10共识基线在内的所有配置中，其90分位与95分位误差最低，相较K=5 medoid分别由225.3、326.0像素降至199.0、290.8像素。

Conclusion: 随机VLM预测既能提供更强的标称路线，也能提供可操作的置信度信号，用于选择性缓解大幅规划误差，验证了不确定性驱动自适应规划的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can generate routes directly from aerial imagery for off-road navigation, but their predictions provide no indication of reliability. We present UDAV, an Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive VLM Waypoint Planner for UAV-guided UGV navigation. UDAV draws multiple stochastic trajectory predictions, selects their medoid as a self-consistent nominal route, and estimates predictive uncertainty from their spatial dispersion. When the maximum uncertainty across interior waypoints exceeds a threshold, UDAV invokes a reconsideration stage; otherwise, it returns the medoid directly. We evaluate UDAV on 400 held-out trajectory queries from two UAV flights. Stochastic medoid selection reduces the mean average displacement error (ADE) from 147.4 pixels for a deterministic prediction to 115.9 pixels. The complete planner achieves a mean ADE of 110.4 pixels, a 25.1% reduction relative to deterministic planning, while producing valid trajectories for all queries. UDAV also yields the lowest 90th- and 95th-percentile errors among all evaluated configurations, including a higher-budget K=10 consensus baseline. Relative to the K=5 medoid, UDAV reduces these errors from 225.3 and 326.0 pixels to 199.0 and 290.8 pixels, respectively. These results demonstrate that stochastic VLM predictions provide both a stronger nominal route and an actionable uncertainty signal for selectively mitigating large planning errors.

</details>


### [134] [Geometry vs Structure: Graph-Based Diagnostics for LiDAR Point-Cloud Simulation Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16378)
*Ghazal Farhani,Taufiq Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于图的谱分析框架，用 $r_\lambda$ 指标评估仿真LiDAR点云的结构保真度，与几何指标CDC互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生为自动驾驶与ADAS传感器验证提供可扩展、低成本的补充手段，但其保真度量化困难，尤其对3D LiDAR点云：传统几何度量会忽略重要的结构差异，无法显式刻画连通性、拓扑与对象级组织。

Method: 对真实与仿真点云分别构图，用Louvain社区检测识别空间一致子图，并基于质心邻近度匹配对应社区；对每个匹配对计算有界图谱指标 $r_\lambda$（受Weyl不等式启发），并以密度感知Chamfer距离(CDC)作为几何基线进行对比；通过受控扰动实验检验指标性质。

Result: 受控扰动实验表明 $r_\lambda$ 对刚体变换不变、对传感器噪声鲁棒，同时对结构形变敏感。在50对真实（Velodyne VLP-32C）与仿真（CARLA）LiDAR扫描上评估，跨车辆、植被、树木、建筑墙面四类共获得1000余个匹配社区；结果显示几何度量与结构度量捕捉到仿真保真度的互补方面。

Conclusion: 图谱分析可作为数字孪生验证的附加诊断层，用于ADAS与自动驾驶应用，弥补几何指标在结构层面的不足。

Abstract: Digital twins provide a scalable and cost-effective complement to real-world testing for validating autonomous-driving and advanced driver-assistance system (ADAS) sensor pipelines. However, quantifying their fidelity remains challenging, particularly for 3D LiDAR point clouds, where conventional geometric metrics may overlook important structural discrepancies. We present a graph-based framework for evaluating the structural fidelity of simulated LiDAR point clouds against real-world scans. While scan-level metrics such as Chamfer distance capture point-wise geometric similarity, they do not explicitly represent connectivity, topology, or object-level organization. Our framework constructs graphs from real and simulated point clouds, applies Louvain community detection to identify spatially coherent subgraphs, and matches corresponding communities using centroid proximity. For each matched pair, we compute $r_λ$, a bounded graph-spectral metric motivated by Weyl's inequality, and compare it with density-aware Chamfer distance (CDC) as a geometric baseline. Controlled perturbation experiments demonstrate that $r_λ$ is invariant to rigid transformations and robust to sensor noise while remaining sensitive to structural deformation. We evaluate the framework on 50 paired real and simulated LiDAR scans acquired using a Velodyne VLP-32C sensor and CARLA, respectively. The dataset contains more than 1,000 matched communities across four representative classes: vehicles, vegetation, trees, and building walls. The results show that geometric and structural measures capture complementary aspects of simulation fidelity, supporting graph-spectral analysis as an additional diagnostic layer for validating digital twins in ADAS and autonomous-driving applications.

</details>


### [135] [Collision-Aware Humanoid Whole-Body Control under Imperfect Tracking Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16405)
*Mohitvishnu S. Gadde,Ashish Malik,Pranay Dugar,Aayam Kumar Shrestha,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RECAL——一种机器人-环境交叉注意力层，包裹现有全身控制器（WBC），利用外部场景几何在目标跟踪与碰撞避免之间权衡，实现碰撞感知的浮动基座与末端执行器指令跟踪，并在仿真与真实Digit V3人形机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身控制器多对场景几何"盲视"，当目标运动因感知、规划或遥操作误差而不安全时，易导致碰撞。因此需要一种能引入外部几何信息、在跟踪目标的同时避免碰撞的通用方案。

Method: RECAL通过点云表示机器人、所持物体与环境，利用机器人/物体点与环境点之间的交叉注意力生成几何感知的控制特征，并以此为"包裹层"嵌入盲式WBC，实现跟踪与避碰的权衡，支持浮动基座与末端执行器指令，并覆盖对所持物体的避碰。

Result: 在仿真中，相较于其他几何感知的WBC架构，RECAL在冻结臂与自适应臂行走、物体搬运和站立操作等场景中提升了避碰能力，同时保持目标跟踪性能；并在真实Digit V3人形机器人上完成控制器验证。

Conclusion: RECAL可作为通用外层模块增强既有盲式WBC的几何感知能力，在多种任务中兼顾跟踪与安全避碰，并具备从仿真到真实人形机器人的可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots often execute motion commands through whole-body controllers (WBCs) that track targets while maintaining balance and stability. However, most WBCs are blind to scene geometry, which can lead to collisions from imperfect target motions that are geometrically unsafe due to perception, planning, or teleoperation errors. We propose RECAL, a Robot--Environment Cross-Attention Layer that wraps a blind WBC to trade off target tracking against collision avoidance using external scene geometry. RECAL supports collision-aware tracking of floating-base and end-effector commands, including collision avoidance for held objects. It represents the robot, held objects, and environment as point clouds, using cross-attention between robot/object points and the environment to produce geometry-aware control features. In simulation, RECAL improves collision avoidance while preserving target-tracking performance across frozen-arm and adaptive-arm locomotion, object-carrying, and standing-manipulation scenarios relative to alternative geometry-aware WBC architectures. We further demonstrate the controller on a real Digit V3 humanoid robot.

</details>


### [136] [XRoboToolKit-T: Teleoperation with High Stability and Precision with Tactile Sensing for Contact-rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16437)
*Xiwen Dengxiong,Xueting Wang,Ke Jing,Rui Li,Yunbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出带触觉驱动辅助的遥操作系统XRoboToolKit-T，实现高频稳定的富接触操作，提升数据采集效率与操作稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富接触操作任务中，机器人需要高质量真实数据来获得实际技能，但现有数据采集方案难以获取稳定且高频的触觉反馈，制约了其在富接触场景中的效果。

Method: 构建融合触觉感知力控架构的遥操作系统，包含两个触觉模块：stabilizer触觉模块快速分析法向力分布并推断伪剪切力，实现实时触觉辅助；refiner触觉模块结合视觉-语言-动作模型，基于触觉传感数据和任务描述预测并优化操作动作。

Result: 在抓取可变形橡胶移液管完成液体转移、将医疗注射器插入血管训练垫等富接触操作任务上验证了触觉力控的有效性；相比无触觉辅助的先进遥操作系统，实现更高的数据采集效率和更好的操作稳定性。

Conclusion: 触觉驱动的辅助遥操作系统可显著提升富接触操作的稳定性与数据采集效率，验证了高频稳定触觉反馈在机器人技能获取中的关键价值。

Abstract: Collecting high-quality robot data for contact-rich manipulation tasks is essential for enabling robots to acquire real-world skills. However, existing data collection solutions often lack the capability to obtain stable and high-frequency tactile feedback, limiting their effectiveness in contact-rich manipulation scenarios. In this work, we propose a versatile teleoperation system with tactile-driven assistance to enable high-frequency and stable contact-rich manipulation. The proposed XRoboToolKit-T teleoperation system incorporates a tactile-informed force control architecture, designed to ensure both stable and precise force control in contact-rich manipulation during teleoperation. The stabilizer haptic module rapidly analyzes the normal force distribution and infers pseudo shear force, enabling real-time tactile-based assistance during manipulation. The refiner haptic module integrates a vision-language-action model to predict and refine manipulation actions based on tactile sensing data and task descriptions. We apply the proposed teleoperation system to challenging contact-rich manipulation tasks, including grasping a deformable rubber pipette for liquid transfer and inserting a medical syringe into a vascular training pad, to demonstrate the effectiveness of tactile-informed force control. Furthermore, the system achieves higher data collection efficiency and improved manipulation stability compared to state-of-the-art teleoperation without tactile assistance.

</details>


### [137] [The Neverwhere Visual Parkour Benchmark Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16443)
*Ziyu Chen,Henghui Bao,Haoran Chang,Alan Yu,Ran Choi,Kai McClennen,Gio Huh,Kevin Yang,Ri-Zhao Qiu,Yajvan Ravan,John J. Leonard,Xiaolong Wang,Phillip Isola,Ge Yang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Neverwhere 基准套件，包含60多个基于3D高斯泼溅重建的城市室内外场景，用于闭环评估视觉运动控制策略，并揭示仅用高斯生成数据训练的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动控制器的真实世界性能评估在部署前变得日益困难，训练与评估之间存在明显差距，且缺乏大规模、可复现的评估环境。

Method: 构建 Neverwhere 基准套件，收集60多个基于3D高斯泼溅重建的城市室内外场景，形成超写实闭环评估环境，并简化将高斯泼溅重建集成到连续仿真测试流程的过程；同时在多个 Neverwhere 场景上训练策略检查点并在新场景中评估。

Result: 提供了多个策略检查点及其在新场景中的评估表现，分析显示仅依赖3D高斯生成数据进行训练会导致泛化性能不足。

Conclusion: 强调需要来源多样化的数据以保证策略性能，并推动大规模、可复现的机器人评估；代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: State-of-the-art visual locomotion controllers are increasingly capable at handling complex visual environments, making evaluating their real-world performance before deployment increasingly difficult. This work intends to narrow this train/evaluation gap by developing a collection of hyper-photo-realistic, closed-loop evaluation environments - The Neverwhere Benchmark Suite - comprised of over sixty 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions of urban indoor and outdoor scenes. Our goal is to encourage large-scale and reproducible robot evaluation by making it easier to create and integrate Gaussian splats-based reconstructions into simulated continuous testing setups. We also underscore the potential pitfalls of relying exclusively on 3D Gaussian-generated data for training, by providing policy checkpoints trained over multiple Neverwhere scenes and their performance when evaluated in novel scenes. Our analysis illustrates the necessity of sourcing diverse data to ensure performance. Code and data are available on the project page: https://ziyc.github.io/neverwhere-bench/.

</details>


### [138] [Dense to MoE Adaptation for Compact Vision Language Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16503)
*Muchun Niu,Shuang Chen,Yuzhou Wu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaDE 将 VLA 策略中的稠密 FFN 块转换为 MoE 层，并依据路由统计动态生成专家保留掩码，在停用 40% LLM 参数的情况下保持 LIBERO 95.1%、RobotWin2.0 42.0% 的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略参数量持续增长，难以部署在资源受限的机器人平台；核心目标是减少部署策略中保留的 LLM 侧参数量，同时不显著损害下游任务性能。

Method: 将选定的稠密前馈块适配为 MoE 层；Dense2MoE 转换在初始化时保持原稠密 FFN 功能，因而无需单独的恢复阶段即可停用专家；专家掩码不采用固定关闭规则，而是根据微调期间的路由器使用统计动态更新，并配合分阶段训练与专家保护机制以避免早期崩塌。

Result: 在停用 40% LLM 参数时，AdaDE 在 LIBERO 上保持 95.1% 平均成功率，在 RobotWin2.0 全部 50 项任务上达到 42.0% 平均成功率。

Conclusion: 稠密到 MoE 的适配结合动态专家停用，是缩小激活 VLA 模型规模且不造成严重性能损失的一条可行路径。

Abstract: Vision language action (VLA) policies continue to grow in parameter count, making deployment on resource-constrained robot platforms difficult. The central goal is to reduce the number of LLM-side parameters retained in the deployed policy while preserving downstream task performance. Our approach, AdaDE, adapts selected dense feed forward blocks into mixture of experts (MoE) layers and derives expert retention masks from router statistics during fine tuning. The Dense2MoE conversion preserves the original dense FFN function at initialization, so expert deactivation can start without a separate recovery stage. Instead of using a fixed shutdown rule, expert masks are updated dynamically from router usage statistics, with staged training and expert protection to avoid early collapse. With 40% of the LLM parameters deactivated, AdaDE retains 95.1% average success in LIBERO and 42.0% average success across all 50 RobotWin2.0 tasks. These results suggest that dense to MoE adaptation with dynamic expert deactivation is a practical direction for reducing active VLA model size without severe performance loss.

</details>


### [139] [UniDex-ViTac: Learning Unified Visuo-Tactile Dexterous Manipulation Policy from Human Video Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16504)
*Hyesung Lee,Si-Hwan Heo,Sungwook Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniDex-ViTac 通过人类视频引导的仿真生成配对指尖触觉观测的机器人示范，训练出可部署的视触觉通用策略，在仿真与真实场景中均显著优于纯点云基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频富含灵巧操作示范，但缺少机器人可执行的动作与触觉测量，难以直接用于机器人策略学习；因此需要一种将人类视频示范转化为带触觉标注的机器人示范数据的框架。

Method: 提出 UniDex-ViTac：利用物体特定的残差强化学习专家，将标注的人-物交互参考适配到机械臂-机械手系统；成功 rollout 将最终机器人动作目标与机器人侧指尖接触观测配对；基于 10 个物体、50 条人类示范收集 10,000 条仿真轨迹，训练单个基于 ACT 的通用策略。策略输入融合点云、本体感知与四路二值接触信号（通过指尖标签与独立 token 编码），部署时无需人类参考，也不需要物体身份与位姿等特权信息。

Result: 仿真中触觉增强配置取得 68.3% 宏平均成功率，纯点云基线为 55.5%。在无真实机器人示范、无策略微调的条件下，6 个已见与 5 个未见物体共 110 次实物试验中成功 73 次（66.4%），基线为 60/110（54.5%），提升 11.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，从视频引导的仿真交互中学习统一的视触觉灵巧操作策略是可行的。

Abstract: Human videos provide demonstrations of dexterous manipulation but lack robot-executable actions and tactile measurements. We present UniDex-ViTac, a framework that uses human-video-guided simulation to generate robot demonstrations paired with fingertip contact observations for training a deployable visuo-tactile policy. Object-specific residual reinforcement learning specialists adapt annotated human-object interaction references to a robotic arm-hand system. Their successful rollouts pair final robot action targets with robot-side fingertip contact observations. From 50 human demonstrations across ten objects, we collect 10,000 simulated trajectories to train a single Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) based generalist. The policy combines point clouds, proprioception, and four binary contact signals encoded through fingertip labels and a separate token, without requiring human references or privileged object identity and pose at deployment. The contact-augmented configuration achieves 68.3% macro-average success in simulation, compared with 55.5% for the point-cloud-only baseline. Without real-robot demonstrations or policy fine-tuning, it succeeds in 73/110 physical trials (66.4%) across six seen and five unseen objects, compared with 60/110 (54.5%) for the baseline, an increase of 11.8 percentage points. These results support the feasibility of learning a unified visuo-tactile dexterous manipulation policy from video-guided simulated interactions. Project page: https://unidex-vitac.github.io/

</details>


### [140] [ProxiDex: Learning Dynamics-Guided Proximity Policy for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16586)
*Yushan Bai,Boyu Zheng,Zhiyang Mao,Hongzheng Sun,Yuchuang Tong,En Li,Zhengtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProxiDex提出以手-物接近度作为交互状态的动力学引导策略框架，构建硬件无关的接触表征，并学习动作条件接近度动力学以稳定灵巧操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧操作依赖稳定的手-物交互，但该交互在现实中只能被部分观测：手部遮挡视觉、触觉传感器带来硬件专有模态与标定负担，且现有策略很少建模这些线索在动作下的演化，导致在接触不确定下表现脆弱。

Method: 将手-物接近度视为交互状态：重建交互点云并把几何距离转换为接近度线索，形成硬件无关的接触表征，为VR遥操作提供沉浸式反馈；在此基础上以耦合的前向-逆向设计学习动作条件接近度动力学——由动作预测未来观测隐变量，并由隐变量变化解码接近度变化；利用该动力学在不同操作阶段自适应重加权接近度token，并以动力学一致性监督引导策略推理。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，在标准、未见物体及扰动场景下，成功率与鲁棒性相较代表性基线均有提升。

Conclusion: 以接近度为交互状态并结合动作条件动力学建模，可在视觉反馈不可靠时提供有效约束，稳定灵巧操作的动作生成，且该表征硬件无关、可跨场景迁移。

Abstract: Multi-finger dexterous manipulation relies on stable hand-object interactions, yet these interactions are partially observable in practice. Visual observations are often occluded by the hand, tactile sensors introduce hardware-specific modalities and calibration burdens, and existing policies rarely model how these cues evolve under actions, making them brittle under contact uncertainty. To address these, we present ProxiDex, a dynamics-guided proximity policy framework that treats hand-object proximity as an interaction state for dexterous manipulation. ProxiDex reconstructs interaction point clouds and converts geometric distances into proximity cues, forming a hardware-agnostic contact representation that provides immersive feedback during VR teleoperation. Built on this representation, ProxiDex learns action-conditioned proximity dynamics with a coupled forward-inverse design: future observation latents are predicted from actions, while proximity variations are decoded from latent changes. Leveraging these dynamics, ProxiDex adaptively reweights proximity tokens across manipulation phases and uses dynamics-consistency supervision to guide policy inference, stabilizing action generation under unreliable visual feedback. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate improved success rates and robustness over representative baselines across standard, unseen objects, and perturbation scenarios. Additional visualizations are available at https://proxidex.github.io/.

</details>


### [141] [Learning to Optimize UAV Path Planning for Data Sensing in Wireless Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16629)
*Sijie Ma,Zeyuan Ma,Weijia Cao,Yue-Jiao Gong,Lingling Ma,Zhiyang Huang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Landscape-Aware Meta Differential Evolution（LAMDE），一种学习辅助的无人机路径规划框架，用于无线传感器网络数据采集，通过双层元学习与景观感知数据增强克服现有方法的设计偏差与环境理想化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机是 WSN 数据感知的灵活平台，路径规划直接影响远程感知效果与能耗。现有方法存在两大局限：一是多为手工设计、带有设计偏差，难以适应未见任务；二是多依赖简化仿真，假设理想化空间复杂度，导致实际部署时性能不佳。

Method: 1) 重新形式化无人机路径规划问题以纳入高难度约束；2) 提出双层学习优化框架，元层为可训练的算法配置策略，为低层规划算法元学习可适应的规划策略；3) 引入景观感知自动增强方案，缓解真实环境中的训练数据稀缺与分布偏移；4) 低层采用差分进化算法求解；5) 设计变长编码策略，在统一搜索空间中动态剪枝冗余悬停点并同时优化连续飞行参数。

Result: 通过元训练 LAMDE 并与代表性基线对比，综合实验表明 LAMDE 在测试的 WSN 数据采集场景下的复杂无人机路径规划任务中达到 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: LAMDE 通过将元学习、差分进化与景观感知数据增强结合，有效提升了复杂约束下无人机路径规划的适应性与真实场景表现。

Abstract: UAVs have emerged as highly flexible platforms for data sensing in Wireless Sensor Networks (WSNs). Path planning for UAVs in such tasks plays a key role to assure remote sensing effectiveness and friendly energy consumption. However, existing approaches show two key limitations: i) they are primarily hand-crafted with certain design biases that harm adaptation on unseen tasks. ii) they predominantly assume idealized spatial complexities of actual environments through simplified simulation, causing them to underperform during real-world deployment. In this paper, we propose a novel learning-assisted planning framework, termed Landscape-Aware Meta Differential Evolution (LAMDE), to tackle the mentioned limitations. The major contributions come from the following aspects. We first re-formulate such UAV path planning problem to embrace challenging constraints. To efficiently navigate this highly constrained space, we propose a bi-level learning to optimize approach, where the meta-level is a trainable algorithm configuration policy that meta-learns an adaptable planning strategy for low-level planning algorithm. To address the potential training data scarcity and distribution shift in real-world environments, we introduce a landscape-aware automatic augmentation scheme that enriches training data. At the low-level, a Differential Evolution algorithm is deployed for solving the path planning tasks. To enhance the solving flexibility, we further design a variable-length encoding strategy that dynamically prunes redundant hover points and optimizes continuous flight parameters concurrently within a unified search space. Based on all proposed designs, we meta-train LAMDE and compare it with representative baselines. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that LAMDE achieves state-of-the-art performance on the tested complex UAV path planning tasks in WSN data collection scenarios.

</details>


### [142] [SAVLA: Symmetry-Aware Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16641)
*Junle Li,Weixian Waylon Li,Fuxiang Wu,Fusheng Hao,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAVLA：一种对称性感知的端到端VLA模型，在冻结预训练视觉-语言主干的基础上，结合等变流匹配动作头与学习式规范化器，从而在LIBERO上提升成功率与旋转鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽输入图像与语言指令本身蕴含几何信息，但其空间能力完全依赖演示数据习得，导致仅在演示覆盖的场景位姿范围内可靠。为此需要提升模型的空间泛化性与数据效率。

Method: 保持预训练视觉-语言主干完全冻结；引入等变流匹配动作头（equvariant flow-matching action head），将状态、动作与条件输入分解为不变通道和等变通道，并在所有层中保持该类型划分；同时使用一个学习式规范化器（canonicalizer），将斜视角图像变换到规范坐标系，并一致地旋转几何条件。

Result: 在LIBERO上评估：相比GR00T N1.5基线，四个LIBERO套件的平均成功率提升5.1个百分点；在LIBERO-Goal上，旋转条件下的平均成功率从41.5%提升至90.4%。

Conclusion: SAVLA通过对称性感知设计，在不微调预训练主干的前提下显著增强了VLA策略的空间鲁棒性和数据效率，尤其在旋转场景下表现突出。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have become the dominant paradigm for language-conditioned robot manipulation. However, although images and language instructions inherently encode geometric information, VLAs acquire their spatial competence purely from demonstrations. As a result, they are reliable only within the range of scene poses that the demonstrations cover. We propose SAVLA, an end-to-end symmetry-aware VLA model for robust and data-efficient policy learning. Our approach keeps the pretrained vision-language backbone entirely frozen while combining it with an equivariant flow-matching action head and a learned canonicalizer. The head decomposes its state, action, and conditioning inputs into invariant and equivariant channels, and preserves this typing throughout all of its layers. The canonicalizer transforms oblique-view images into a canonical frame and rotates the geometric conditions consistently. We evaluate our model on LIBERO. Compared with the GR00T N1.5 baseline, SAVLA improves the success rate averaged over all four LIBERO suites by 5.1 points and increases the mean success rate under rotation on LIBERO-Goal from 41.5% to 90.4%.

</details>


### [143] [WholeBodyWAM: Generalizing Pre-trained World-Action Priors to Humanoid Loco-Manipulation via WBC-Grounded Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16644)
*Zhuo Li,Yiming Yao,Jim Tan,Mengjie Jing,Zhipeng Dong,Fei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WholeBodyWAM，联合预测未来视觉动态、操作动作与全身控制意图，实现可泛化的人形loco-manipulation，无需从零学习全身行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World Action Models（WAMs）多聚焦桌面或机械臂操作，人形全身移动操作（loco-manipulation）研究不足，缺乏可扩展的全身智能方案。

Method: 在预训练world-action先验基础上，引入结构化全身控制器（WBC）语义grounding与全身行为协调，联合建模视觉动态、操作动作和全身控制意图。

Result: 仿真任务总体成功率达91.9%；真实世界OOD任务进度提升0.23；相比基线，跨WBC成功率方差降低70%。

Conclusion: 通过结构化WBC grounding与协调扩展预训练world-action先验，而非从头重学全身行为，为可扩展的人形全身智能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) offer a promising approach to general-purpose robot manipulation by jointly modeling visual dynamics and actions. However, most WAM studies focus on tabletop or arm-centric manipulation, while humanoid loco-manipulation remains less explored. To address this gap, we introduce WholeBodyWAM, which jointly predicts future visual dynamics, manipulation actions, and whole-body control intents for generalizable humanoid loco-manipulation. It preserves pre-trained world-action priors while grounding heterogeneous whole-body controller (WBC) semantics and coordinating whole-body behavior. Extensive experiments show that WholeBodyWAM achieves an overall simulation task success rate of 91.9%, with a 0.23 improvement in real-world out-of-distribution task progress and a 70% reduction in success-rate variance across WBCs relative to the respective baselines. These results suggest a path toward scalable humanoid whole-body intelligence by extending pre-trained world-action priors through structured WBC grounding and coordination, rather than relearning whole-body behavior from scratch. Project page: https://wholebodywam.github.io/.

</details>


### [144] [Weave: Learning Whole-Body Dexterous Loco-Manipulation from Human-Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16683)
*Liu Cao,Xingze Wu,Jingzhi Cui,Botian Xu,Mingzhi Pei,Ruoqu Chen,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Weave 框架，从人类演示中学习人形机器人全身灵巧的物体交互技能，含接触感知重定向与接触/几何感知策略，实现92.5%训练交互成功率与65.0%零样本泛化成功率，并开源约9000条实机轨迹数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人物体交互需协调全身平衡、移动与灵巧手接触，同时控制机器人与物体运动。人类演示提供协调交互范例，但由于不同本体结构与动力学差异，如何建立并维持有效接触、将人类行为迁移到人形机器人仍是挑战。

Method: Weave 为统一框架：首先通过接触感知重定向（contact-aware retargeting）与接近运动补全（approach-motion completion），将采集的人-物交互转换为可执行的机器人-物体参考轨迹；核心是一个接触与几何感知策略，联合控制29个身体关节与12个驱动手指关节，覆盖多物体、多交互序列。

Result: 在9个物体上评估：已训练交互成功率92.5%；在无需额外训练的情况下，未见过的交互序列成功率达65.0%。此外发布约9,000条物理执行 rollout（约23小时），含带接触标注的机器人-物体轨迹。

Conclusion: Weave 验证了从人类演示学习全身灵巧人形物体交互的可行性，并具备一定零样本泛化能力。所发布的大规模实机轨迹与接触标注可用于下游交互策略学习及物理一致的 HOI 动作生成。

Abstract: Learning humanoid-object interaction requires coordinating whole-body balance, locomotion, and dexterous hand contact to control both robot and object motion. Human demonstrations provide examples of coordinated interaction, but transferring these behaviors to humanoid robots requires learning how to establish and maintain effective contacts under different embodiments and dynamics. We present Weave, a unified framework for learning whole-body dexterous humanoid-object interaction from captured human demonstrations. Weave first converts captured human-object interactions into executable robot-object references through contact-aware retargeting and approach-motion completion. At its core is a contact- and geometry-aware policy that jointly commands 29 body joints and 12 actuated finger joints across multiple objects and interaction sequences. Evaluation across nine objects yields a 92.5% success rate on trained interactions and, without any additional training, 65.0% on sequences never seen during training. We additionally release ~9,000 physically executed rollouts spanning ~23 hours, providing robot-object trajectories with contact annotations for downstream interaction-policy learning and physically consistent HOI motion generation. Project website: https://xiaohu-art.github.io/Weave/

</details>


### [145] [Differentiable Mesh State Estimation via Factor Graph Inference for Deformable Object Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16686)
*Lidia Al-Zogbi,Fangjie Li,Samuel Tobin,James Ferguson,Nithesh Kumar,Alejandro Chara,Kuan-I Chung,Mingxing Rao,Ayberk Acar,Susheela Sharma Stern,Robert Webster,Daniel Moyer,Alan Kuntz,Caleb Rucker,Tucker Hermans,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于因子图的概率网格状态估计框架，直接更新四面体网格，统一融合物理先验、噪声传感器测量与时间平滑约束，用非线性最小二乘（Levenberg-Marquardt）求解，在气道阻塞实验与形变立方体仿真中实现可靠重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可形变物体的状态估计在机器人与仿真领域仍是基础性难题，需要一种有原则、由测量驱动且物理合理的网格状态估计方法。

Method: 构建因子图框架进行概率网格状态估计，直接对四面体网格这一物理化环境表示进行更新；在统一概率公式中融合物理先验、含噪传感器测量与时间平滑约束；将估计问题建模为非线性最小二乘优化，并采用Levenberg-Marquardt算法求解。

Result: 离体中央气道阻塞实验与形变立方体模型仿真表明，在刚体运动与形变两种情形下均能实现可靠且准确的重建。

Conclusion: 该概率方法在可形变物体重建中展现出进行有原则、测量驱动的网格状态估计的潜力。

Abstract: Estimating deformable object states remains a fundamental challenge in robotics and simulation. We propose a novel factor graph-based framework for probabilistic mesh state estimation of deformable objects. The method directly updates a tetrahedral mesh, a rich and physically-grounded representation of an environment, by combining physics priors, noisy sensor measurements, and temporal smoothness constraints within a unified probabilistic formulation. The estimation problem is posed as a nonlinear least-squares optimization and solved using Levenberg-Marquardt. Ex vivo central-airway obstruction experiments and simulations on deforming cube models demonstrate reliable and accurate reconstruction under both rigid motion and deformation, highlighting the potential of this probabilistic approach for principled, measurement-driven mesh state estimation in deformable object reconstruction.

</details>


### [146] [IL-ACT: Imitation Learning with Adaptive Cartesian Tracking Control for a 30-ton Excavator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16696)
*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Seihun Kim,Soyi Jung,Soohyun Park,Jouni Mattila,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IL-ACT运动控制框架，结合模仿学习与自适应笛卡尔跟踪，在30吨级挖掘机上实现更短任务时长与更低跟踪误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主挖掘机控制面临耦合运动学、执行器滞后与不确定性等挑战，需要一种能兼顾示范先验与实时反馈校正的运动控制方法。

Method: IL-ACT：以锚定的14输入模仿策略（基于操作员示范预训练）生成标称关节速率，再经自适应笛卡尔反馈与门控增益/偏置估计校正指令，最后由停止距离调节器约束关节参考生成。在Simscape中进行100个连续目标及螺旋、8字形、圆角栅格轨迹评估，并补充88次运行，覆盖3个训练种子、2种初始化与多速度、多液压响应及传感条件。

Result: 相比Teacher+ACT，IL-ACT在两种响应条件下均完成全部目标，时长更短、终端误差更低。遥测初始化的IL-ACT在全部24组8字形与圆角栅格种子对比中降低RMSE，附加负载螺旋平均RMSE约降低29%；原始螺旋RMSE亦优于IL-only与PID。共享传感噪声实现下，遥测初始化IL-ACT平均RMSE比Teacher+ACT低27.67%；启用估计较冻结估计器降低平均RMSE 22.44%。预训练权重效应不一致，原始教师对比存在螺旋RMSE—最大误差权衡。

Conclusion: 分析给出自适应状态与笛卡尔反馈的有界性，并指出参考可行性依赖于调节器的可行性条件。

Abstract: Autonomous excavator control is challenged by coupled kinematics, actuation lag, and uncertainty. We propose imitation learning and adaptive Cartesian tracking (IL-ACT), a novel motion control framework for a 30-ton-class excavator. An anchored, 14-input imitation policy pretrained on operator demonstrations generates nominal joint rates; adaptive Cartesian feedback and gated gain/bias estimation correct these commands before a stopping-distance governor constrains joint-reference generation. Simscape evaluation covers 100 sequential goals and spiral, figure-eight, and rounded-raster tracking, including 88 additional runs across three training seeds, two initializations, and speeds, under hydraulic response and sensing conditions. Compared with Teacher+ACT, IL-ACT completes all goals with shorter duration and lower terminal errors under both response conditions. Telemetry-initialized IL-ACT lowers RMSE in all 24 figure-eight and rounded-raster seed comparisons and lowers additional-load spiral mean RMSE by approximately 29%. Original spiral RMSE also improves over IL-only and PID. Under a shared sensor-noise realization, telemetry-initialized IL-ACT achieves 27.67% lower mean RMSE than Teacher+ACT; enabling estimation reduces mean RMSE by $22.44\%$ relative to the frozen estimator. Pretrained-weight effects remain mixed, and the original teacher comparison exhibits a spiral RMSE--maximum-error tradeoff. Analysis establishes bounded adaptive states and Cartesian feedback, with reference admissibility conditional on governor feasibility.

</details>


### [147] [World Models for Embodied Intelligence: From Plausible to Controllable to Actionable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16697)
*Nanjie Yao,Hao Wang,Chong Cheng,Zhikang Chen,Wenzhe Li,Jiafei Lyu,Li Shen,Peilin Zhao,Zongqing Lu,Gao Huang,Steven Hoi,Dacheng Tao,Deheng Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以"能力"而非"架构"为核心的层次化框架（Plausible→Controllable→Actionable）及3×4矩阵，用以评估世界模型是否真正改善具身智能体的行为，并据此综述操作、导航、运动、自动驾驶与通用具身学习领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的价值应体现在改进行为而非视觉保真度；但现有综述按架构、输出模态或应用领域组织，隐含了"哪些预测能力能改善行为"这一问题而未予回答。因此需要一个以能力为导向的评估视角。

Method: 提出三个递进能力层级：Plausible（保持任务相关的时间/几何/物理结构）、Controllable（额外预测干预如何改变该结构）、Actionable（将预测转化为规划、行动、学习、评估、验证、恢复或数据选择上的可测收益）；再构建3×4矩阵，将几何、物理、动作接地三轴与以数据、奖励、策略、模型本身为中心的改进闭环交叉；以此框架综述多个具身领域并考察数据集、基准与评价协议。

Result: 系统梳理了操作、导航、运动、自动驾驶与通用具身学习的技术演进脉络，明确了各层级的能力需求，并识别出长时程一致性、不确定性校准、因果干预测试、延迟、验证与恢复、跨具身迁移等关键挑战。

Conclusion: 评价世界模型应从视觉合理性转向预测是否捕捉任务相关状态、是否反映干预效应、是否提升具身智能体的闭环行为。

Abstract: World models connect perception and decision-making in embodied intelligence by maintaining hidden state, anticipating consequences, comparing interventions, and adapting when execution departs from expectations. Although progress is often measured by visual fidelity, their value lies in improving behavior. Before reaching for a cup, a person anticipates its weight and resistance to grasping, shaping the hand before contact. Such anticipation is coarse and rarely pictorial, yet it guides action. This raises a central question: which predictive capabilities improve behavior? Existing surveys, organized by architecture, output modality, or application domain, leave this question implicit. We introduce three progressively stronger capability levels: Plausible models preserve task-relevant temporal, geometric, or physical structure; Controllable models additionally predict how interventions alter that structure; and Actionable models translate predictions into measurable gains in planning, action, learning, evaluation, verification, recovery, or data selection. We complement this hierarchy with a 3 x 4 matrix crossing geometry, physics, and action grounding with improvement loops centered on data, rewards, policies, and the model itself. Using this framework, we survey manipulation, navigation, locomotion, autonomous driving, and general embodied learning, tracing technical progressions, clarifying capability requirements, and examining datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation protocols. We identify challenges in long-horizon consistency, uncertainty calibration, causal intervention testing, latency, verification and recovery, and cross-embodiment transfer. This perspective shifts evaluation from visual plausibility toward whether predictions capture task-relevant state, reflect intervention effects, and improve the closed-loop behavior of embodied agents.

</details>


### [148] [The Robot Data Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16705)
*Sami Haddadin,Ivan Laptev,Ian Reid,Dezhen Song,Cesare Stefanini,Abdalla Swikir,Xingxing Zuo,Lyes Saad Saoud,Mahmoud Hamandi,Mohamed Heshmat,Oualid Doukhi,Abdeldjallil Naceri,Attique Bashar,Abdelrahim Mohamed,Teodor Tomic,Yue Peng,Samuel Schneider,Cheng-Chung Lee,Janine Guo,Qinghao Zhang,Kim Jeffery*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"机器人数据工厂"(RDF)，将物理AI的核心资源从静态机器人数据集重新定义为"机器人经验"(感知-行动-结果闭环)，构建了覆盖生成、验证、基准测试与复用的闭环基础设施，并给出形式化定义、层级体系、缩放律与面向具身AI集群的算法综合流程，在家庭、环境、能源三类训练场实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI的发展不能仅靠不断扩大的机器人数据集规模；智能机器人需通过与物理世界持续交互来获取知识。因此论文主张物理AI的决定性科学资源不是原始机器人数据，而是具有物理根基的"机器人经验"——其观测、行动、具身形态、上下文与结果共同保留了"感知-行动-后果"闭环。现有数据范式把数据集当作静态终端产物，缺乏持续生成、验证与复用的科学化生产机制。

Method: 提出RDF（Robot Data Factory）任务驱动型基础设施与方法论：通过可复现任务（reproducible missions）、技能课程（skill curricula）、同步多模态感知、外部真值（external ground truth）、智能体机器人网络、数据流水线与"活基准"（living benchmarks）组织异构机器人与环境专用训练场；以闭环Deploy-Measure-Learn-Repeat循环替代静态数据集范式；形式化定义机器人经验及其质量；建立"任务-任务项-技能-回合-数据集-基准-能力"层级体系；推导连接机器人集群规模、传感器速率、存储、学习表征、分词、训练算力、推理与延迟到具身AI集群需求的定量缩放律与算法综合流程；并在家庭、环境、能源三个互补物理训练场完成实例化。

Result: RDF框架已在三类互补物理训练场（家庭、环境、能源应用）实现实例化；给出了机器人经验的形式化定义与质量度量、多层级的科学资源组织体系，以及量化缩放律和算法综合流程，将集群规模、传感速率、存储与算力、推理延迟等要素与具身AI集群需求建立定量关联。

Conclusion: 论文将机器人数据生成重新表述为一个持续的科学生产过程，而非一次性数据集构建；RDF为物理AI提供了可复现、可扩展、并最终趋向联邦化（federated）的基础设施路径，支撑世界模型、视觉-语言-行动模型、具身策略与数字孪生等下游能力。

Abstract: Physical AI requires more than increasingly large robot datasets: intelligent robots acquire knowledge through continuous interaction with the physical world. We argue that the defining scientific resource of Physical AI is therefore not raw robot data alone, but robot experience - physically grounded interaction whose observations, actions, embodiment, context, and outcomes preserve the perception-action-consequence loop. We introduce the Robot Data Factory (RDF), a mission-driven infrastructure and methodology for continuously generating, validating, benchmarking, and reusing such experience. RDF organizes heterogeneous robots and environment-specific training grounds through reproducible missions, skill curricula, synchronized multimodal sensing, external ground truth, an agentic robot network, data pipelines, and living benchmarks. Rather than treating datasets as static end products, RDF implements a closed Deploy-Measure-Learn-Repeat cycle in which validated physical experience supports world models, vision-language-action models, embodied policies, digital twins, and subsequent robot deployment. We further formalize robot experience and its quality, introduce a mission-task-skill-episode-dataset-benchmark-capability hierarchy, and derive quantitative scaling laws and an algorithmic synthesis procedure connecting robot fleet size, sensor rates, storage, learning representations, tokenization, training compute, inference, and latency to Embodied-AI cluster requirements. The framework is instantiated in three complementary physical training grounds for domestic, environmental, and energy applications. RDF thus reframes robot data generation as a continuous scientific production process and provides a pathway toward reproducible, scalable, and eventually federated infrastructure for Physical AI.

</details>


### [149] [CorrRisk-WM: Corridor-Conditioned Risk World Modeling for Safety-Critical Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16724)
*Tingyu Guo,Reza Langari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CorrRisk-WM 局部世界模型，将环境演化与候选轨迹走廊内的侵入/近失事件预测耦合，实现规划导向的风险预测与安全候选选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全局部规划需要预测周围智能体运动并评估"候选轨迹特定"的风险，因为相同智能体运动对不同自车轨迹构成的风险不同；现有方法多缺少候选条件化的几何推理与事件级风险监督。

Method: 规划导向的部分世界模型：潜空间环境模型递归预测智能体状态并更新智能体-智能体、智能体-地图交互；每个候选轨迹通过足迹感知几何与学习的智能体-走廊表示查询演化环境；轻量循环风险模块用时间上下文估计逐切片危险，生存聚合得到首次进入与时间跨度级事件概率。

Result: 在 100 个 Waymo 验证分片的 29,176 个场景上，侵入 AP 0.8567，1 m 近失首次进入 AP 0.8671；在三个距离阈值的近失 AP 均为最高，开环碰撞率最低（4.88%），路线进度 15.35 m。三个随机种子下，移除动态环境建模或候选条件几何交互使平均侵入 AP 由 0.8590 分别降至 0.7624 与 0.7252。

Conclusion: 结果支持将环境演化与候选条件化几何推理相耦合，用于风险预测与面向安全的候选轨迹选择。

Abstract: Safe local planning requires forecasting surrounding-agent motion and evaluating candidate-specific risks, since identical agent motion can pose different risks to different ego trajectories. We present CorrRisk-WM, a planning-oriented partial world model coupling environment evolution with supervised intrusion and near-miss prediction over bounded candidate-trajectory corridors. A latent environment model recursively predicts agent states and updates agent-agent and agent-map interactions. Each candidate queries the evolving environment through footprint- aware geometry and learned agent-corridor representations. A lightweight recurrent risk module uses temporal context to estimate per-slice hazards; survival aggregation yields first-entry and horizon-level event probabilities. On 29,176 scenarios from 100 Waymo validation shards, CorrRisk-WM achieves intrusion average precision (AP) of 0.8567 and 1-m near-miss first-entry AP of 0.8671. In baseline comparisons, it attains the highest near-miss AP at all three distance thresholds and the lowest observed open-loop collision rate (4.88%), with route progress of 15.35 m. Across three seeds, removing dynamic environment modeling or candidate-conditioned geometric interaction reduces mean intrusion AP from 0.8590 to 0.7624 and 0.7252, respectively. These results support coupling environment evolution with candidate-conditioned geometric reasoning for risk prediction and safety-oriented candidate selection.

</details>


### [150] [Seeing What Matters: Visual Cue Guided Video Planning for Generalizable Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16737)
*Hojin Lee,Sizhe Lester Li,Maximilian Hilger,Susie Lu,Achim J. Lilienthal,Vincent Sitzmann,Daniel A. Duecker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CueNav：以视觉线索引导视频规划，结合具身IDM，实现长时程导航与跨平台零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式视频导航多依赖短时程引导并通过场景重建恢复几何路点，长时程规划与精确的视频到动作转换尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出CueNav框架：以BEV地图编码全局任务上下文、在自我中心观测中保留部分机器人本体以暴露具身信息，作为视觉线索引导视频规划器；由具身特定的逆动力学模型（IDM）将从视频规划中提取的稠密光流场转换为机器人动作。

Result: 迷宫导航成功率较无线索规划提升近2倍；结合本体感知视角与IDM在狭窄通道中达70%成功率，对比方法基本失败；实现零样本语义条件导航，并可在不同机器人平台上复用同一视频规划器。

Conclusion: 视觉线索引导的视频规划与具身特定动作接地相结合，为长时程规划与具身感知控制的通用导航框架提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Generative video models can serve as a promising backbone for robot navigation by predicting future observations as video plans. Recent approaches often condition video planning on short-horizon guidance and recover geometric waypoints through scene reconstruction, leaving longer-horizon planning and precise video-to-action translation less explored. We present CueNav, a video model-based navigation framework combining visual cue guided video planning with an embodiment-specific Inverse-Dynamics Model (IDM). As visual cues, we use a Bird's-Eye View (BEV) map to convey global task context and retain part of the robot body in the egocentric observation to expose embodiment context. These cues guide the video planner, while the IDM translates dense flow fields extracted from the video plan into robot actions. With the visual cue encoding global task context, CueNav achieves nearly 2x higher success in maze navigation than planning without the cue. The body-aware view with the IDM enables precise navigation with 70% success in a narrow passage where comparison methods largely fail to complete the task. We further demonstrate zero-shot semantic-conditioned navigation and deployment of the same video planner across different robot platforms. Our results show that visual cue-guided video planning with embodiment-specific action grounding paves the way toward a generalizable navigation framework for longer-horizon planning and embodiment-aware control. Additional results and code are available on our project website: https://cuenav.github.io.

</details>


### [151] [The Latent That Never Was: A Forensic Re-run of the CVAE Ablation in Action Chunking Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16745)
*Bo Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新检验了ACT论文中"移除编码器导致成功率从35%跌至2%"的消融结论，发现该下降无法复现，并进一步分析训练时长与检查点选择的影响及编码器的实际作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACT中被广泛使用的条件变分自编码器编码器本意是捕捉演示间的差异，原论文报告的编码器移除导致成功率大幅下降的结论被视为该组件重要性的关键证据，因此有必要验证该发现是否依赖具体实现或训练数据。

Method: 在原代码上重跑消融实验，并改变训练时长与用于评估的检查点选择方式，检验结论的稳健性；同时在不同潜在信息惩罚权重下评估采样潜变量对动作重建的帮助，并测量跳过编码器对训练吞吐量的影响。

Result: 原论文报告的显著成功率下降在测试中未能复现，虽仍有不确定的成功率小幅增减；训练时长与检查点选择均可能反转哪个策略得分更高，但原下降的成因仍不明确。在测试的ACT基准上，采样潜变量在所有非零惩罚权重下对动作重建几乎没有增益，且推理时ACT将潜变量置零、实际未使用；跳过编码器可提升两种实现的训练吞吐量。

Conclusion: 原ACT编码器消融结论不可靠，其收益难以归因于潜变量提供的信息；作者发布代码、评估工具与结果以便他人复现并进一步在其他任务上检验该编码器。

Abstract: Action Chunking Transformers (ACT) are widely used to learn robot manipulation from demonstrations. Their conditional variational autoencoder includes an encoder meant to capture differences between demonstrations during training. The original ACT paper reported that encoder removal dropped the mean success rate from 35% to 2% on two simulated tasks with human demonstrations. We re-ran this ablation in the original code and checked whether the findings depend on the implementation or training data. The published drop does not reappear in our tests, although smaller gains or losses in success rate remain uncertain. To investigate the discrepancy, we varied training length and how checkpoints are selected for evaluation. Both can reverse which policy scores higher, but the published drop's cause remains unknown. Success rates alone leave open whether the encoder provides information that helps the policy reconstruct demonstrated actions. On the tested ACT benchmark, the sampled latent provides little reconstruction benefit at every tested nonzero weight of the penalty on latent information. At inference, ACT leaves this latent unused and sets it to zero. Skipping the encoder increases training throughput in both implementations we timed. We release code, evaluation tools and results so others can repeat the comparisons and test the encoder on other tasks.

</details>


### [152] [Optimal Excitation Trajectories for System Identification of Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16786)
*Fotis Panetsos,Kostas J. Kyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种通过设计最优激励轨迹进行水下航行器系统辨识的结构化方法，并用实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航行器动力学参数辨识依赖高质量数据，而数据质量取决于激励轨迹的设计，因此需要系统化的轨迹优化方法来获得可靠参数估计。

Method: 用Bezier曲线参数化轨迹以保证平滑可微的运动曲线，并通过控制点操作施加约束；构建优化问题求解满足安全限制且最大化数据质量的动态可行激励轨迹，再用最小二乘法估计动力学参数。

Result: 在实验室水槽中实验验证，从优化轨迹辨识得到的动力学参数被用于在未见轨迹上前向仿真预测航行器速度，从而评估辨识效果。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在安全约束下生成最优激励轨迹，提升数据质量，实现可靠的动力学参数辨识，并通过速度预测实验得到验证。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a structured methodology for the system identification of underwater vehicles through the design of optimal excitation trajectories. To this end, the trajectories are parameterized using Bezier curves, which ensure smooth and differentiable motion profiles while facilitating the enforcement of constraints through appropriate manipulation of the control points. An optimization problem is formulated to determine a dynamically feasible excitation trajectory that respects safety limits and maximizes the quality of the collected data, thereby enabling reliable estimation of the vehicle's dynamic parameters using least squares. The proposed methodology is experimentally validated in a laboratory water tank, where the dynamic parameters, identified from the optimized trajectory, are evaluated by predicting the vehicle's velocity through forward simulation on previously unseen trajectories.

</details>


### [153] [Motion planning in high dimensional spaces hybridizing RRT and HAR via position-direction decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16810)
*Frederic Cazals,Nelson Feyeux*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合RRT与Hit-and-Run随机游走的采样路径规划方法，将点扩展与方向扩展解耦，并引入稀疏移动策略，在高维多机器人问题上最多比经典RRT快两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维空间路径规划仍具挑战，尤其存在窄通道和小间隙时；同时多机器人、复杂分子系统等大规模问题对算法效率提出了更高要求。

Method: 基于采样的路径规划框架：将RRT与Hit-and-Run随机游走结合，把被扩展的点与扩展方向解耦；并将RRT和HAR视为泛化算法中分别对点和方向施加不同偏置的特例；进一步研究每步仅移动比例p_r机器人的稀疏移动策略。

Result: 在两类模型上测试：3D经典钢琴搬运问题，以及涉及数十个刚体域相对运动的复杂分子系统（建模为独立机器人在SE(3)^N运动空间探索）。标准笔记本上数秒内求解最多64个机器人、384个自由度的问题。

Conclusion: 推荐HARF作为复杂多机器人规划问题的首选方法：在经典RRT每步移动所有机器人成功的情况下，其速度最多快两个数量级；在两者均使用各自最优p_r时，多数实例上仍快至2.4倍。

Abstract: The exploration of high-dimensional spaces remains a challenging problem, in particular in the presence of narrow passages and small clearances. We propose novel sampling-based path-planning methods for high-dimensional spaces combining Rapidly-exploring Random Trees (RRT) and Hit-and-Run (HAR) random walks by decoupling the point being extended from the direction of extension. We also show that RRT and HAR appear as special cases of a generic algorithm coupling the biases used for the point and direction extension, respectively. We further study a sparse-move strategy in which only a fraction p_r of the robots is moved at each step, helping both RRT and the proposed HAR algorithms handle cluttered instances. Tests are presented for two families of models: classical piano mover problems in 3D, and complex molecular systems involving tens of rigid domains moving relatively to one another -- the latter viewed as independent robots exploring the motion space SE(3)N . Within seconds on a standard laptop, our algorithms solve instances with up to 64 robots and 384 degrees of freedom. We conclude by suggesting one of our methods, HARF, as the method of choice for complex multi-robot planning problems, being up to two orders of magnitude faster than the classical RRT moving all robots at each step--when it succeeds at all, and still up to 2.4 fold faster on most instances when both use their best p_r.

</details>


### [154] [Rethinking Visual Embodiment Dependence in Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16815)
*Hongjie Fang,Yuxuan Lu,Chenxi Wang,Haoxiang Qin,Shirun Tang,Zihao He,Shangning Xia,Jingjing Chen,Wanxi Liu,Shiquan Wang,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉运动策略中的"视觉具身依赖"(VED)现象，主张不应消除而应结构化具身视觉信息，提出在3D点云中进行具身规范化（CER）并配合配置去相关增强，显著提升跨具身迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略同时观察任务场景与执行具身，导致具身特定的视觉线索可能成为动作预测的捷径，即"视觉具身依赖"(VED)。作者认为简单去除具身信息并非最优，而应将其围绕支持控制与泛化的具身信息加以结构化。

Method: 通过线索冲突干预实验验证VED的存在；提出在3D点云中进行具身规范化，用规范化末端执行器表示(CER)替代原始具身，在保留控制相关几何的同时抽象掉具身特定形态；利用CER的可编辑性实现配置去相关增强，以应对陌生机器人配置。

Result: 实验表明，具身规范化在无机器人示范条件下显著改善人机策略迁移，而单纯移除具身且不保留控制相关几何则效果不足；同时发现CER在机器人配置与任务进度解耦时本身可能成为捷径，配置去相关增强可缓解该失效模式，在不牺牲已知配置性能的前提下属恢复鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的视觉运动学习得益于对视觉具身信息进行"结构化"而非"移除"，CER与配置去相关增强共同构成有效方案。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies observe both the task scene and the acting embodiment, allowing embodiment-specific visual cues to influence action prediction. We study this phenomenon as visual embodiment dependence (VED) and show, through cue-conflict interventions across representative policies, that visible robot configuration can become a shortcut to task progress. Rather than eliminating VED, we argue that it should be structured around embodiment information that supports control and generalization. We realize this through embodiment canonicalization in 3D point clouds, replacing the original embodiment with a canonical end-effector representation (CER) that preserves control-relevant geometry while abstracting embodiment-specific morphology. Its editable form further enables configuration-decorrelation augmentation for unfamiliar robot configurations. Experiments show that embodiment canonicalization substantially improves human-to-robot policy transfer without robot demonstrations, while simply removing the embodiment is insufficient without preserving control-relevant geometry. We further find that CER itself can become a configuration shortcut when robot configuration becomes decoupled from task progress; configuration-decorrelation augmentation mitigates this failure mode and restores robust recovery without sacrificing performance on seen configurations. Together, these results show that robust visuomotor learning benefits from structuring, rather than removing, visual embodiment information. Project website: https://tonyfang.net/ved

</details>


### [155] [TEMPO: Learning Temporal Context for Dynamic Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16864)
*Zhenyang Feng,Jimin Heo,Erik B. Sudderth,Unnat Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出VLA模型在动态操作中受限于"运动模糊"与"状态混叠"两类表征缺陷，提出TEMPO方法，通过注入运动摘要与本体感受历史两类时序输入加以解决，显著提升动态操作成功率并发布TEMPO-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在准静态操作中表现优异，但在动态操作任务中因推理时仅依赖单帧观测而表现不佳。作者识别出两个根本性的表征失败：一是运动模糊（单帧观测缺乏场景动态信息，无法预判运动物体的未来状态），二是状态混叠（任务不同阶段中视觉相似的观测却需要不同动作）。作者进一步论证这些缺陷与模型规模及推理延迟无关，说明瓶颈在于缺失时序上下文，而非模型容量。

Method: 提出TEMPO方法，在预训练VLA之上增强两类时序输入：(1) 从冻结的视频基础模型中提取的运动摘要，用于消解运动模糊；(2) 紧凑的本体感受历史，用于消解状态混叠。该方法无需修改主干网络，训练与部署阶段的计算开销极低。

Result: 在四类动态操作任务上验证：Bottle Handover任务成功率从44%提升至74%，且是唯一能解决状态混叠问题的方法。探针分析与消融实验证实，两类时序信号分别独立地解决了对应的表征缺陷。此外发布了TEMPO-Bench，包含超过5万帧标注数据，用于以回归与多项选择两种形式评估运动感知的机器人感知能力。

Conclusion: VLA模型在动态操作中的瓶颈是缺失时序上下文而非模型容量；通过轻量级地引入运动摘要与本体感受历史两类互补时序输入，可在不修改主干与低开销条件下有效解决运动模糊与状态混叠，显著提升动态操作性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved impressive performance in quasi-static manipulation, but struggle in dynamic manipulation tasks because they operate on a single observation at inference time. We identify two representational failures that underlie this limitation. The first is motion ambiguity, where a single observation does not include scene dynamics and therefore cannot anticipate the future state of moving objects. The second is state aliasing, where visually similar observations from different points in a task require different actions. We argue that these failures persist regardless of model scale and inference latency, showing that the bottleneck is missing temporal context rather than model capacity. Based on this insight, we propose TEMPO, which augments a pretrained VLA with two temporal inputs: a motion summary extracted from a frozen video foundation model to resolve motion ambiguity and a compact proprioceptive history to resolve state aliasing. TEMPO requires no modification to the backbone and adds minimal compute overhead at training or deployment. Across four dynamic manipulation tasks, it improves Bottle Handover success from 44% to 74% and is the only method that solves state aliasing. Probing and ablation studies confirm that each temporal signal independently addresses its corresponding failure. We further release TEMPO-Bench, a benchmark of over 50k annotated frames for evaluating motion-aware robot perception in both regression and multiple-choice formats.
  Project Website: https://tempo-robot.github.io/

</details>


### [156] [Artificial Intelligence-Enabled Space Robot Operations: Technologies, Challenges and Prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16880)
*Zeyuan Huang,Gang Chen,Zixuan Hao,Guoqin Tang,Junyi Zong,Guoyou Ban,Jiale Wang,Haoyang Lv,Chaoqian Ren,Sitong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 从"能力构建"视角综述AI赋能的空间机器人操作（AI-SRO），提出"能力基础—能力形成—能力部署/演化"三层框架，并给出未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间机器人需在有限人工干预下完成长时、接触密集、多阶段任务；AI、机器人学习与具身基础模型虽带来机遇，但其向空间迁移受任务数据稀缺、空间特有动力学与感知条件、星载资源受限及严格安全要求等因素制约，亟需系统性梳理。

Method: 综述性研究：先归纳典型操作场景、自主化趋势与空间特有约束；再构建三层技术框架——能力基础、能力形成、能力部署/演化，并在框架内组织与评述文献。

Result: 在能力基础层梳理仿真环境、数据集与基准；在能力形成层梳理任务与环境理解、状态感知、决策与规划、动作执行；在能力部署/演化层梳理星载部署、地面到空间适配、持续学习与能力迁移。

Conclusion: 提出未来关键方向：可信仿真与数据、开放世界多模态认知、长时程安全决策、物理约束下的策略学习、以及空间计算基础设施。

Abstract: Space robots are increasingly expected to perform long-duration, contact-rich, and multi-stage operations with limited human intervention. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI), robot learning, and embodied foundation models provide new opportunities to improve the autonomy and adaptability of such systems, but their transfer to space is constrained by scarce mission data, space-specific dynamics and sensing conditions, limited onboard resources, and stringent safety requirements. This article reviews artificial intelligence-enabled space robot operations (AI-SRO) from a capability-building perspective. We first summarize representative operational scenarios, autonomy trends, and space-specific constraints. We then establish a three-layer technical framework comprising capability foundations, capability formation, and capability deployment/evolution. Within this framework, we review simulation environments, datasets and benchmarks; task and environment understanding, state perception, decision-making and planning, and action execution; and onboard deployment, ground-to-space adaptation, continual learning, and capability transfer. Finally, we propose key research directions toward trustworthy simulation and data, open-world multimodal cognition, long-horizon safe decision-making, physically constrained policy learning, and space computing infrastructures.

</details>


### [157] [Waggle Dance Inspired Motion Communication for Multiple UAVs in MuJoCo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16958)
*Zhang Nengbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受蜜蜂摇摆舞启发的MuJoCo通信系统，将MoCom的单对单运动通信扩展为一名表演者对多名观察者，表演者用四种飞行原语与显式空信号广播六位导航载荷，观察者各自从RGB图像提取光流、识别符号、解析消息并在确认完整帧后开始导航。标准测试中17次广播共53次接收暴露得到44条正确完整消息，13次广播通过全组解码与执行检查，另有3个对照条件符合预期；补充测试在3次广播中得到14次成功接收暴露。近距与广角配置暴露感知与协议局限，未成功接收者保持静止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜜蜂摇摆舞通过一个体的运动向同伴传递空间信息并引导其行动，这一机制可用于多智能体间由运动承载的隐式通信。已有MoCom工作局限于点对点运动通信，缺少一名表演者对多名观察者广播并各自触发导航的场景，因此需要构建并验证可扩展的广播到行动机制及其感知与协议边界。

Method: 在MuJoCo中构建一跳多收系统：表演者以四种飞行原语和显式空信号编码并广播六位导航载荷；1至5名观察者各自基于机载RGB图像计算光流轨迹、识别符号、解析消息，并在确认自身完整帧后才启动导航。各观察者的接收状态与执行触发相互独立，仿真控制与安全校验使用共享真值。实验采用静止观察者、25 Hz图像输入与理想状态反馈控制，在固定标准套件、补充套件以及无消息/输入故障对照下评估，并测试近距与广角几何配置。

Result: 标准套件：17次名义广播、53次接收暴露中，得到44条正确完整消息，13次广播通过组级解码与执行检查。补充套件（同一冻结代码、默认几何）在3次广播中取得14次成功接收暴露。3个无消息或输入故障对照均达到预期结果。近距与广角配置出现跟踪与识别失败，未成功接收的观察者保持静止。

Conclusion: 有限的仿真结果表明，在测试条件下摇摆舞启发的“广播到行动”机制具有可行性，同时揭示了当前感知能力与通信协议在近距、广角等条件下的局限；后续工作需针对这些边界改进跟踪、符号识别与协议鲁棒性。

Abstract: The honeybee waggle dance motivates a communication mechanism in which one agent's movement conveys spatial information that guides other agents' actions. This paper presents a MuJoCo system that extends the point-to-point motion communication setting of MoCom to one performer and multiple observers. A performer broadcasts a six-bit navigation payload using four flight primitives and explicit null signals. Each of one to five observers processes its own onboard RGB images, extracts optical-flow trajectories, recognizes symbols, parses the message, and starts navigation only after confirming its own complete frame. Reception states and execution triggers are separate across observers, while simulation control and safety checks use shared ground truth. With stationary observers, 25 Hz image input, and ideal state-feedback control, a fixed standard suite yielded 44 correct complete messages from 53 receiver exposures across 17 nominal broadcasts; 13 broadcasts passed all group-level decoding and execution checks. Three additional no-message or input-fault controls met their expected outcomes. A separately reported supplemental suite, using the same frozen code at the default geometry, achieved 14 successful receiver exposures across three broadcasts. Near-range and wide-angle configurations exposed tracking and recognition failures, while unsuccessful receivers remained stationary. These finite simulation results support the feasibility of a waggle-dance-inspired broadcast-to-action mechanism under the tested conditions and identify the present perceptual and protocol limits.

</details>


### [158] [Overcoming technical adoption barriers for mobile service robots in rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16996)
*Christian Sternitzke,Sebastian Blumenthal,Lukas Kleedoerfer,Verena Deserno,Anke Mayfarth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文阐述如何通过增加外围功能与小幅改进，使已获批为医疗设备的移动服务机器人提升技术成熟度，从而获得临床环境中的用户接受度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗机器人领域的多数文献聚焦于低技术成熟度的早期研究，缺乏对已获批医疗设备如何进一步提升成熟度、实现用户接受与广泛应用的探讨。

Method: 以一台已获医疗设备批准的移动服务机器人 为案例，通过在实际临床与操作环境中测试，识别并添加外围功能及小幅改进。

Result: 这些外围功能与改进对临床环境下的用户接受至关重要，且往往无法通过事先询问用户而获得，因为部分需求只有在实际测试中才会被意识到。

Conclusion: 建议医疗领域服务机器人开发者提前规划此类后续开发，因其可能耗费大量实施时间，却是实现用户接受与系统广泛应用的必要条件。

Abstract: Many publications on robotic systems in healthcare describe early-stage work on low technology readiness levels. This paper describes how a mobile service robot approved as a medical device reaches higher technology readiness levels by adding peripheral functions and smaller improvements, which are pivotal for user acceptance in clinical environments and which often cannot be elicited by questioning users ex-ante as certain aspects only come in mind from testing the systems in clinical settings or operational environments. Especially developers of service robots in healthcare are advised to plan with such downstream developments, which can take significant implementation time, to obtain user acceptance and achieve widespread adoption of their robotic systems.

</details>


### [159] [SWIM: Vision-Language-Grounded Soft Whole-Body Interactive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17035)
*Tingcong Liu,Aye Phyu Phyu Aung,Junjie Xiong,Siyi Ma,Bo An,Ke Wu,Senthilnath Jayavelu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SWIM框架，将RGB观测与语言指令映射为软体机器人的全身驱动指令序列，通过扩散动作头与视觉软本体感知共享表征，并在仿真与硬件上验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体与连续体机器人依赖分布式形变和接触实现操作，但将语言与视觉上下文转化为可执行的全身驱动指令仍是基础性难题。

Method: SWIM-VLA策略将扩散动作头与视觉软本体感知（VSP）结合，在RGB观测、语言指令与肌腱状态的共享表征上建模专家指令块的条件分布；VSP利用仿真真值监督有序身体锚点预测，以在有限示范下保留身体几何信息。通过不断演化的仿真观测进行迭代虚拟滚动生成指令序列，并由具身机械智能完成物理执行，内在顺应性实现局部接触自适应而无需在线策略查询。

Result: 在平面肌腱驱动软体机器人上评估装填、到达与抓取任务（抓取目标带锚点）。仿真中成功率分别为100%、96%、88%，优于适配的OpenVLA-OFT基线及受控消融；硬件上分别为100%、80%、75%，而同一策略检查点直接在线部署仅为75%、40%、25%。

Conclusion: 共享表征与本体感知监督结合迭代虚拟滚动，可有效提升软体机器人语言条件下的全身操作性能，并显著缩小仿真到硬件的差距。

Abstract: Soft and continuum robots enable manipulation through distributed body deformation and contact, yet translating language and visual context into executable whole-body actuation remains a fundamental challenge. We present SWIM, a framework that maps an initial RGB observation and a language instruction to a complete actuation-command sequence. Its vision-language-action (VLA) policy, SWIM-VLA, combines a diffusion action head with Visual Soft Proprioception (VSP) through a shared representation of RGB observations, language instructions, and tendon states. The diffusion head models conditional distributions of expert command chunks, while VSP supervises ordered body-anchor predictions using simulation ground truth, encouraging the representation to retain body geometry when learning from limited demonstrations. Embodied mechanical intelligence supports physical execution of command sequences generated through iterative virtual rollout from evolving simulated observations, with intrinsic compliance providing local contact adaptation without online policy queries. We evaluate SWIM on packing, reaching, and grasping on a planar tendon-driven soft robot, with grasping targets anchored. In simulation, SWIM-VLA achieves success rates of 100\%, 96\%, and 88\%, respectively, outperforming an adapted OpenVLA-OFT baseline and controlled ablations. On hardware, SWIM achieves success rates of 100\%, 80\%, and 75\%, compared with 75\%, 40\%, and 25\% for direct online deployment of the same policy checkpoint.

</details>


### [160] [BRAVE-6D: Benchmark for Robotic Active Vision in 6DOF Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17106)
*Philipp Ausserlechner,Bernhard Neuberger,Alessandro Scherl,Michael Schebek,Stefan Thalhammer,Markus Vincze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BRAVE-6D基准，基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）视图合成技术，为机器人主动视觉系统中的物体位姿估计提供统一的评测场景与工具，并给出视觉伺服基线方案，实现对小型物体的准确位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型物体的检测与抓取在机器人领域仍是难题。主动视觉（让机器人靠近物体）是直观的解决思路，但不同方法难以在相同物理场景下进行公平比较，因为需要完全一致的真实场景搭建。

Method: 构建BRAVE-6D基准，利用基于高斯泼溅（3DGS）的视图合成技术生成场景与评测工具，用于评估面向物体位姿估计的机器人主动视觉系统，并提供在场景内执行视觉伺服的基线方案。

Result: 基线方案能够在合成场景中完成视觉伺服，并准确估计小型物体的位姿。

Conclusion: BRAVE-6D为主动视觉系统提供了可复现、统一的评测平台，有助于推动小型物体位姿估计与抓取研究的发展。

Abstract: Detecting and grasping small objects remains a significant challenge in robotics. Active vision, where the robot moves closer to the object, is an intuitive solution, yet comparing approaches on common ground is difficult since identical physical scene setups are required. Hence, we introduce BRAVE-6D, a benchmark designed to evaluate robotic active vision systems for object pose estimation, a crucial first step in grasping objects. BRAVE-6D leverages view synthesis based on Gaussian Splats (3DGS) to provide scenes and tools for benchmarking active vision systems. We show baseline solutions performing visual servoing within the scene and accurately estimating the poses of small objects.

</details>


### [161] [Intrinsic Robot Rewarding: Reusing VLA Representations for Autonomous Evaluation and Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17115)
*Tobias Schaffer,Mohab Elkhayat,Daniela Nicklas,Mustafa Almohamad,Elham Al-Fuqara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IRR 复用 VLA 系统的冻结视觉编码器与成功示范端点，通过参考库与评分操作实现机器人结果自评与奖励生成，无需额外评估器或感知主干，TRL 4 演示器已验证，下一步是连接内部评估与物理策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 系统已具备丰富视觉表征与成功示范两类资源，但目前仅用于表征与示范本身；作者希望将这两项资源复用于机器人自身结果评估与策略改进反馈，以降低集成成本、提高奖励计算效率并减少反复的人工结果打分。

Method: 提出内在机器人奖励（IRR）：以成功示范终点构建任务特定参考，在策略冻结视觉编码器提供的特征空间中评估新结果；核心奖励机制仅在既有流程上增加参考库与评分操作，不引入独立学习型评估器或额外感知主干。基于视觉奖励与经验学习既有研究，给出现有工业机器人流程内的奖励公式、核心研究问题及评估方法。

Result: 在 COMAU Racer 3 上搭建了运行中的演示系统，达到技术成熟度 4（TRL 4），验证了该奖励机制在实验室条件下的可运行性；评估方法将奖励可靠性与任务成功率、监督投入相挂钩。

Conclusion: IRR 提供了一种可复用的、利用工业机器人系统已有数据与经验进行学习与改进的路径；当前实验室基础支撑下一阶段研究，即把内部结果评估连接到物理策略改进，从而以更低集成成本与更少人工评分实现策略持续优化。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) systems already bring together two valuable resources for robot learning: rich visual representations and demonstrations of successful task execution. Intrinsic Robot Rewarding (IRR) proposes to use these resources for a second, complementary purpose: evaluating the robot's own outcomes and providing feedback for policy improvement. Successful demonstration endpoints define task-specific references, and the policy's frozen visual encoder provides the feature space in which new outcomes are assessed. The core reward mechanism adds a reference bank and a scoring operation to the existing pipeline, without requiring a separate learned evaluator or an additional perception backbone. Our position is that this reuse offers a promising route to lower integration effort, efficient reward computation, and reduced recurring human outcome scoring. Building on established research in visual rewards and learning from experience, IRR brings these ideas into the robot's existing perception and demonstration pipeline. An operational COMAU Racer 3 demonstrator is available at technology readiness level 4 (TRL 4). This laboratory foundation supports the next research step: connecting internal outcome evaluation to physical policy improvement. We present the reward formulation, central research questions, and an evaluation methodology linking reward reliability to task success and supervision effort. The intended contribution is a reusable approach to learn and improve from the data and experience already available in industrial robot systems.

</details>


### [162] [LOTUSim-Energy: A Maritime Simulator for Human-Drone Interaction in Autonomous Offshore Operation \&amp; Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17124)
*Juliette Grosset,Marie Dubromel,Hélène Lechêne,Quentin Arzel,Cédric Buche*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LOTUSim-Energy——面向海上运维的实时多域人机交互仿真平台，统一异构无人载具、环境力耦合与沉浸式监控，并通过多域巡检场景验证系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上运维需跨空中、水面与水下多域作业并依赖人工监督，现实中难以在真实海况下开展可重复、低风险的自主性验证，缺乏统一环境物理、异构载具与沉浸式监督的一体化测试平台。

Method: 构建LOTUSim-Energy实时海事仿真器：采用分布式架构统一UAV、USV、AUV、ROV等异构无人载具，耦合风、浪、流等环境力；提供桌面与虚拟现实沉浸式监督界面；集成结构化海上任务库、真实物理、能量感知电池建模与故障检测流水线，并以模块化方式提供验证工具。

Result: 在单桩与过渡段结构的多域巡检场景中完成系统级性能演示；在实时能量监测下，评估了集成航路点跟随插件与基于AIS参考的轨迹跟踪的可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过统一环境物理、异构载具仿真与沉浸式监督的结合，LOTUSim-Energy为海上人机协作工作流的原型设计与预演提供了集成测试平台，有助于降低海上部署风险。

Abstract: Offshore maintenance requires operations in the air, the surface, and the subsea domain and include human supervision. This paper presents LOTUSim-Energy, a real-time maritime simulator designed for multi-domain human--drone interaction for offshore operation and maintenance. The plat- form unifies heterogeneous unmanned vehicles (Unmanned Aerial Vehicles: UAVs, Unmanned Surface Vehicles: USVs, Autonomous Underwater Vehicles: AUVs, Remotely Operated Vehicles: ROVs) within a distributed architecture coupling environment forcing (wind, waves, currents) and provides immersive user interfaces for supervision (desktop and virtual reality). A structured offshore task library enables repeatable evaluation of autonomy stacks under realistic metocean disturbances. The simulator supports realistic physics, energy-aware battery modeling, and fault-detection pipelines as modular validation tools.
