System-level performance is demonstrated on a multi-domain inspection scenario for monopile and transition piece structure, where we evaluate the reliability of integrated waypoint-follower plugin and Automatic Identification System (AIS)-referenced trajectory tracking under real-time energy monitoring. By combining unified environmental physics, heterogeneous vehicle simulation, and immersive supervision, LOTUSim-Energy provides an integration testbed for prototyping and rehearsing offshore human--robot collaboration workflows, as a step toward de-risking sea deployment.

</details>


### [163] [Continual Learning for Traversability Prediction with Uncertainty-Aware Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17141)
*Hojin Lee,Yunho Lee,Daniel A Duecker,Cheolhyeon Kwon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向非结构化环境可通行性预测的持续学习框架，利用生成式经验回放模型增量适应新地形，无需存储历史数据，并能感知生成样本的不确定性以缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境中机器人-地形交互复杂且不确定，易导致牵引力损失与动态失稳，可通行性预测至关重要。现有基于学习的方法难以适应新地形，且即便实现适应，也会遗忘此前训练环境中的经验，即灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出面向可通行性预测的持续学习框架，通过生成式经验回放模型增量适应新地形。该框架的核心优势在于：i）在不存储历史数据的前提下保留过往经验；ii）将回放模型生成样本的不确定性纳入其中，实现不确定性感知的适应。

Result: 在滑移转向机器人的真实世界实验中验证了框架的有效性，表明其能够在一系列多样环境中实现适应，同时缓解灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 该持续学习框架可支持机器人在非结构化环境中跨多样地形持续适应可通行性预测，且无需保存历史数据并具备不确定性感知能力，有效减轻灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Traversability prediction is a critical component of autonomous navigation in unstructured environments, where complex and uncertain robot-terrain interactions pose significant challenges such as traction loss and dynamic instability. Despite recent progress in learning-based traversability prediction, these methods often fail to adapt to novel terrains. Even when adaptation is achieved, retaining experience from previously trained environments remains a challenge, a problem known as catastrophic forgetting. To address this challenge, we propose a continual learning framework for traversability prediction that incrementally adapts to new terrains using a generative experience recall model. A key virtue of the proposed framework is two folds: i) retain prior experience without storing past data; and ii) incorporate the uncertainty of the generated samples from the recall model, enabling uncertainty-aware adaptation. Real-world experiments with a skid-steering robot validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, demonstrating its ability to adapt across a series of diverse environments while mitigating catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [164] [LiLi: Lie Theory Based 3D LiDAR Scan Alignment Degeneracy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17145)
*Vsevolod Hulchuk,Jan Bayer,Jan Faigl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LiLi方法，利用李理论识别SE(3)中的退化变换集合，用于3D LiDAR扫描对齐的退化检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在直线走廊、平坦田野等退化场景中，3D LiDAR扫描对齐解不唯一，影响定位与建图精度。现有退化检测方法忽略数据点重新关联的可能性，对噪声和复杂退化敏感。

Method: 基于李理论，识别SE(3)李群中全部退化变换。通过对优化解施加扰动并比较所得优化位姿，实现鲁棒退化检测；利用se(3)李代数生成元，系统描述退化变换集合。

Result: 合成数据上相比基于Hessian的SOTA方法降低对齐误差50%，含噪声数据集改进更显著；真实退化数据集上集成到LiDAR里程计，在260 m轨迹上定位性能优于Hessian退化检测参考方案，并在430 m往返隧道轨迹上成功，而参考方案失败。

Conclusion: LiLi通过李理论系统刻画退化变换集合，实现更鲁棒的退化检测，显著提升退化场景下的对齐与定位性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we study 3D LiDAR scan alignment in challenging scenarios with degeneracies, such as straight corridors or flat fields, where the alignment solution is not unique and compromises localization and mapping accuracy. Existing degeneracy detection methods that neglect the potential for reassociating data points are prone to being sensitive to noise and complex degeneracies. Therefore, we propose LiLi - a novel method that leverages Lie theory to identify the full set of degenerate transformations within the SE(3) Lie group of rigid transformations. The method employs perturbations of the optimized solution and compares the resulting optimized poses to ensure robust detection of degeneracies. By leveraging generators from the Lie algebra se(3), the method provides a systematic approach to describing the set of degenerate transformations. Quantitative evaluations on synthetic data show significant improvement over the state-of-the-art Hessian-based method, reducing alignment error by 50%, with more significant improvements for datasets featuring noise. In the real-world degenerate datasets, the proposed method integrated into LiDAR-based odometry yields superior localization performance compared to the reference solution based on the Hessian-based degeneracy detector on a 260 m long trajectory, and succeeds on a 430 m long round-trip tunnel trajectory where the reference fails.

</details>


### [165] [Kernel-Based Metrics Learning for Uncertain Opponent Vehicle Trajectory Prediction in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17147)
*Hojin Lee,Youngim Nam,Sanghun Lee,Cheolhyeon Kwon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于异构核度量（heterogeneous kernel metrics）的深度核学习（DKL）方法，用于自动驾驶赛车中对具有不确定轨迹的对手车辆进行轨迹预测，实现安全超车。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车中，对手车辆（OV）的行驶策略未知，导致其轨迹具有不确定性，安全超车面临重大挑战。因此需要一个能够鲁棒捕捉多样行驶策略并给出精确轨迹预测及不确定性估计的方法。

Method: 提出面向深度核学习（DKL）的异构核度量（heterogeneous kernel metrics）。该核度量的核心特性在于：在无监督方式下，基于自车（EV）与对手车辆（OV）的交互观测，将相似行驶策略对齐、将不相似策略分离，从而鲁棒建模OV的多样驾驶策略并进行轨迹预测与不确定性估计。

Result: 在1/10比例赛车平台上开展实验，结果表明预测精度提升，并实现对OV的安全超车；同时该方法对车载计算单元具有计算高效性，适用于快节奏赛车环境。

Conclusion: 所提异构核度量DKL方法能有效提升OV轨迹预测精度并支持安全超车，且计算开销低，适合车载实时应用；代码与视频已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous racing confronts significant challenges in safely overtaking Opponent Vehicles (OVs) that exhibit uncertain trajectories, stemming from unknown driving policies. To address these challenges, this study proposes heterogeneous kernel metrics for Deep Kernel Learning (DKL), designed to robustly capture the diverse driving policies of OVs, and carry out precise trajectory predictions along with the associated uncertainties. A key virtue of the proposed kernel metrics lies in their ability to align similar driving policies and disjoin dissimilar ones in an unsupervised manner, given the observed interactions between the Ego Vehicle (EV) and OVs. The efficacy of the proposed method is substantiated through experimental studies on a 1/10th scale racecar platform, demonstrating improved prediction accuracy and thereby safely overtaking against OVs. Furthermore, our method is computationally efficient for onboard computing units, affirming its viability in fast-paced racing environments. The video and source code can be found at https://github.com/HMCL-UNIST/OpponentPredictionWithKMDKL.git.

</details>


### [166] [HuMemSLAM: Efficient Human-Inspired Semantic Place Recognition for Robust Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17168)
*Mayowa Adebambo,Sebastian Donnelly,Armand Amaritei,Andrew Bradley,Alexander Rast*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HuMem-VPR（受人类记忆与感知启发的视觉位置识别方法）及HuMemSLAM（其与ORB-SLAM3的集成），兼顾高检索精度与低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统的SLAM需要可靠的位置识别。传统几何视觉SLAM依赖低层特征与几何一致性，易受感知混叠（不同地点外观相似）和感知变化（同一地点外观不同）影响。语义SLAM与现代学习式VPR虽提升鲁棒性，但实时部署仍需高检索精度与低延迟兼得。

Method: 受人类记忆与感知启发，提出HuMem-VPR，利用自下而上的感知证据与自上而下的上下文推理之间的双向关系，实现高层次的地点理解；并进一步将HuMem-VPR与ORB-SLAM3集成，构建HuMemSLAM。

Result: HuMem-VPR在真实图像基准上取得最高综合检索精度，在CARLA基准上精度具竞争力，延迟约为所评估的SOTA VPR方法的1/2至1/3。在评估的数据集族与在线实验中，HuMemSLAM相比ORB-SLAM3原生检索显著提升整体Recall@1，同时减少提交给几何后端的候选数量。

Conclusion: 通过模拟人类记忆与感知的双向机制，HuMem-VPR在精度与延迟间取得良好平衡，并作为ORB-SLAM3的检索前端有效提升SLAM系统的位置识别性能与效率。

Abstract: Autonomous systems require reliable place recognition for efficient and effective simultaneous localisation and mapping (SLAM). Traditional geometric visual SLAM approaches rely on low-level features and geometric consistency, but remain vulnerable to perceptual aliasing, where different places appear similar, and perceptual variation, where the same place appears different. Although semantic SLAM and modern learned visual place recognition (VPR) methods improve robustness under challenging perceptual conditions, real-time deployment requires both high retrieval accuracy and low latency. Inspired by human memory and perception, we propose HuMem-VPR, which exploits the bidirectional relationship between bottom-up perceptual evidence and top-down contextual reasoning to achieve high-level place understanding. We further introduce HuMemSLAM, the integration of HuMem-VPR with ORB-SLAM3. HuMem VPR achieved the highest aggregate retrieval accuracy on the real-image benchmark, competitive accuracy on the CARLA benchmark, and approximately two to three times lower latency than the evaluated state-of-the-art VPR methods. Across the evaluated dataset families and online experiments, HuMemSLAM substantially improved integrated Recall @1 over ORB-SLAM3's native retrieval while reducing the proposals submitted to its geometric backend.

</details>


### [167] [Fingers as Legs: Learning Self-Supported Locomotion and Manipulation with an Anthropomorphic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17172)
*Amirhossein Kazemipour,Hehui Zheng,Robert Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了一只拟人手如何在不改变其手指设计与位置控制器的前提下，同时学习身体移动、支撑自重与环境交互等技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行走式机器手需要用同一组手指完成移动身体、支撑重量和交互环境等任务，但通常需要单独的运动机构。作者希望探索拟人手能否复用其手指实现运动与操作，从而构建紧凑的移动操作平台。

Method: 采用强化学习方法，考虑手部手指不等的特点，在基于硬件测量校准的仿真环境中训练。平台具备机载电源与计算，实现自包含；使用针对该手部设计的奖励函数进行训练，并与为四足机器人调优的奖励进行对比。

Result: 在仿真中，本文奖励公式使手部移动速度优于四足调优奖励。在硬件上，任务专用策略实现了无缆爬行、转向与跌倒恢复；在支撑自重的同时，可无视觉执行连续键盘指令，并借助俯视视觉反馈将物体推向目标。

Conclusion: 结果证明了一种紧凑的移动操作器，能够复用手指同时用于运动与交互，而无需单独的运动机构。

Abstract: A walking robotic hand must use the same fingers to move its body, support its weight, and interact with the environment. We show how an anthropomorphic hand can learn these skills while retaining its finger design and position controller. Onboard power and computation make the platform self-contained. Our reinforcement learning approach accounts for the hand's unequal fingers, with training in a simulator calibrated from hardware measurements. In simulation, the hand moves faster with our reward formulation than with tuned rewards originally designed for quadrupeds. On hardware, task-specific policies enable untethered crawling, steering, and fall recovery. While supporting its own weight, the hand also executes successive keyboard commands without vision and pushes an object to targets using overhead visual feedback. These results demonstrate a compact mobile manipulator that reuses its fingers for locomotion and interaction, without a separate locomotion mechanism.

</details>


### [168] [Fleet-To-Lab: A Transfer Learning Framework For Lunar Rover Slippage Estimation Via Model Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17187)
*Riccardo Viviano,Saki Omi,Andrej Orsula,Miguel Olivares-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Fleet-to-Lab迁移学习框架与AcoMerge混合群体智能算法，通过融合多台异构月球车模型缓解地-月域差异，实现少数据下的车轮滑移估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的车轮滑移估计对自主月球车移动与导航至关重要。基于地球数据训练的机器学习模型难以泛化到月球地形，而真实月球数据因任务数量有限、采集成本高昂而极为稀缺，存在显著的地-月域差异（Earth-Moon domain gap）。

Method: 提出Fleet-to-Lab迁移学习框架，利用以往部署的异构月球车采集的本体感知（proprioceptive）数据；将多个异构专家模型融合为单一架构，并使用月球车部署后采集的小规模数据集。进一步提出AcoMerge——一种混合群体智能算法，通过搜索专家参数的最优组合来实现模型融合。

Result: 在高保真物理仿真实验中，相比深度模型融合基线，在平衡准确率（balanced accuracy）与宏F1（macro-F1）上均取得提升；AcoMerge在深度架构联合训练上表现相当，同时在更小的模型上获得更高的macro-F1与balanced accuracy。

Conclusion: 该框架表明，模型融合可作为数据受限的空间机器人任务中滑移估计的一种可行迁移学习替代方案。

Abstract: Accurate wheel slip estimation is essential for autonomous lunar rover mobility and navigation. Machine Learning models trained on terrestrial data generalize poorly to lunar terrain, and real lunar datasets are scarce due to the limited number of missions and costly data acquisition. We present Fleet-to-Lab, a transfer learning framework that leverages proprioceptive data collected by previously deployed heterogeneous lunar rovers to mitigate the Earth-Moon domain gap in slip estimation for a future deployable unit. We fuse several heterogeneous expert models into a single architecture, using a modest dataset collected after the rover deployment. We propose AcoMerge, a new hybrid swarm-intelligence algorithm that performs model fusion by searching for an optimal combi- nation of expert parameters. Experiments conducted in a high- fidelity physics simulation show balanced accuracy and macro- F1 improvements compared to deep model fusion baselines. AcoMerge exhibits competitive performance with joint training on deep architectures, while achieving higher macro-F1 and balanced accuracy on a smaller model. Overall, our framework shows model fusion as a possible transfer learning alternative for slippage estimation in space robotic missions with limited data.

</details>


### [169] [TIO-Former: Ultra-Lightweight 6-Directional ToF-Inertial Odometry for Nano-UAVs via a Streaming Causal Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17198)
*Yang Liu,Yifan He,Wenhao Zhao,Xiangyu Mo,Yang Xu,Hao Wei,Mingze Ma,Huan Li,Yifan Wu,Zipeng Dai,Xin Zhou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TIO-FORMER：一种无相机、无光流、无地图的测距-惯性里程计框架，仅用IMU与15g六正交8x8 ToF阵列实现6-DoF估计，并在真实飞行中显著降低漂移且可在RISC-V边缘平台实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主纳米无人机导航需在严格SWaP-C约束下获得精确自运动估计，但视觉与LiDAR超载荷限制，光流在弱纹理场景退化，纯惯性估计存在累积漂移。多区ToF阵列虽轻量且提供度量信息，然而每帧仅384个距离读数，面临无效回波、各向异性可观测性和随时间增长的计算开销三大挑战。

Method: 1) 前端：对连续测距网格采用双边门控差分（bilateral gated difference）进行配对；2) IMU引导的交叉注意力，依据平台运动学动态路由方向性特征；3) 流式因果Transformer，将未压缩的Local KV缓存与压缩的Chunk-FIFO记忆耦合，使推理成本与内存占用不随飞行时长增长（有界）。

Result: 真实飞行实验中，相比纳米无人机光流将开环位置误差降低54.4%，相比学习型惯性基线降低66.4%–89.1%；并在多环境与严重传感退化条件下评估鲁棒性。部署于RISC-V边缘伴飞计算机，P95延迟10.466 ms，峰值常驻内存6.324 MiB（<5%系统RAM）。

Conclusion: 稀疏测距感知可为资源受限微型飞行器提供实用的几何锚定，TIO-FORMER在精度与SWaP-C约束间取得良好平衡，具备边缘实时部署可行性；代码已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous nano-UAV navigation requires accurate ego-motion estimation under stringent size, weight, power, and computing (SWaP-C) constraints, where visual sensors and LiDARs exceed payload limits, optical flow degrades in low-texture scenes, and inertial-only state estimation is susceptible to accumulated drift. While multi-zone time-of-flight (ToF) arrays provide a lightweight metric complement, 6-DoF estimation from merely 384 ranges per frame is challenged by invalid returns, anisotropic observability, and temporal computational scaling. We propose TIO-FORMER, a camera-free, optical-flow-free, and mapless range-inertial odometry framework driven by an IMU and an ultra-lightweight (15 g) payload of six orthogonal 8 x 8 ToF arrays. Our frontend pairs consecutive range grids with a bilateral gated difference, while IMU-guided cross-attention dynamically routes directional features conditioned on platform kinematics. A Streaming Causal Transformer couples an uncompressed Local KV cache with compressed Chunk-FIFO memory, maintaining bounded inference cost and memory footprint independent of flight duration. In real-flight evaluations, TIO-FORMER reduces open-loop position error by 54.4% compared to nano-UAV optical flow and by 66.4%-89.1% over learned inertial baselines. We also evaluate performance across multiple environments and robustness under severe sensing degradation. Deployed on an edge RISC-V companion computer, TIO-FORMER achieves a P95 latency of 10.466 ms and peak resident memory of 6.324 MiB (less than 5 percent system RAM), demonstrating that sparse range sensing provides practical geometric anchoring for resource-constrained micro-aerial robots. Code is available at https://github.com/Ly041021/TIO-Former.

</details>


### [170] [FluxVLA Engine: A One-Stop VLA Engineering Platform for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17210)
*Yinhao Li,Weixin Mao,Zihan Lan,Jikun Rong,Qirui Hu,Yiming Zhang,Weipeng Deng,Bowen Shen,Minzhao Zhu,Yiming Mao,Yan Yang,Chenguang Cui,Hongyuan Chen,Xu Huang,Zheyi Zhao,Pinxi Shen,Bozhen He,Zhen Fu,Yifan Wang,Zexin Zhang,Ang Gao,Haoyu Chen,Chengqi Shi,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FluxVLA Engine 是一个开源的、配置驱动的具身策略平台，通过标准化数据、模型、训练、评估、推理与机器人接口，实现从数据到部署的可复现工作流，解决具身学习算法工程化落地中的碎片化瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型、世界动作模型（WAM）与离线强化学习方法快速扩展了具身策略的设计空间，但算法转化为可靠机器人系统仍受限于碎片化的数据格式、训练栈、评估协议、推理运行时以及各具身平台专用接口，缺乏可复现的工程化路径。

Method: 提出 FluxVLA Engine，一个配置驱动、开源的具身策略平台。它不引入新的策略模型，而是为数据集、视觉语言模型与世界模型、动作头、基于奖励/优势加权的学习、分布式训练、仿真评估、优化推理及机器人算子标准化统一接口。平台集成组合式双臂仿真、可扩展自动数据生成、模型解耦的人机回环（rollout、接管、纠错采集、奖励标注）；面向实时物理执行，结合 Real-Time Chunking (RTC)、加速推理后端、轻量远程 GPU 服务与可配置轨迹后处理。

Result: 通过共享且可审计的契约，将离线学习、仿真验证、在线纠错与真机执行贯通为统一流程；代码已开源于 https://github.com/FluxVLA/FluxVLA。

Conclusion: FluxVLA 针对具身学习算法从有前景的研究成果到可复现评估与可靠部署之间的工程瓶颈，提供了标准化、可复现、端到端的平台化解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models, world-action models (WAMs), and offline reinforcement learning methods are rapidly expanding the design space of embodied policies, yet turning these algorithms into reliable robot systems remains constrained by fragmented data formats, training stacks, evaluation protocols, inference runtimes, and embodiment-specific interfaces. We present $\mathrm{FluxVLA}$ Engine, an open, configuration-driven platform that turns heterogeneous embodied-policy components into a reproducible data-to-deployment workflow. Rather than introducing another policy model, $\mathrm{FluxVLA}$ standardizes interfaces for datasets, visual-language and world models, action heads, reward- or advantage-weighted learning, distributed training, simulation evaluation, optimized inference, and robot operators. The engine further integrates compositional dual-arm simulation, scalable automatic data generation, and model-decoupled human-in-the-loop rollout, takeover, correction collection, and reward annotation. For responsive physical execution, it combines Real-Time Chunking (RTC) with accelerated inference backends, lightweight remote GPU serving, and configurable trajectory post-processing. Together, these capabilities connect offline learning, simulation validation, online correction, and real-robot execution through shared and auditable contracts. $\mathrm{FluxVLA}$ therefore targets the engineering bottlenecks separating promising embodied-learning algorithms from reproducible evaluation and dependable deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/FluxVLA/FluxVLA

</details>


### [171] [Swim-and-Breach at Palm Scale: A Rudder-Steered Two-Propeller Underwater Robot Platform with Differential-Thrust Pitch Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17240)
*Daehyun Choi,Ian Bergerson,Hengjia Zhu,Tianjun Lan,Saad Bhamla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种手掌大小（65 mm、34 g）的"游动-跃出"两栖机器人平台，通过双桨推进与尾舵控制实现高速游动及无缝跃出水面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建可跨越障碍与干涸间隙的小型机器人，用于溪流、被淹结构及工业系统的巡检，需要一种兼具高速游动与跃出水面能力的紧凑平台。

Method: 采用两枚垂直叠放的螺旋桨，在PID回路下同时提供推进与差动推力俯仰控制，尾舵提供偏航控制；船体经流场仿真评估，阻力较等效长方体降低五倍；螺旋桨基于B系列（B-series）建模优化，并用测功机测量验证。

Result: 游速达13.9体长/秒，转向速率209度/秒，达到水下机器人报道的上限水平；自由游动中俯仰回路可将机体转至任意指令抬头角，尾舵稳定出水姿态，实现1.6体长高度、3.7体长距离的巡航-跃出-巡航无缝序列。

Conclusion: 该平台可用于构建跨越障碍与干涸间隙的小型机器人，服务于溪流、被淹结构及工业系统的巡检应用。

Abstract: We present a palm-scale (65 mm, 34 g) swim-and-breach robot platform. Two vertically stacked propellers provide both propulsion and differential-thrust pitch control under a proportional-integral-derivative (PID) loop, and a tail rudder adds yaw control. The hull, evaluated by flow simulation, reduces the drag five-fold relative to an equivalent cuboid, and the propellers are optimized using B-series modeling validated by dynamometer measurements. The current robot swims at 13.9 body lengths per second and turns at 209 deg per second, corresponding to the upper limits reported for underwater robots. In free swimming, the pitch loop turns the body to any commanded nose-up pitch angle, and, with the rudder stabilizing the exit, the current robot leaps 1.6 body lengths high and 3.7 long in a seamless cruise-leap-cruise sequence. The platform can be used to build small-scale robots that cross barriers and dry gaps between pools for inspection in streams, flooded structures, and industrial systems.

</details>


### [172] [DriveMCP: An Agentic AI framework for Advanced Driver Assistance System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17247)
*Farzad Nadiri,Mehdi Cina,Ahmad B. Rad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DriveMCP，一个模块化、可审计的智能体式驾驶辅助框架，将感知、合规推理、车辆状态解读与安全仲裁整合为流水线，并在CARLA仿真中验证其降低交通违规与超速表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶辅助/VLM方案多将合规要素（如限速、辖区信息）作为仿真器真值直接注入，缺乏真实感知到合规推理的闭环；同时缺少对法规、天气、车辆健康等多源信息的结构化融合与安全仲裁，导致违规率与超速风险较高、缺乏可审计性。

Method: DriveMCP包含：1）类传感器感知栈与DriveLM视觉语言前端，生成图结构场景理解（GVQA）与语言化驾驶信息；2）结构化解析层从DriveLM输出中提炼world_state中的限速与辖区线索，而非注入仿真真值；3）有状态编排层协调以MCP服务器形式暴露的专家模块——Rules服务器（基于RAG的辖区交通法规与标志规范推理）、Weather服务器（牵引力风险与情境化速度建议）、MCP-CAN服务器（CAN/OBD遥测与诊断上下文）；4）融合输出生成结构化决策建议，再由RSS启发的护栏在受限在线自适应下仲裁“speak vs act”。

Result: 在CARLA的多语言、跨境及动态限速场景中，DriveMCP相较VLM-Direct、VLM-Direct+RAG和VLM-Tools-NoArbiter基线，降低了交通违规与超速，改善了危险响应时间，并保持亚秒级建议延迟。

Conclusion: 模块化MCP专家编排结合结构化合规解析与RSS式安全仲裁，能够有效提升驾驶辅助的合规性与响应性能，同时保持低延迟与可审计性，为多辖区、多语言场景下的智能体驾驶辅助提供了可行范式。

Abstract: An agentic AI driver-assistance framework that integrates perception, compliance reasoning, vehicle-state interpretation, and safety arbitration into a modular and auditable pipeline. The architecture, referred to as DriveMCP, incorporates a sensor-like perception stack alongside DriveLM as the vision-language front end to generate a graph-structured scene understanding (Graph Visual Question Answering) and language-grounded driving information. Key compliance elements in world_state, including posted speed limits and jurisdiction cues, are derived from DriveLM outputs through a structured parsing layer rather than being injected as simulator ground truth. A stateful orchestration layer coordinates specialized experts exposed as Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers: (i) a Rules server that performs retrieval-augmented compliance reasoning over jurisdiction-specific traffic codes and sign conventions, (ii) a Weather server that estimates traction risk and contextual speed advisories, and (iii) an MCP-CAN server that surfaces Controller Area Network (CAN)/On-Board Diagnostics (OBD) telemetry and diagnostic context for health-aware risk shaping. These outputs are fused to generate a structured decision that prompts a recommended course of action. The outcome is then further filtered by a Responsibility-Sensitive Safety (RSS)-inspired guardrail that arbitrates speak versus act decisions under bounded online adaptation. In CARLA simulation across multilingual, cross-border, and dynamic speed-limit scenarios, DriveMCP reduces traffic infractions and overspeed relative to the VLM-Direct, VLM-Direct+RAG, and VLM-Tools-NoArbiter baselines, while improving hazard response time and maintaining sub-second advisory latency.

</details>


### [173] [Port-Hamiltonian Koopman Operator Synthesis for Mechanical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17249)
*Rajpal Singh,Aditya Singh,Jishnu Keshavan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于广义动量坐标的结构保持Koopman框架，通过结构约束神经网络联合学习提升函数与端口哈密顿Koopman生成元，并用Cayley中点离散化在离散时间精确保持存储-耗散平衡，实现对Euler-Lagrange系统的被动、稳定预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯轨迹数据学习的有限维Koopman模型可能违背底层力学的能量结构，导致预测出现人为能量增长并在递归传播下发散，因此需要一种结构保持的建模方法。

Method: 在广义动量坐标下构建Koopman框架，使机械驱动表现为已知且状态无关的端口并在提升动力学中显式保留；设计结构约束神经网络联合学习提升函数与端口哈密顿Koopman生成元，使学习动力学被动性由构造保证；采用Cayley中点离散化在离散时间精确保持存储-耗散平衡，并解析推导离散存储平衡与稳定性保证。

Result: 仿真与实验表明，相比Koopman基线，该方法在预测精度、数据效率和闭环跟踪上均有提升，且随系统维度升高增益更明显。

Conclusion: 该结构保持Koopman框架通过动量坐标、被动性构造与保结构离散化，从根本上保证了学习预测器的能量结构与稳定性，优于无结构约束的基线方法。

Abstract: Finite-dimensional Koopman models enable efficient linear prediction and control of nonlinear robotic systems. However, models learned purely from trajectory data may violate the energetic structure of the underlying mechanics, producing predictions that exhibit artificial energy growth and diverge under recursive propagation. This work presents a structure-preserving Koopman framework for Euler-Lagrange systems built on generalized-momentum coordinates. The momentum transformation exposes the mechanical actuation as a known, state-independent port, which is preserved explicitly in the lifted dynamics. A structure-constrained neural architecture is developed to jointly learn the lifting functions and a port-Hamiltonian Koopman generator, rendering the learned dynamics passive by construction rather than through penalty terms or post-hoc projection. A Cayley-midpoint discretization further preserves the corresponding storage-dissipation balance exactly in discrete time. These properties are established analytically by deriving the discrete storage balance and associated stability guarantees of the learned predictor. Simulation and experimental studies demonstrate improved prediction accuracy, data efficiency, and closed-loop tracking over Koopman baselines, with increasing gains for higher-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [174] [CAD-Based Relation Learning and Geometric-Symbolic Planning for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17263)
*Fabian Harlacher,Christian Friedrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合装配序列规划框架，结合学习式关系抽取与几何-符号推理，从非理想CAD数据生成可行的机器人拆解序列，在ASAP数据集上达到85.83%规划成功率并将中位规划时间降低一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配序列规划（ASP）具有组合爆炸特性，穷举方法难以适用于复杂工业装配；同时许多CAD模型缺乏可靠的语义接触信息或需要大量人工预处理，限制了现有方法的适用性。

Method: 用神经网络从点云预测语义几何关系，并引入人在回路（human-in-the-loop）验证以修正不确定预测与规划失败；将抽取的关系转化为符号装配图，由几何-符号规划器高效计算局部有效的机器人操作原语集合；采用基于可见性的光线投射策略引导可行拆解方向搜索，避免穷举组合搜索，局部解空间支持序列优化。

Result: 在自建装配数据集和ASAP测试数据集上评估。ASAP上规划成功率达85.83%；相比基线，各装配规模下中位规划时间降低超过一个数量级，30个以上零件的装配降低超过50倍。

Conclusion: 该混合框架可从非理想CAD数据实现高效机器人装配序列规划并显著缩短规划时间，通过学习式特征分割、人在回路验证与几何-符号推理的结合，为可扩展、可适配的机器人装配与拆解规划提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Assembly Sequence Planning (ASP) remains a challenging problem due to its combinatorial nature, making exhaustive planning approaches impractical for complex industrial assemblies. Furthermore, many CAD models lack reliable semantic contact information or require extensive manual preprocessing, limiting the applicability of existing methods. This paper presents a hybrid ASP framework combining learning-based relation extraction with geometric-symbolic reasoning to generate feasible robotic disassembly sequences from imperfect CAD data. A neural network predicts semantic geometric relations from point clouds, while human-in-the-loop verification enables correction of uncertain predictions and planning failures. Extracted relations are transformed into a symbolic assembly graph, enabling a geometric-symbolic planner to efficiently compute locally valid sets of robotic manipulation primitives. A visibility-based ray-casting strategy guides the search for feasible disassembly directions without requiring an exhaustive combinatorial search, while the local solution space enables efficient sequence optimization. The framework is evaluated on an introduced assembly dataset and on the ASAP test dataset. On the ASAP test dataset, the proposed planner achieves an 85.83% planning success rate while reducing the median planning time by more than one order of magnitude across all assembly sizes and by more than a factor of 50 for assemblies with more than 30 components compared to the baseline. The results demonstrate that the proposed hybrid framework enables efficient robotic assembly sequence planning from imperfect CAD data while substantially reducing planning time. By combining learning-based feature segmentation, human-in-the-loop verification, and geometric-symbolic reasoning, the framework provides a practical foundation for scalable and adaptable robotic assembly and disassembly planning.

</details>


### [175] [Escape-Aware Control Barrier Functions for Quadrotor Safety under Body-Rate Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17292)
*Lei Shi,Haosong Wen,Qichao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对输入受限系统，指出仅依赖状态的障碍函数在四旋翼上会错误认证无法及时逃逸的状态，提出基于"状态+上一步输入"增广对的逃逸障碍函数，并嵌入预测控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入受限系统的控制障碍函数通常将状态作为唯一变量，但四旋翼必须先重新定向推力矢量才能实现减速，而重定向受可达机体角速率限制。因此仅凭状态认证会认为某些状态是安全的，实际上却无法在时限内逃脱，形成"认证缺口"。

Method: 1) 以闭式形式刻画认证缺口，证明其宽度正比于接近速度、反比于机体角速率上限；2) 在状态与上一步施加输入的增广对上定义逃逸障碍函数，用单步可达推力上限度量逃逸权限；3) 给出闭式解及最大可认证接近速度的解析逆；4) 将该障碍嵌入预测控制器，且不增加额外状态开销。

Result: 在13状态四旋翼上开展550组配对闭环实验：所提控制器完成全部测试场景；同预测时域下的停止距离障碍函数在恰好进入预测缺口的两类场景中分别失败15%与25%；与在到达状态强制同一逃逸条件的在线backup-CBF基线相比，方向裕度大29–74度、净空为3–18倍；独立的保守rollout裁判未发现任何认证状态出现逃逸失败。

Conclusion: 仅状态障碍函数在含输入约束的敏捷系统中存在不可忽视的认证缺口，将最近输入纳入障碍定义可恢复认证可靠性；所提逃逸障碍具有闭式表达、可解析求逆、可无缝嵌入预测控制，并在四旋翼实验中实现零失败与更大安全裕度。

Abstract: Control barrier functions for input-constrained systems place the admissible input set inside the definition of the safe set, yet the resulting barrier is almost always a function of the state alone; On a quadrotor this is not cosmetic: because the thrust vector must be reoriented before it can decelerate an approach, and reorientation is limited by the attainable body rate, a state-only barrier certifies states from which no escape is reachable in time; We characterize the certification gap in closed form and show its width is proportional to closing speed and inversely proportional to the body-rate limit; We then define an escape barrier on the augmented pair of state and previously applied input, with escape authority measured over the one-step reachable thrust cap; It admits a closed form and an analytic inverse for the maximum certifiable closing speed, and embeds in a predictive controller at no additional state cost; Across 550 paired closed-loop episodes on a 13-state quadrotor, the proposed controller completes every tested scenario, whereas the stopping-distance barrier enforced over the same horizon fails 15% and 25% of episodes in exactly the two scenarios that enter the predicted gap; Against an online backup-CBF baseline enforcing the same escape condition at the reached state, it holds a 29-74 degree larger directional margin and 3-18 times the clearance, and an independent conservative rollout referee finds no certified state from which escape fails.

</details>


### [176] [Online Geometric Change Detection via Scene Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17302)
*David Thorne,Samuel Jia Cong Chua,Nakul Joshi,Aiden Wong,Christa S. Robison,Philip Osteen,Brett T. Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了基于场景分解的几何变化检测框架CDSD，利用LiDAR或RGB-D传感器将环境空间分解为独特场景，通过比较稠密局部子图高效检测变化，支持单会话在线检测，并在自建及开源多会话数据集上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在动态环境中执行长时间、单会话及多会话任务时，需要识别诸如倒伏树木或开启的门等环境变化，为在线规划提供上下文信息。现有基于几何SLAM的稠密对齐地图虽无需后处理，但全局地图跨会话比较计算代价高，且无法支持单会话在线变化检测。

Method: CDSD将已建图环境空间分解为独特场景，在场景内通过比较全局地图的稠密局部子集（子图）来高效发现变化。作为首个基于子图的几何变化检测方法，针对四项核心挑战提出解决方案：(1)确定冗余信息最少、适合变化检测的场景；(2)为每个场景生成稠密且具代表性的子图；(3)检测视场不同的子图间的变化；(4)处理检测到的变化以支持实时地图重建。

Result: 在格拉西斯角（马里兰州）陆军研究实验室设施采集的自定义数据集以及开源多会话变化检测数据集上验证了该算法。

Conclusion: CDSD通过场景分解与子图比较实现了高效、准确的在线几何变化检测，克服了全局地图比较的计算瓶颈，并支持单会话在线检测，为动态环境下的长期自主任务提供了有效的变化感知能力。

Abstract: Autonomous robots are increasingly deployed on long duration single- and multi-session missions in dynamic environments, where the ability to identify environmental changes such as fallen trees or opened doors provides important contextual information for online planning. We propose a framework called Change Detection via Scene Decomposition (CDSD) for accurate online geometric change detection using LiDAR or RGB-D sensors. Recent advances in geometric SLAM have made it possible to generate dense, tightly aligned maps without post processing, but comparing global maps across entire sessions is computationally expensive and does not allow for single-session online change detection. CDSD instead spatially decomposes mapped environments into unique scenes where changes can be found efficiently by comparing dense, local subsets of the global map called submaps. As the first submap-based approach for geometric change detection, we identify and address the following core challenges: 1) identifying appropriate scenes for change detection that require minimal redundant information; 2) generating dense and representative submaps for each scene; 3) detecting changes between submaps with differing fields of view; and 4) processing detected changes for real-time map reconstruction. Results demonstrate our algorithm on custom datasets collected at the Army Research Laboratory facility in Graces Quarters, Maryland, and on open-source multi-session change detection datasets.

</details>


### [177] [XPACE: Joint World and Action Modeling from Heterogeneous Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17372)
*Jiacheng Wei,Jerry Bai,Xiaoyu Yue,Zidong Wang,Xiaoyang Guo,Cheng Chen,Fanqi Pu,Fan Wu,Zhixu Yue,Yizhuo Li,Feng Qiu,Bo Liu,Yuying Ge,Hui Zhou,Chenyi Chen,Yixiao Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XPACE是一种统一的具身世界模型，同时充当世界动作模型（联合预测机器人动作与未来视频）和世界模拟器（预测给定动作的视觉后果），通过共享视频骨干与由粗到细的训练课程，实现异构经验学习与仿真驱动的策略自改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人需要利用多样化经验、选择动作并预判动作对世界的影响。核心动机在于：视频预测既能将异构经验与动作学习连接起来，又能生成新经验以改进策略，从而突破仅依赖机器人示教数据的局限。

Method: 提出XPACE统一具身世界模型。策略与模拟器共享视频骨干：用无动作标签视频学习视觉动力学，用带动作标签的人类与机器人示教联合学习视频与动作预测。采用由粗到细的训练课程，逐步强化机器人控制同时保留人类经验。此外，利用模拟器自适应其生成上下文，围绕专家示教合成"偏离-恢复"轨迹，并在筛选后的恢复样本上微调策略。

Result: 在小鹏IRON人形机器人上的实验表明：异构训练提升了鲁棒性，并实现了将人类观测技能迁移到机器人示教未覆盖的任务；由模型自身模拟器生成的恢复数据进一步提升了真实世界任务完成率。

Conclusion: 联合世界建模与动作建模能够将异构经验学习与仿真驱动的策略自改进相连接，验证了统一具身世界模型在提升机器人泛化性与真实任务表现方面的有效性。

Abstract: A general-purpose robot needs to draw on diverse experience, choose actions, and anticipate how those actions will change the world. We introduce XPACE, a unified embodied world model that serves as both a world action model, jointly predicting executable robot actions and future video, and a world simulator, predicting the visual consequences of prescribed actions. Our key insight is that video prediction can both connect heterogeneous experience to action learning and generate new experience for policy improvement. With a shared video backbone between the policy and simulator, we use action-unlabeled video to learn visual dynamics and action-labeled human and robot demonstrations to jointly learn video and action prediction. Building on this architecture, a coarse-to-fine training curriculum progressively emphasizes robot control while retaining human experience, allowing the policy to learn behaviors beyond those covered by robot demonstrations. Beyond learning from recorded experience, XPACE uses its simulator to create additional recovery supervision for the policy. Specifically, we adapt the simulator to its own generated context, synthesize deviation-recovery trajectories around expert demonstrations, and fine-tune the policy on filtered recovery examples. Experiments on XPENG's IRON humanoid robot show that heterogeneous training improves robustness and enables transfer of human-observed skills to tasks absent from robot demonstrations, while recovery data generated by the model's own simulator further improves real-world task completion. Together, these results demonstrate how joint world and action modeling connects learning from heterogeneous experience with simulation-driven policy self-improvement.

</details>


### [178] [Exact Fusion and Coordinated Exploration in Multi-Robot Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17384)
*Peng Wu,Mohsen Imani,Amidu Kamara,Md Tamzeed Islam,Seyede Fatemeh Ghoreishi,Mahdi Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文指出机器人团队共享环境模型中的"双重计数"误差：融合时局部后验之积把公共先验计了 n 次，规划时各机器人以同一共享信念评估计划导致团队收敛到同一未知量。作者提出以"证据增量"（融合时为已实现增量，规划时为期望增量）累加到共享自然参数上加以修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共轭指数族信念下的多机器人主动感知中，共享信念被"每个机器人各计一次"，造成信息增益被系统性高估：融合端的冗余与规划端的探索冗余。需要一种统一的、能保持理论保证的修正方案。

Method: 在共享自然参数上累加证据增量而非乘性融合局部后验：融合时用已实现增量，规划时用期望增量；以候选队友期望增量为下一机器人提供条件增益，并采用顺序承诺（sequential commitment）式规划。

Result: 修正后的增益之和等于联合增益；被剔除的冗余量等于计划观测流的总相关（total correlation）；顺序承诺保留 1/2 贪心保证。期望增量对固定采样路径的高斯信念与离散主动推断新奇近似下的 Dirichlet 信念精确成立，其团队目标在显式界内为闭式凹函数并逼近精确互信息；对有限假设类不成立，改以短程精确枚举替代。在协作 RockSample、觅食与野外监测任务中：融合修正不改变探索冗余，预期证据可消除冗余，顺序承诺以线性于团队规模的代价恢复接近集中式联合规划的大部分价值。

Conclusion: 共享信念的"逐机器人计数"是团队主动推断中两类冗余的共同根源，用（期望）证据增量替换共享先验即可统一消除，且在常见共轭信念下保持精确性、凸性与贪心近似保证，为分布式团队规划提供了可扩展且理论可控的替代方案。

Abstract: Robot teams that learn a common environment model exchange belief summaries and plan by the expected information gain of their actions. Under conjugate exponential-family beliefs the shared belief is counted once per robot at two points: at fusion, the product of local posteriors counts the common prior $n$ times, and at planning, every robot scores its plan under the same belief and the team converges on the same unknown. Both errors are removed by adding evidence increments to the shared natural parameter, realized increments at fusion and expected increments at planning. The expected increment of a committed teammate gives the next robot its conditional gain; corrected gains sum to the joint gain, the redundancy removed equals the total correlation of the planned observation streams, and sequential commitment keeps the $1/2$ greedy guarantee. The expected increment is exact for Gaussian beliefs with fixed sampling paths and for Dirichlet beliefs under the novelty approximation of discrete active inference, whose team objective has a closed concave form within an explicit bound of the exact mutual information, and fails for finite hypothesis classes, where a short exact enumeration replaces it. Experiments on cooperative RockSample, foraging, and field monitoring show that fusion correction leaves exploration redundancy unchanged, anticipated evidence removes it, and sequential commitment recovers most of the value of centralized joint planning at cost linear in the team size.

</details>


### [179] [Residual Fault Adaptation for Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation Under Runtime Joint Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17404)
*Linan Deng,Xing Liu,Lin Hong,Feng Hua,Guijun Ma,Zuogong Yue,Fumin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出残差故障适应（RFA）框架，通过冻结健康教师策略与循环残差策略补偿隐藏的命令通道故障，并在仿真与真机上验证了其提升灵巧手操作性能的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手内操作需要多关节协调控制，运行时关节故障会突然破坏成功操作所需的接触构型；且部署时控制器无法获取故障标签或切换信号，隐藏的命令通道故障难以被直接感知和补偿。

Method: 提出RFA，一种教师锚定框架：保留冻结的健康教师提供标称行为，训练循环残差策略从本体感受与命令响应历史中推断纠正动作。训练时用故障注入域随机化（FIDR）改变故障模式、受影响关节、严重程度与发生时刻，并用自适应采样提高近期表现较差的故障模式采样频率。冻结的Direct FIDR策略仅在故障激活的训练样本上提供分布参考，不参与部署。

Result: 在灵巧手仿真实验中，RFA在固定混合故障协议下相比健康策略提升了操作性能；在软件注入故障的真机实验中，实现了学习到的适应策略的零样本部署。

Conclusion: RFA能够在无故障标签和切换信号的情况下有效补偿隐藏命令通道故障，兼顾仿真性能提升与真机零样本部署能力。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation requires coordinated control of multiple actuated joints, and a runtime joint fault can abruptly disrupt the contact configuration required for successful manipulation. In this work, we propose residual fault adaptation (RFA), a teacher-anchored framework for compensating for hidden command-channel faults. RFA retains a frozen healthy teacher to provide nominal behavior and trains a recurrent residual policy to infer corrective actions from proprioceptive and command-response history. During training, fault-injection domain randomization (FIDR) varies the fault mode, affected joint, severity, and onset time, while adaptive sampling increases the frequency of fault modes associated with lower recent performance. A frozen Direct FIDR policy provides a distributional reference only on fault-active training samples and is absent from deployment. The deployed controller receives neither fault labels nor controller-switching signals. Simulation experiments on the dexterous hand indicate that RFA can improve manipulation performance relative to the healthy policy under a fixed mixed-fault protocol. Real-robot experiments with software-injected faults further demonstrate zero-shot deployment of the learned adaptation policy.

</details>


### [180] [Optimized Wrench Polytope Analysis for Real-Time Stability Control of Legged Robots in Complex Multi-Contact Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17405)
*Friedrich Graaf,Elias Birkefeld,Christian Eichmann,Elias Hofele,Tristan Schnell,Georg Heppner,Arne Roennau,Rüdiger Dillmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于任意接触场景的全驱动扳量多面体评估优化算法，可在49 Hz控制频率内计算各关节力矩，并成功部署于实际足式机器人硬件，在复杂场景下实现稳定控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人在斜坡、洞穴、脚手架等难以通行的地形中仍面临巨大挑战，现有控制器无法在这些复杂场景下实现稳定控制，因此需要一种能够应对任意接触场景的稳定控制器。

Method: 提出一种针对任意接触场景的全驱动扳量多面体（actuatable wrench polytope）评估优化算法，基于改进的分析算法计算机器人各关节力矩，并将其嵌入常规控制回路中用于驱动不同接触场景下的机器人位姿。

Result: 改进后的分析算法可在49 Hz控制频率内完成各关节力矩计算，实现了速度提升，从而可部署于常规控制回路；在仿真场景中进行了大量评估，并在实际足式机器人硬件上验证了适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的稳定性控制器在目前其他控制器无法实现的复杂场景中实现了稳定控制，验证了其在真实足式机器人应用中的有效性与可行性。

Abstract: Legged robots offer a variety of automation applications in real-world scenarios. But areas that are difficult to traverse, like slopes, caves, or scaffolding, still pose a great challenge for traversal. To tackle this problem, we propose an optimized algorithm for evaluating the full actuatable wrench polytope for arbitrary contact scenarios. With our improved analysis algorithm, the torques for each joint of the robot can be calculated within a control frequency of 49 Hz. The achieved speedup allows for deployment within a regular control loop for actuating robot poses for different contact scenarios. We evaluated our stability controller extensively in simulation scenarios and validated its applicability by deploying it on actual walking robot hardware. The proposed controller achieved stability in very complex scenarios that are currently not achievable by any other controller.

</details>


### [181] [Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability for Hybrid Systems: Unified Goal-Driven Control with Safety Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17430)
*Javier Borquez,Shuang Peng,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将经典 Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) 可达性分析扩展到混合动力系统，构建了跨离散与连续状态的安全集与广义值函数，并提出混合最小限制安全滤波器与混合后向可达-规避管两种性能集成机制，在四足机器人仿真与实物实验中验证了其在混合模式规划与安全关键应用中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合动力系统是接触丰富环境下机器人建模的有力框架，但连续动力学与离散模式切换之间的复杂耦合使安全性与性能保证极具挑战。经典 HJ 可达性仅适用于连续时间非线性系统，无法直接处理离散模式跳变，因此需要将其推广至混合系统。

Method: 1) 扩展经典 HJ 可达性分析至混合动力系统，在离散与连续状态上定义广义值函数，用以刻画安全集，并纳入控制约束与模型不确定性；2) 给出计算该值函数的数值算法；3) 提出混合最小限制安全滤波器，仅在必要时对名义控制器的离散与连续分量进行干预；4) 构建并计算混合后向可达-规避管，同时保证安全与目标可达，合成连续与离散控制策略。

Result: 通过仿真研究与四足机器人真实实验验证了框架的有效性，展示了其在混合模式规划与安全关键应用中的表现，包括安全滤波的按需干预特性以及安全与任务完成的双重保证。

Conclusion: 该工作将 HJ 可达性成功推广到混合动力系统，并首次在混合 HJ 可达性框架下处理可达-规避问题，为接触丰富机器人系统的安全与性能协同设计提供了形式化验证与控制器合成工具。

Abstract: Hybrid dynamical systems provide a powerful modeling framework for robotic systems, particularly in contact-rich environments. However, ensuring safety and performance in such systems remains challenging due to the intricate coupling between continuous dynamics and discrete mode transitions. In this work, we extend classical Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability analysis, a formal verification method for continuous-time nonlinear systems, to hybrid dynamical systems. Our framework characterizes safe sets for hybrid systems through a generalized value function defined over both discrete and continuous states while accounting for control constraints and model uncertainty. We additionally provide a numerical algorithm to compute this value function.
  Building on these safe sets, we propose two different mechanisms to integrate performance objectives. First, we introduce a hybrid least-restrictive safety filter that intervenes on both the discrete and continuous components of a nominal controller only when necessary to avoid unsafe states, thereby preserving nominal behavior whenever possible. Second, we formulate and compute hybrid backward reach-avoid tubes, enabling the simultaneous enforcement of safety and goal-reaching behavior, an extension not previously addressed within hybrid HJ reachability. This enables the synthesis of continuous and discrete control policies that guarantee both safety and task completion. We validate our framework through simulation studies and real-world experiments on a quadrupedal robot, demonstrating its effectiveness in hybrid mode planning and safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [182] [Gaussian Processes for Modelling Spatial Fields with Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17463)
*Guillermo Legarda Herranz,Gianpiero Francesca,Mauro Birattari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出位置无关高斯过程回归（LU-GPR），使无外部定位系统的机器人群体仅凭局部感知与通信即可协同建模空间场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人群体空间场建模方法依赖外部定位系统，各机器人需自行确定空间位置；在缺乏此类定位系统时无法工作，因此需要一种无需位置信息即可建模空间场的方法。

Method: 提出LU-GPR，允许每个机器人在与同伴就共同参考系达成一致的同时推断空间场的后验均值与方差，仅使用局部感知与通信。设计在线算法，使各机器人在其局部参考系收敛到共同参考系的过程中持续一致地推断局部估计；并借助专家乘积（product of experts）模型，将同伴估计与自身估计融合以获得全局模型。

Result: 结果表明，LU-GPR随机器人数量增加具有良好的可扩展性，且对有限通信范围具有鲁棒性；并在真实监测场景中演示了其用于估计疏散人群流的可行性。

Conclusion: LU-GPR为无外部定位系统条件下机器人群体协同建模空间场提供了可行方案，兼顾可扩展性与通信受限下的鲁棒性，并具备实际监测应用潜力。

Abstract: Robot swarms, by virtue of their decentralised architecture, are a natural tool for scalable, robust modelling of spatial fields, such as water temperature, wind velocity, or terrain elevation. However, existing methods rely on external positioning systems that allow each robot to determine its own position in space. Here, we introduce location-unaware Gaussian process regression (LU-GPR) as a solution to the modelling of spatial fields in the absence of such positioning systems. LU-GPR allows each robot to infer the posterior mean and variance of the field in space, while simultaneously agreeing on a common frame of reference with its peers, using only local sensing and communication. We propose an online algorithm that allows each robot to consistently infer local estimates as its local frame of reference converges to the common one. By means of a product of experts model, each robot also combines the estimates of its peers with its own to obtain a global model. Our results show that LU-GPR scales well with the number of robots and is robust to limited communication ranges. We also demonstrate how it can be used in real-world monitoring scenarios to estimate the flow of an evacuating crowd.

</details>


### [183] [Dissecting Motion-Prior Regularization for Data-Scarce Robotic Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17484)
*Ning Hu,Shuai Li,Jindong Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在仅用15个示教样本训练扩散策略的条件下，比较不同运动先验正则化对插入任务成功率的影响；联合正则与单一最小加加速度正则均达87.5%，但无协同增益证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究训练阶段的运动先验正则化能否提升少样本（仅15个示教）扩散策略在插入任务中的成功率，并甄别哪种先验机制更有效。

Method: 对比五类设置：最小加加速度、速度-曲率正则、两者联合、两者皆无、通用平滑正则；每种设置在四类记录工况下共进行80次真机试验，并以成功率及Wilson 95%置信区间进行可视化比较。

Result: 联合与仅最小加加速度均为70/80（87.5%）；仅速度-曲率为69/80（86.3%）；两者皆无为66/80（82.5%）；通用平滑为67/80（83.8%）。联合正则较无先验高出5.0个百分点，但相对仅最小加加速度无观测增益。

Conclusion: 结果支持将最小加加速度作为更简单、更易复现的候选先验；未证实协同效应、生物力学特异性、安全性提升或分布漂移鲁棒性。

Abstract: This study asks whether training-time motion-prior regularization can improve insertion success when a diffusion policy is learned from only 15 demonstrations. Minimum jerk discourages abrupt changes in predicted translational acceleration; speed-curvature regularization instead couples movement speed to path geometry. These are candidate mechanisms for task completion, not safety guarantees. We compare the priors individually and jointly, neither prior, and generic smoothness, with 80 real-robot trials per setting pooled over four recorded condition classes. Joint and minimum-jerk-only settings each achieved 70/80 successes (87.5%), versus 69/80 (86.3%) for speed-curvature only, 66/80 (82.5%) for neither prior, and 67/80 (83.8%) for generic smoothness. Success rates and Wilson 95% confidence intervals are visualized for direct comparison. Joint regularization exceeded neither by 5.0 percentage points but provided no observed gain over minimum jerk alone. The results motivate minimum jerk as the simpler candidate for replication, without establishing synergy, biomechanical specificity, improved safety, or distribution-shift robustness.

</details>


### [184] [Modality-Autoregressive World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17524)
*Adam Hung,Bardienus P. Duisterhof,Deva Ramanan,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ModAR，首个在预测动作前自回归去噪多种未来模态的世界-动作模型，系统研究模态组合与WAM公式的影响，性能优于现有方法且训练开销低约20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）通常将未来预测为RGB图像，但深度、预训练视觉特征和点轨迹等其他视觉模态能更高效地捕捉几何、语义和运动特征；然而如何在WAMs中最佳地组合这些模态仍是未解问题。

Method: 提出ModAR，首个在预测动作前对多个未来模态进行自回归去噪的WAM，使每次预测都能以已生成的模态为条件。从头训练，系统研究训练数据混合、预测模态及WAM公式对性能的影响。

Result: WAMs受益于预测点轨迹、DINO特征和深度图，额外预测未来RGB无一致收益；ModAR的序列化生成在所有评估数据规模下取得最高平均成功率，优于现有WAM公式。相比视频模型初始化的WAM Flex-π，ModAR在相同数据微调下成功率略高（75% vs. 72%），训练FLOPs少约20倍且无需预训练；在三个真实双臂任务上优于基线并随人类视频提升。

Conclusion: 多模态自回归去噪（ModAR）能更高效地利用几何、语义与运动特征，在更低训练成本下超越RGB预测范式与预训练WAM，并展现真实机器人任务与人类视频利用上的优势。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly model future observations and actions, typically predicting the future as RGB images. Other visual modalities such as depth, pretrained visual features, and point tracks can more efficiently capture geometric, semantic, and motion features. However, how best to combine these modalities within WAMs remains an open question. We introduce ModAR, the first WAM to autoregressively denoise multiple future modalities before predicting actions. This allows each prediction to condition on previously generated modalities. We train from scratch to systematically study how training-data mixtures, predicted modalities, and WAM formulations affect performance. In our evaluations, WAMs benefit from predicting point tracks, DINO features, and depth maps, while additionally predicting future RGB does not provide a consistent benefit. We also find that ModAR's sequential generation outperforms existing WAM formulations, with the highest average success rate at all evaluated data scales. We also fine-tune the video-model-initialized WAM Flex-$π$ on the same data; ModAR achieves a slightly higher observed average success rate (75% vs. 72%) while using approximately $20\times$ fewer training FLOPs and no pretraining. On three real-world bimanual tasks, ModAR outperforms baselines and improves with human videos.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Quantum Critical Solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16147)
*Subham Dutta Chowdhury,Leo Radzihovsky,Luca V. Delacrétaz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了横向声子速度消失的"软"量子固体的命运。此类固体具有立方弹性非线性，在$d<4$时相关，驱动系统在$d=3$趋于强耦合Lifshitz量子临界点，并用$\\epsilon=4-d$展开分析。相变一般为一级，但其临界数据可在Ginzburg区内获取。立方非线性在$d=2$时消失，主导相互作用变为四阶且边际，导致弱对数奇异性。讨论了在量子材料中实现"晶体Lifshitz临界"的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究横向声子速度消失的"软"（floppy）量子固体的临界行为，探索其是否受强耦合Lifshitz量子临界点支配，并寻找在量子材料中的潜在实现。

Method: 采用$\\epsilon=4-d$展开对$d=3$的强耦合Lifshitz量子临界点进行分析，识别立方弹性非线性在$d<4$时的相关性；在$d=2$中立方非线性消失，转而分析边际的四阶相互作用及其对数奇异性。

Result: $d<4$时立方弹性非线性相关，驱动系统趋向$d=3$的强耦合Lifshitz量子临界点；相变一般是一级，使得系统无法完全到达强耦合不动点，但临界数据仍可在相变附近的Ginzburg区内获得。$d=2$时立方非线性消失，四阶边际相互作用导致弱对数奇异性。

Conclusion: 软量子固体的相变通常为一级，预占了强耦合不动点的完全访问，但临界数据在Ginzburg区内可及；$d=2$表现出由边际四阶相互作用主导的弱对数奇异性。论文讨论了该"晶体Lifshitz临界"在量子材料中可能的实现。

Abstract: We study the fate of quantum solids with a vanishing velocity of transverse phonons. Such ``floppy'' solids exhibit a cubic elastic nonlinearity that is relevant in spatial dimensions $d<4$ and drives the system to a strongly-coupled Lifshitz quantum critical point in $d=3$, that we analyze in an $ε= 4-d$ expansion. The associated phase transition is generically first-order, preempting the system's full access to the strongly-coupled fixed point. Nevertheless, the critical data of this quantum critical point is accessible within a Ginzburg region in the vicinity of the transition. This cubic nonlinearity vanishes exactly in $d=2$, where the leading interactions are instead quartic and are marginal, leading to weak logarithmic singularities. We discuss possible realizations of this ``crystal Lifshitz criticality'' in quantum materials.

</details>


### [186] [Analytical solutions to the non-equilibrium Green's functions in large-$q$ SYK models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16438)
*Jan C. Louw*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文给出了非对易 q/2-body SYK 哈密顿量淬火后各时间段格林函数的闭式解析解,发现淬火前后能量的简单关系与精确温度更新规则,并用柯西-施瓦茨不等式证明闭系统加热不可避免,从而几何地导出第二定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK 模型在 1/q 领头阶精确可解,但淬火后除瞬时热化时间块外的非平衡时间段此前仅有数值结果,缺乏解析理解;同时需澄清有限 q 下"瞬时热化"的含义。

Method: 对非对易 q/2-body SYK 哈密顿量之间的淬火进行解析求解,推导各时间块格林函数的闭式表达式;提取淬火前后能量关系 ε1 ∝ ε0 及精确温度更新规则;借助柯西-施瓦茨不等式分析加热行为。

Result: 获得每一时间块格林函数的闭式解;得到 ε1 ∝ ε0 的简单关系与精确温度更新规则;证明闭系统加热不可避免。

Conclusion: SYK 淬火后的非平衡动力学可完全解析化,第二定律在该模型中作为几何必然性出现;该解为有限 q 下"瞬时热化"的研究奠定基础。

Abstract: It is known that the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model is exactly solvable at leading order in $1/q$; specifically, the instantaneously thermal time block after the quench is known in closed form. Thus far the remaining (inherently out of equilibrium) blocks have only been numerically accessible. Here we provide their analytical solutions for a quench between non-commuting $q/2$-body SYK Hamiltonians. We obtain the Green's functions in closed form for every time block. We extract a simple relation between the pre- and post-quench energies $ε_1 \propto ε_0$ leading to an exact temperature update rule. Via a Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we show that heating is inevitable for the closed system; hence, the second law emerges geometrically. The solution is used in a companion paper to address what ``instantaneous thermalization'' means at finite $q$.

</details>


### [187] [Hartree-Fock Density-Matrix Renormalization Group for Very Long Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16587)
*Steven R. White*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 将Hartree–Fock表述为单Slater行列式的DMRG扫描，在普通台式机上实现了十万电子量级、含长程库仑与交换的HF计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Hartree–Fock计算在包含长程Hartree与交换相互作用时计算与存储成本极高，传统方法难以在常规硬件上处理十万电子量级体系，因此需要借助张量网络压缩来降低复杂度。

Method: 把Hartree–Fock表述为针对单个Slater行列式的DMRG扫描，假设密度–密度型电子–电子相互作用；对占据态与长程相互作用分别进行压缩，使每一步扫描归结为小规模局域Fock问题，同时全程保留交换项。以均匀反铁磁氢链为测试体系，保留库仑作用及其1/r长程尾。

Result: 在64GB内存的Mac mini上，约两小时内初始化并收敛了包含100,000个电子、180万个空间基函数的链，内存占用不足三分之一；每次扫描的计算量近似与链长成正比，收敛所需扫描次数从1000原子到100,000原子变化很小。

Conclusion: DMRG对状态与环境的压缩使含长程Hartree与交换的大规模HF计算可在普通台式计算机上实际完成，显著降低了此类计算的门槛。

Abstract: We formulate Hartree--Fock as a density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) sweep of a single Slater determinant, assuming a density--density electron--electron interaction. We compress the occupied state and long-range interaction separately, reducing each sweep step to a small local Fock problem. Exchange is retained throughout. Uniform antiferromagnetic hydrogen chains provide an almost ideal setting for this approach, while retaining the Coulomb interaction and its $1/r$ tail. We initialize and converge a chain of 100,000 electrons in 1.8 million spatial basis functions in about two hours on a 64-GB Mac mini, using less than a third of its memory. The work per sweep is nearly proportional to chain length, and the number of sweeps needed for convergence changes little from 1000 to 100,000 atoms. DMRG's compression of states and environments thus makes large HF calculations with long-range Hartree and exchange practical on an ordinary desktop computer.

</details>


### [188] [La substitution studies on the heavy-fermion superconductor CeRh$_2$As$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17080)
*Sushma Lakshmi Ravi Sankar,Manuel Brando,Jochen Wosnitza,Seunghyun Khim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过La替代在Ce$_{1-x}$La$_x$Rh$_2$As$_2$中引入负压与无序，发现$T_c$和$T_0$快速被抑制，超导相脆弱性更符合无序配对破坏机制，暗示相I可能源于巡游电子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CeRh$_2$As$_2$具有异常的两相超导行为，其超导相出现在Ce-4$f$磁矩有序态（相I）中，但相I的微观本质尚未确定。作者希望通过La替代调控晶格、Kondo能标与无序，来揭示这些低温相的本质。

Method: 生长Ce$_{1-x}$La$_x$Rh$_2$As$_2$（$x$至0.1）单晶，测量晶格参数随$x$的变化，并通过电阻率、价态与非费米液体行为分析，研究La替代对$T^*_{\mathrm{max}}$、$T_c$与$T_0$的影响。

Result: 晶格参数随$x$单调增大，对应$x=0.1$约-0.3 GPa的有效负压；Ce$^{3+}$局域价态与非费米液体行为保持不变；$T^*_{\mathrm{max}}\approx45$ K随$x$增大向低温移动，表明Kondo能标被抑制。$T_c$和$T_0$在$x>0.05$时快速降至0.1 K以下。$T_c$的快速下降与中等负压下预期不符，更符合替代引入无序所导致的非常规超导强配对破坏。

Conclusion: 相I在压力和替代无序下均表现出脆弱性，暗示其可能具有巡游电子起源，而非局域磁矩起源。

Abstract: CeRh$_2$As$_2$ has been receiving considerable attention due to its unusual two-phase superconductivity. The superconducting (SC) phase appears at $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ = 0.35 K in an ordered state (phase I) of the Ce-4$f$ moments, which develops below $T_{\mathrm{0}}$ = 0.55 K. The microscopic nature of phase I has not been fully established yet. We report a single-crystal study of the effect of La substitution on these low-temperature phases in Ce$_{1-\textit{x}}$La$_\textit{x}$Rh$_2$As$_2$ up to $x$ = 0.1. The lattice parameters increase monotonically with $x$, corresponding to an effective negative pressure of approximately -0.3 GPa for $x$ = 0.1. While the Ce$^{3+}$ local valence state and the non-Fermi-liquid behavior are preserved, the resistivity coherence maximum $T^{*}_{\mathrm{max}}$ $\approx$ 45 K in the pristine sample shifts to lower temperatures with increasing $x$, indicating a suppression of the Kondo energy scale upon lattice expansion. On the other hand, both $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ and $T_{\mathrm{0}}$ are rapidly suppressed to below 0.1 K for $x$ $>$ 0.05. Notably, such a rapid decrease of $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ under moderate negative pressure is unexpected, but is rather consistent with substitution-induced disorder effect which leads to strong pair breaking in unconventional superconductors. The observed fragility of phase I under both pressure and disorder may imply its itinerant origin.

</details>
